## Overview

**The Beginning**

- The Dragons of Norrath expansion was announced on January 11, 2005, and went live on February 15, 2005. The initial expansion announcement may be found [here](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/history/news-2005jan11.html). The initial patch message may be found [here](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/history/patches-2005-1.html).

**What's Going On:**

- A series of events has transpired across the world of Norrath and the familiar faces of Firiona Vie and her nemesis Lanys T\`Vyl return to find themselves at the forefront of these events. Unbeknownst to them, a dark influence has seeped into the world of Norrath causing new tensions to arise and conflicts to intensify as the dawn of a new age emerges. The Age of War is upon the people of Norrath and heroes return in this ultimate, unending battle between good and evil.
- A gnome mining expedition has managed to dig their way through the northern mountains in Lavastorm. They came upon the dragon nesting grounds, a previously unexplored part of northeastern Antonica which is full of Nest dragons, dragonkin and goblins; and thriving with wildlife.

**What's New:**

- The Guild Hall is your guild's own instanced zone, including guild bank, a portal to many druid/wizard destinations, and buff stoppage.
- The Guild Lobby (now available to everyone) is a place for guilds to recruit and for players to summon their corpses.
- New Models: Dragons, Stone Sentinels, Kirins, Drakes, Spiders, Pumas, Dervishes.
- New AAs retrieved through Dragons of Norrath progression.

**Progression:**

- In order to progress through this expansion, you would be advised to pick a faction and stick with it. You have two to choose from: The good-aligned [Norrath's Keepers](http://eqbeastiary.allakhazam.com/faction.html?faction=398) or the evil-aligned [Dark Reign](http://eqbeastiary.allakhazam.com/faction.html?faction=395). Neither faction has any effect on any other zones in EverQuest.
- Both factions have camps set up in the network of caves in Lavastorm's north. In addition to these two camps, there is a group of Wayfarers to the south-southeast of the caves who also offer tasks.
- Completing tasks (both groups and raids) yields X amount of Radiant Crystals or Ebon Crystals, which can be used to purchase items and augmentations from adventure merchants [Tatsujiro the Serene](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=17804) (for the Norrath's Keepers) and [Xeib Darkskies](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=17792) (for the Dark Reign).
- Faction is gained by completing tasks and turning in the token you receive after each task. These tokens are LORE, so you need to make the turn-in after each task completed for maximum faction gains.
  - Norrath's Keepers Tokens are turned in to [Tatsujiro the Serene](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=17804).
  - Dark Reign Tokens are turned in to [Xeib Darkskies](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=17792).

**Zones:**

- A list of Dragons of Norrath zones may be found [here](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/zone.html?zcont=Dragons+of+Norrath).
- A screenshot of the animated in-game map of the Dragons of Norrath territory may be found [here](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/gallery/ingamemaps/antonica2.jpg).

**Travel to the Dragons of Norrath:**

- The Broodlands are entered through the cave system in Lavastorm's north.

* * *

## Zones

**A Breakdown of Static Zones**:

- Guild Lobby (all levels; entered through the Plane of Knowledge)
- Guild Hall (all levels; entered through the Guild Lobby; must be guilded)
- The Broodlands (40th-60th level; entered through the northern caves of Lavastorm)
- Lavaspinner's Lair (45th-60th level; entered through the Broodlands)
- Tirranun's Delve (45th-65th level; entered through Lavaspinner's Lair)
- Stillmoon Temple (55th-65th level; entered through the Broodlands)
- The Ascent (60th-70th level; entered through Stillmoon Temple)
- Thundercrest Isles (60th-70th level; entered through the Broodlands)
- The Accursed Nest (70th level; entered through the dropdown in the rocks centered in the Broodlands lake)

**A Breakdown of Instanced Zones:**

- Lavaspinner's Lair (instanced) (missions available for 60th-70th level; multiple entrances through the Broodlands)
- Tirranun's Delve (instanced) (missions available for 60th-70th level; multiple entrances through the Broodlands)
- Stillmoon Temple (instanced) (missions available for 60th-70th level; multiple entrances through the Broodlands)
- The Ascent (instanced) (missions available for 60th-70th level; multiple entrances through the Broodlands)
- Thundercrest Isles (instanced) (missions available for 60th-70th level; multiple entrances through the Broodlands)
- The Accursed Nest (instanced) (missions available for 70th-75th level; multiple entrances through the Broodlands)

## Progression

### Norrath's Keepers Progression

Quest details can be found [here](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3061). This is a brief rundown of Norrath's Keepers progression:

- **Progression pre-requisite:**Reach indifferent faction with the Norrath's Keepers.
  - Do so by doing solo or group tasks offered by Wayfarers Mercenaries [Elanye](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=17811) and [Bitral](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=17810), or NPCs in the Norrath's Keepers camp.

- **Once your faction is indifferent, begin Tier 1.**
  - Hail [Chieftain Relae Aderi](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=17800) to receive a flag.
  - Hail [Private Nylaen Kel\`Ther](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=17801).
    - Complete the task [Creating an Antidote](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3107).
    - Complete the task [Burning Arrows](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3108).
    - Complete the task [Greed of the Goblins](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3103).
  - Hail [Chieftain Relae Aderi](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=17800) to receive a flag.
  - Hail [Keeper Dilar Nelune](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=17799).
    - Complete the task [Children of Gimblax](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3072).
  - Hail [Chieftain Relae Aderi](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=17800) to receive two AAs and the AA Gift of the Keepers.

- **Once your faction is amiable, begin Tier 2.**
  - Hail [Chieftain Relae Aderi](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=17800) to receive a flag (you are also now able to request the Tirranun raid).
  - Hail [Private Nylaen Kel\`Ther](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=17801).
    - Complete the task [Turn the Tides of the Sand](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3128).
    - Complete the task [Recover the Lost Map](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3127).
    - Complete the task [Defend the Kirin Contact](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3126).
  - Hail [Keeper Dilar Nelune](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=17799).
    - Complete the task [Sickness of the Spirit](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3071).
  - Hail [Chieftain Relae Aderi](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=17800) to receive a flag.
  - Hail [Lieutenant Ekiltu Verlor](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=17798).
  - Loot a Glowing Stone Fragment from [Emoush the Destroyer](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=17751) in the [Calling Emoush](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3060) raid task.
    - Side Note: You need only participate and loot the item; you do not have to be the one who requested the raid.
  - Hail and give the Glowing Stone Fragment to [Chieftain Relae Aderi](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=17800) to receive a flag, two AAs, the AA Valor of the Keepers, and your 69th level spell/tome.

- **Once your faction is high amiable, begin Tier 3.**
  - Hail [Chieftain Relae Aderi](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=17800) to receive a flag.
  - Hail [Private Nylaen Kel\`Ther](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=17801).
    - Complete the task [Protect the Waters](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3288).
    - Complete the task [The Sacred Scrolls](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3289).
    - Complete the task [Snowfoot Revenge](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3287).
  - Hail [Chieftain Relae Aderi](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=17800) to receive a flag).
  - Hail [Keeper Dilar Nelune](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=17799).
    - Complete the task [History of the Isle](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3262).
  - Hail [Chieftain Relae Aderi](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=17800) to receive a flag).
  - Hail [Lieutenant Ekiltu Verlor](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=17798).
  - Loot Lava Spider Spinners from [Volkara](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=17726) in the [Volkara's Bite](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3465) raid task.
    - Side Note: You need only participate and loot the item; you do not have to be the one who requested the raid.
  - Give Lava Spider Spinners to [Chieftain Relae Aderi](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=17800) to receive a flag.
  - Hail [Lieutenant Ekiltu Verlor](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=17798).
  - Loot Goblin Warlord's Beads from [a Stillmoon Warlord](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=17783) in the [Trial of Perseverance](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3067) raid task.
    - Side Note: You need only participate and loot the item; you do not have to be the one who requested the raid.
  - Give the Goblin Warlord's Beads to [Chieftain Relae Aderi](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=17800) to receive a flag, two AAs, and the AA Embrace of the Keepers.

- **Once your faction is kindly, begin Tier 4.**
  - Hail [Chieftain Relae Aderi](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=17800) to receive a flag.
  - Hail [Lieutenant Ekiltu Verlor](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=17798).
  - Loot Quintessence of Sand from [Shogurei](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=17725) in the [Guardian of the Sands](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3058) raid task.
    - Side Note: You need only participate and loot the item; you do not have to be the one who requested the raid.
  - Give the Quintessence of Sand to [Chieftain Relae Aderi](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=17800) for a character flag.
  - Loot Meditation Stone from [Storm Reach Sensei](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=17820) in the [Goblin Dojo](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3076) raid task.
    - Side Note: You need only participate and loot the item; you do not have to be the one who requested the raid.
  - Give the Meditation Stone to [Chieftain Relae Aderi](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=17800) for a character flag.
  - Loot Yar'Lir's Fang from [Yar'Lir the Matriarch](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=17663) in the [An End to the Storms](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3089) raid task.
    - Side Note: You need only participate and loot the item; you do not have to be the one who requested the raid.
  - Give Yar'Lir's Fang to [Chieftain Relae Aderi](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=17800) for a character flag, two AAs, and the AA Power of the Keepers.

- **Once your faction is warmly, begin Tier 5.**
  - Hail [Chieftain Relae Aderi](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=17800) to receive a flag.
  - Hail [Keeper Dilar Nelune](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=17799).
    - Complete the task [Origins of the Curse](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3263).
    - Recieve a flag after task's completion.
  - Hail [Lieutenant Ekiltu Verlor](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=17799).
  - Loot Diseased Wing Fragment from [Ju\`rek the Corrupted](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=17734) in [The Curse of Ju\`rek](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3290) raid task.
    - Side Note: You need only participate and loot the item; you do not have to be the one who requested the raid.
  - Give the Diseased Wing Fragment to [Chieftain Relae Aderi](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=17800).
  - Hail [Chieftain Relae Aderi](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=17800) to receive a flag.
  - Hail [Lieutenant Ekiltu Verlor](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=17798).
  - Loot Shadowscale of Vishimtar from [Vishimtar the Fallen](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=17716) in [In the Shadows](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3063) raid task.
    - Side Note: You need only participate and loot the item; you do not have to be the one who requested the raid.
  - Give Shadowscale of Vishimtar to [Chieftain Relae Aderi](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=17800) to receive your final AA in Dragons of Norrath, Sanctity of the Keepers.

### Dark Reign Progession

Quest details can be found [here](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3062). This is a brief rundown of Dark Reign progression:

- **Progression pre-requisite:**Reach indifferent faction with the Dark Reign
  - Do so by doing solo or group tasks offered by Wayfarers Mercenaries [Jeryx](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=17812) and [Varein](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=17813), or NPCs in the Dark Reign camp.

- **Once your faction is indifferent, begin Tier 1.**
  - Hail [Commander Zaerr Ty\`Dar](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=17795) to receive a flag.
  - Hail [Officer Sirrikis Ryktor](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=17794).
    - Complete the task [Blood of the Basilisk](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3291).
    - Complete the task [Clearing the Path](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3292).

    - Complete the task [Have Note Will Travel](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3294).
  - Hail [Commander Zaerr Ty\`Dar](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=17795) to receive two AAs and the AA Gift of the Dark Reign.

- **Once your faction is amiable, begin Tier 2.**
  - Hail [Commander Zaerr Ty\`Dar](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/npc.html?id=17795) to receive a flag (you are also now able to request the Tirranun raid).

## Quests

Quests to do While Progressing Through the Dragons of Norrath

### Cultural Armor Quests

- With this expansion came the introduction of progressive cultural armor for each race. An overview of this process can be found [here](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3056). For information specific to each race, visit...
  - [Barbarian Cultural Armor](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3044)
  - [Dark Elf Cultural Armor](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3045)
  - [Dwarf Cultural Armor](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3049)
  - [Erudite Cultural Armor](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3043)
  - [Froglok Cultural Armor](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3052)
  - [Gnome Cultural Armor](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3046)
  - [Half Elf Cultural Armor](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3039)
  - [Halfling Cultural Armor](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3042)
  - [High Elf Cultural Armor](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3040)
  - [Human Cultural Armor](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3038)
  - [Iksar Cultural Armor](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3050)
  - [Ogre Cultural Armor](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3048)
  - [Troll Cultural Armor](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3047)
  - [Vah Shir Cultural Armor](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3051)
  - [Wood Elf Cultural Armor](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3041)

### Mask of the Hidden

- Each class gets its own top-of-the-line loot from [this quest](https://everquest.allakhazam.com/db/quest.html?quest=3090), which involves gathering the eyes of both Yar'Lir the Matriarch and Vishimtar the Fallen.

Modified: Tue Dec 5 05:21:04 2023 | | **Click here** [quest=3056] for an overview of cultural armor introduced with Dragons of Norrath.
_(If you are starting in Qeynos, click here [quest=3038].)_
_Find Vivian Selgan in East Freeport (location -1015, -865)._
You say, 'Hail, Vivian Selgan'
Vivian Selgan says 'Hello \_\_\_\_\_\_. What brings you back 'ere? Wanted to be round yer own [kind] again?'
You say, 'Kind?'
Vivian Selgan says 'Aye. A lot of us have returned home these past few months. Seems distrust is brewing among them other races. Now, I don't want to be accused of bigotry, some of those other races are okay--dwarves and elves are pretty reliable in a fight--but the rest: you can't trust 'em. Like them blasted dark elves: always plotting something. And those lizard things. . . don't even get me started with those. Folks don't feel safe out there anymore so they come back here for protection. Why do you think I came back to the city! To be 'round more humans, folks I can [trust]!'
You say, 'Trust?'
Vivian Selgan says 'Aye, you know what I mean. We need to stick together, form a unified front and let our enemies know we mean business. In fact, right now I'm making armor for the city militia. Hmm, you look like you'd be good in a fight. Perhaps you'd be [interested] in these armor designs. Or maybe you want to know more about the military and scholarly [symbols] we humans have used over the ages? I can share my patterns for either the symbols or the armor with you provided you don't go blabbing my trade secrets to no dark elves. Can't trust those kind!'
You say, 'I am interested.'
Vivian Selgan says 'Excellent, I can make you a pattern book, but first I'm gonna need some materials. Bring me a raw-hide belt for the cover, a low quality cat pelt for the pages, a snake venom sac for the ink, and a cutthroat insignia ring for the binding. I have heard most of these items can be found in the Oasis of Marr, South Ro and the Commonlands. Bring them to me and the pattern book shall be yours.'
_On handing in the items:_
Artisan Vivian Selgan says 'Aye, well done, my friend! As promised, 'ere is the pattern book. It may take some practice to get a hang of those designs, but they should serve you well. Don't forget about us if a war breaks out. I want you up on those city walls with me, aye!'
_You receive your book of Ancestral Human Armor._
You say, 'What symbols?'
Vivian Selgan says 'Once ye've got yer armor, ye might want ta reinforce it with these symbols. Fetch me a few things so I can make the symbol patterns. There's reagents I need in [Befallen], [The Hole], [The Dragon Necropolis], and the [valley] beyond the Lavastorm Mountains.'
**Journeyman's Book of Human Culture**
You say, 'Befallen.'
You have been assigned the task 'Into Befallen'.
Vivian Selgan says 'Deep in Befallen lay Ice Boned Skeletons. Their crystallized marrow is what I need to inscribe this pattern. Hunt around down there for a bit and bring me four of them.'
**Task given to get your Journeyman's Book:**
1) Kill 20 Ice boned skeletons in Befallen
2) Loot four Crystalized Marrow
3) Hand Vivian the marrow
4) Kill 10 willowisps
5) Loot one Burned Out Lightstone
6) Return Lightstone to Vivian
_Receive Journeyman's Book._
**Expert's Book of Human Culture**
You say, 'The Hole.'
You have been assigned the task 'Searching Old Paineel'.
Vivian Selgan says 'You think you're ready for the hole do you? This place is ripe with magical energies. I need you to put some of the elemental warriors to rest and bring me twenty drops of mercury.'
**Task given to get your Expert's Book:**
1) Kill 50 elemental warriors in The Hole
2) Loot 10 Drops of Mercury
3) Return Mercury to Vivian
4) Kill 5 Elemental Monstrosities in The Hole
5) Loot 1 Drop of Mercury
6) Return Mercury to Vivian
_Receive Expert's Book._
**Master's Book of Human Culture**
You say, 'The Dragon Necropolis.'
You have been assigned the task 'Delve the Necropolis'.
Vivian Selgan says 'Within the tomb of the Dragon Necropolis you will find a race of rat creatures.. In order to create this symbol I need lots of their old dragon horns. Return to me when you have acquired these items.'
**Task given to get your Master's Book:**
1) Kill 80 Chetari-aligned NPCs in Dragon Necropolis
2) Loot 2 Old Dragon Horns
3) Return Horns to Vivian
4) Kill 5 Phase Spiders
5) Loot 1 Phase Spider Silk
6) Return 1 Phase Spider Silk to Vivian
_Receive Master's Book._
**Grandmaster's Book of Human Culture**
You say, 'What valley?'
You have been assigned the task 'Ripples in Stillmoon'.
Vivian Selgan says 'Ready for something more challenging, eh? Very well. The goblins in stillmoon temple are known for their resilient skin. For this next pattern I need four of the thickest rough goblin skins you can find. Go collect a large quantity and bring me the finest of the haul. You may wish to seek out some operatives in Lavastorm to aid you in tracking down these rare items.'
**Task given to get your Grandmaster's Book:**
1) Kill 120 Denizens of Stillmoon in Stillmoon Temple
2) Loot 80 Rough Patches of Goblin Skin
3) Return Skins to Vivian
4) Kill 10 Denizens of Stillmoon Temple
5) Loot 1 Stone Necklace
6) Return 1 Stone Necklace to Vivian
_Receive Grandmaster's Book._
..................................................................................
**QEYNOS**
_Find Tar Cordell in North Qeynos by the Priest of Discord, at +230, +395._
**Ancestral Human Armor**
You say, 'Hail, Tar Cordell'
Tar Cordell says 'Hello \_\_\_\_\_\_. What brings you back 'ere? Wanted to be 'round yer own [kind] again?'
You say, 'My kind?'
Tar Cordell says 'Aye. A lot of us 'ave returned 'ome these past few months. Seems distrust is brewing among them other races. Now, I don't want to be accused of bigotry, some of those other kind are okay--dwarves and elves are pretty reliable--but the rest . . . you can't trust 'em. Like them blasted dark elves always plotting something. And those lizard things . . . don't even get me started with those. Folks don't feel safe out there anymore so they're coming back to the cities for protection. Why do you think I came back to 'ere! To be 'round more 'umans, folks I can [trust]!'
You say, 'Trust?'
Tar Cordell says 'Aye, you know what I mean. We need to stick together, form a unified front and let our enemies know we mean business. In fact, right now I'm making armor for the city militia. Hmm, you look like you'd be good in a fight. Perhaps you'd be interested in these armor designs. I can share my pattern [book] or even my armor [patterns] with you provided you don't go blabbing my trade secrets to no dark elves. Can't trust those kind!'
You say, 'What book?'
Tar Cordell says 'Excellent, I can make you a pattern book, but first I'm gonna need some materials. Bring me a fire beetle carapace for the cover, a patch of zombie skin for the pages, a golden bandit tooth for the clasp, and a lion tail to grind down and make a salve which will protect and preserve the finished product. I hear these resources can be found in Western Karana. Once I have these things, I can create the pattern book for ya.'
_On handing in the items:_
Tar Cordell says 'Aye, well done, my friend! As promised, 'ere is the pattern book. It may take some practice to get a 'ang of these designs, but they should serve you well. Don't forget about us if a war breaks out. I want you up on those city walls with me, aye!'
You say, 'What patterns?'
Tar Cordell says 'Aye, I can give you these patterns provided you can help me with some missions for Qeynos. What do ya say? Can you help ol' Tar out? I need someone to spearhead expeditions to [Erud's Crossing], [Kedge Keep], the [Dragon Necropolis] or, if you've got the metal for it, the [valley] on the other side of the lavastorm mountains. I can reward you with some armor patterns for yer efforts. You know the drill. The more the danger, the more the reward! Choose wisely, now.'
**Journeyman's Book of Human Culture**
You say, 'Erud's Crossing.'
You have been assigned the task 'Clearing the Western Sea Lanes'.
Tar Cordell says 'Ha ha! I knew I could count on ya! Seems our ocean trade routes are in trouble again, my friend. I need ya' to travel over to Erud's Crossing and kill twenty o' dem sharks. That should clear the way a bit for our sailors! Bring me three lake pebbles from their stomachs as proof of your kills. Not that I don't trust ya, I just need something to show the boys when I let 'em know the way is clear.'
Task details
1) Kill 15 Sharks in Kerra Isle
2) Loot three Lake Pebbles
3) Return and hand in pebbles to Tar Cordell
4) Kill 10 Will-O-Wisps
5) Loot one Lightstone
6) Return and hand in Lightstone to Tar Cordell
Well done. Lucky I found you or that job never would have gotten done. As promised, 'ere's those armor runes. Let me know if you need any more of these [patterns]. Sure as the sunrise, there's always more work to be done 'round here.
You have been given: Journeyman's Book of Human Culture
**Expert's Book of Human Culture**
You say, 'Kedge Keep.'
You have been assigned the task 'Clearing the Eastern Sea Lanes'.
Tar Cordell says 'Our ocean trade routes still aren't as secure as we need 'em to be. Several of Qeynos' finer cargo vessels have disappeared while sailing from `ere to Freeport and Faydwer. Sure enough, the wreckage always turns up floating around Kedge Keep. Get over there an' wreck the place. Kill fifty mermaids and return to me with four vials of deepwater ink. That should clear the way for our ships.'
**Name of task and hand-in dialogues needed.**
1) Kill 40 Mermaids in Kedge Keep
2) Loot 15 Deepwater Inks
3) Deliver four Deepwater Inks to Tar Cordell
4) Kill any five Cauldron Sharks in Kedge Keep
5) Loot one Shark Skin
6) Deliver one Shark Skin to Tar Cordell
**Master's Book of Human Culture**
You say, 'Dragon Necropolis.'
You have been assigned the task 'Scale and Bone'.
Tar Cordell says 'We've lost several expeditions into the dragon necropolis over the past few months. It's the rats that have been sneaking upon our ranks in those dark tunnels. Kill eighty of those things and return to me four old dragon horns. That should clear the depths out a bit.'
**Hand-in dialogues and task details needed.**
**Grandmaster's Book of Human Culture**
You say, 'Valley.'
You have been assigned the task 'Lair of the Lavaspinner'.
Tar Cordell says 'Aye, glad you volunteered! Our scouts have been having a heap o' trouble in the dragon lands north of Lavastorm. Seems the spiders there have been tracking our movements and ambushing our scouts. I need you to lead an expedition into the Lavaspinner's Lair and slay six score denizens of the delve. Bring me four arachnid eye clusters as proof of your kills. You may wish to seek out some operatives in Lavastorm to aid you in tracking down these rare items.'
**Dialogues and task details needed.**
**Types of Armor**
_Claviger - Berserker, Ranger, Rogue_
_Sagacious - Druid, Monk_
_Stalwart - Bard, Cleric, Paladin, Shadowknight, Warrior_
_Stargazer - Enchanter, Magician, Necromancer, Wizard_

_Find Vor Westrider in North Qeynos near the Priest of Discord._
You say, 'Hail, Vor Westrider'
Vor Westrider says, 'And hail to you as well, traveler. What news? It's nice to hear from someone of mixed heritage like myself. I often fear our kind will never be fully understood by either elves or human. Though we pass through their lands, we are always a little [uncomfortable] in either community. At least we half elves understand each other.'
You say, 'Uncomfortable?'
Vor Westrider says, 'You know what I mean. When traveling through elven communities, we must duck under their doorways and when visiting our human kin, we receive skeptical stares at our pointed ears. Not to mention, it's impossible to find armor from either race that fits properly. Human clothing is baggy in the shoulders and elven armor tends to chafe in the . . . well. . . you know what I mean, [right]?'
You say, 'Right'
Vor Westrider says 'Tell you what -- I contracted a half elven artisan to make me a pattern book for armor specifically for us. I made a suit and it fits perfectly. If you'd like, I can make you a copy of the pattern [book]. I also have some advanced armor [patterns] you may be interested in.'
You say, 'I'll take a book'
Vor Westrider says 'Excellent, I can make you a pattern book, but first I'm going to need some materials. Bring me a fire beetle carapace for the cover, a patch of zombie skin for the pages, a golden bandit tooth for the clasp, and a lion tail to grind down and make a salve which will protect and preserve the finished product. I have heard these resources can be found in Western Karana. Once I have these things, I can create the pattern book for ya.'
**Quest Items to get your Ancestral Book:**
Fire Beetle Carapace, Zombie Skin, Bandit Tooth, Lion Tail
You say, 'And the patterns?'
Artisan Vor Westrider says 'These, I cannot give away, but I can share them with you provided you are willing to help make Norrath safe for us wanderers again. I'm currently working with the city of Qeynos to secure trade routes outside the city gates. We need someone to lead some expeditions and reduce the numbers of dangerous creatures out there. As proof of your deeds we do not want actual messy body parts, but instead we would like you to make impressions of certain body parts in impression books. These books will magically take deep impressions or even just surface patterns. Interested? The more difficult the expedition the more powerful the armor pattern, I can provide. I have also heard about a new [underground] place.'
A task window pops up offering the following tasks:
Blessed Impressions (which variant?)
Revered Impressions (which variant?)

Eminent Impressions

Sublime Impressions [quest=4569]

Illustrious Impressions [quest=5211]
Numinous Impressions [quest=5524]
Darkhollow Geode [quest=5070]

Modified: Tue Dec 5 05:21:04 2023 | | _**Click here** [quest=3056] for an overview of cultural armor introduced with Dragons of Norrath._
_Find Elistar Vorann in Felwithe, located in the building across from the bank._
You say, 'Hail, Elistar Vorann'
Elistar Vorann says 'Hello, \_\_\_\_\_\_. Welcome back to the fair city of [Felwithe]. You must tell me of your travels!'
You say, 'What of Felwithe?'
Elistar Vorann says 'While you were away, we have made much advancement. Our trade alliance with Kaladim has secured us fresh mithril from their mines. In the meantime, our artisans have been refining our armor [designs] in preparation for the new alloys.'
You say, 'What designs?'
Elistar Vorann says 'Yes, our old designs relied too heavily on the stopping power of the materials used in the armor. The new designs promote lighter, more flexible armor suits while providing the same amount of protection. It's difficult to explain until you have worn a new suit yourself. Would you be [interested] in these new armor patterns, or have you heard about the beneficial [symbols] I have discovered?'
You say, 'I am interested.'
Elistar Vorann says 'Very well. To make you a pattern book, I'll need some zombie skin and darkbone marrow from the Estate of Unrest. I can use those to create the cover and ink for the book. I'll also need some of the battle beads from Crushbone to help bind the book and a rotting orc tooth to complete the ink. Bring me these things and the pattern book shall be yours.'
_Hand in the items._
Elistar Vorann says 'Well done, \_\_\_\_\_\_. Here, take this pattern book. It may require some time to master the technique of making armor, but these new patterns should set you out in the right direction. Best of luck!'
_You receive Ancestral High Elven Armor._
You say, 'What symbols?'
Elistar Vorann says 'I have found a great many symbols that can be created and added to our new armor designs. Once you placed these symbols on the armor the effect is nothing short of amazing. The capabilities of the armor are improved beyond the sum of its parts. If you wish I would be willing to part with instructions for these symbols in exchange for some [favors].'
You say, 'What favors?'
Elistar Vorann says 'I have several sets of research components I have been after for some time now. Unfortunately I have not had the time to be able to gather them myself. If you would be willing to retrieve them for me they can be found in several areas, the [Ocean of Tears], [Lower Guk], [Fungus Grove], or [Lavaspinner's Lair].'
You say, 'Ocean of Tears?'
You have been assigned the task 'Exploring the Ocean'.
You say, 'Lower Guk?'
You have been assigned the task 'Denizens of Guk'.
You say, 'Fungus Grove?'
You have been assigned the task 'Plants of Fungus Grove'.
You say, 'Lavaspinner's Lair?'
**Dialogue needed. Must be level 70 to obtain the last task.**

Modified: Tue Dec 5 05:21:04 2023 | | **Click here** [quest=3056] for an overview of cultural armor introduced with Dragons of Norrath.
**Ancestral Wood Elven Armor**
_Find Calian Silverlake in Kelethin, location +942, +660._
You say, 'Hail, Calian Silverlake'
Calian Silverlake says 'Ah, the blessings of Tunare upon you \_\_\_\_\_. Perhaps you have come in search of lessons on how to create some new [armor]? Or maybe it was to hear a bit about the natural world and our history?'
You say, 'What armor?'
Calian Silverlake says 'I thought you might be interested in my new [designs], your current armor looks a bit worn. I've recently discovered a way of crafting the essence of the natural world into my armor pieces. Armor crafted in this manner comes out better than anything the elders have seen before. You may also be interested in the [runes] I have discovered for these armor pieces.'
You say, 'What designs?'
Calian Silverlake says 'I suppose I could share a book of my designs with you, but I cannot give you mine. It's the only copy I have! I'll make you a deal though, if you can bring me the [materials] to create a new book I'll make a copy for you to use.'
You say, 'What runes?'
Calian Silverlake says 'These runes can be quite complicated to create but are well worth the effort. They serve to enhance the natural forces in the armor and can bring untold benefits when put to use by a skilled crafter. If you're interested, I would be willing to give you instructions on how to create these runes in exchange for your [help].'
You say, 'What materials?'
Calian Silverlake says 'I'll need a some zombie skin and darkbone marrow from the Estate of Unrest. I can use those to create the cover and ink for the book. I'll also need some of the battle beads from Crushbone to help bind the book and a rotting orc tooth to complete the ink. Once I have those I will have a copy made for you.'
- Crushbone Battle Beads [item=18271] from Crushbone
- Darkbone Marrow [item=13094]
- Rotting Orc Tooth [item=18277] from Crushbone
- Zombie Skin [item=7772]
_Hand in the items._
Artisan Calian Silverlake says "Well done! You truly are a resourceful one. Undoubtly Tunare looks kindly upon you. Once you have mastered your craft enough, perhaps you will be able to use these patterns to your benefit."
You gain party experience
_You receive Ancestral Wood Elven Armor._
You say, 'I will help.'
Calian Silverlake says 'There are several items I've been meaning to collect, but have not been able to quite get around to going after. If you would be willing to go to [Crushbone], the [City of Mist], the [Acrylia Caverns], or to [Thundercrest Isles], I would happily exchange the instructions for the runes in return for your efforts.'
**Journeyman's Book of Wood Elven Culture**
You say, 'Crushbone.'
You have been assigned the task 'Scouting Crushbone'.
Task Details
Crushbone: Kill 20 orc legionnaires
Crushbone: Loot 4 Preserved Orc Hide
Greater Faydark: Hand in 4 Preserved Orc Hide to Calian Silverlake
Crushbone: Kill 10 orc oracles
Crushbone: Loot 1 Illegible Scroll
Greater Faydark: Hand in 1 Illegible Scroll to Calian Silverlake
**Dialogue needed.**
_You receive Journeyman's Book of Wood Elven Culture._
**Need dialogue for handing in Preserved Orc Hide.**
**Expert's Book of Wood Elven Culture**
You say, 'City of Mist.'
You have been assigned the task 'Dismantling Golems'.
Task Details
City of Mist: Kill 50 skeletons
City of Mist: Loot 20 Bone Chips
Greater Faydark: Hand in 4 Bone Chips to Calian Silverlake
City of Mist: Kill 5 army behemoths
City of Mist: Loot 1 Strange Ochre Clay
Greater Faydark: Hand in 1 Strange Ochre Clay to Calian Silverlake
_Upon handing in the Strange Ochre Clay:_
Calian takes the clay and studies it closely. 'Hmmm this is very interesting. I've never seen the like before. Thank you for your work, here are the instructions.'
_You receive Expert's Book of Wood Elven Culture._
**Need dialogue for handing in Bone Chips.**
**Master's Book of Wood Elven Culture**
You say, 'Acrylia Caverns.'
You have been assigned the task 'Acrylia Expedition'.
Task Details
Acrylia Caverns: Kill 80 sanctum grimlings
Acrylia Caverns: Loot 20 Grimling Toes
Greater Faydark: Hand in 4 Grimling Toes to Calian Silverlake
Acrylia Caverns: Kill 5 sanctum corpsemasters
Acrylia Caverns: Loot 1 Brick of Black Acrylia
Greater Faydark: Hand in 1 Brick of Black Acrylia to Calian Silverlake
_Upon handing in the Brick of Black Acrylia:_
Calian taps his finger on the brick. 'This is an excellent sample. I'm sure it will be perfect for my needs. Here are the instructions you were interested in.'
_You receive Master's Book of Wood Elven Culture._
**Need dialogue for handing in Grimling Toes.**
**Grandmaster's Book of Wood Elven Culture**
You say, 'Thundercrest Isles.'
You have been assigned the task 'Denizens of the Isles'.

Task Details

Greater Faydark: Hand in 1 Impeccable Kirin Horn to Calian Silverlake
_Upon handing in the Impeccable Kirin Horn:_
Calian takes the horn and carefully applies some sort of coating to the horn, 'There, those oils should preserve the horn until I'm able to study it more closely. Thank you for collecting that for me, here are the instructions you were looking for.'
You have been given: Grandmaster's Book of Wood Elven Culture

_Find Bill Whistletop at location -283 -123 -17.50 (one of the smithing buildings)._
You say, 'Hail, Bill Whistletop'
Bill Whistletop says 'Hi-oh there, \_\_\_\_\_. You startled me. I'm busy compiling notes for my compendium; it'll be a complete [history] or Rivervale for an entire century. Imagine that!'
You say, 'What history?'
Bill Whistletop says 'Well, it's not really a history book, not exactly. It's more like I just sit around and write about whatever's [going on] in Rivervale at the moment. I've been doing this for thirty seven years now, so, um, hey, I'm almost half done!'
You say, 'So, what has been going on?'
Bill Whistletop says 'Yeah, I write down everything: fishing trips, crop yields, new card games I pick up. Hmm, I guess it's more of a journal then a history book. . . Oh well, it's still chalk full of historical [significance].'
You say, 'What kind of significance?'
Bill Whistletop says 'Heck yes, I write down everything so some of it has got to be important. For example, I copied down all those [armor patterns] and [cultural symbol patterns] that were displayed during that spring festival eleven years ago. You know the one where we had that competition to see who could make the best suit of armor! Boy, that was a great festival. The apple tarts were amazing. I'm glad I wrote down the recipe for those too!'
You say, 'What armor patterns?'
Bill Whistletop says 'Oh, you're interested in the armor, huh? I guess you do look like the adventuring sort. Well, heck, I can make you an armor pattern book if you want? I have all the designs written down and recorded somewhere in one of my journals; all I'll need is the materials for the pattern book itself. Just bring me a cat skull cap for the cover, a fractured femur for the spine, a minotaur horn for the clasp, and a bunch of optic nerves to grind down and make a salve, which will protect and preserve the finished product. I hear these items can be found in the Runnyeye Goblin territory and the Beholder's Maze, but I'm not really sure? Anyway, once I have those, I can make you a pattern book. Hey, then we can go fishing!'
**Obtain your Ancestral Book:** _Turn in a cat skull cap, fractured femur, a minotaur horn, and a bunch of optic nerves._
Bill Whistletop says 'Oh, hey there, Dryssa. I'm still working on my history book. In fact, I just wrote down the part where we first met. Here let me read it for you!' Bill clears his throat, 'Ahem, so I was writing my history book when Dryssa comes up to me and says 'Hail.' And so I reply. . .' Bill continues to ramble on for many minutes. Finally, he gets to the end of his journal and looks up. He blinks as though surprised that you are still here. 'Oh, yeah, the armor pattern book. Sorry, I forgot about that. Here you go!'
_Receive Ancestral Halfling Armor Book._
You say, 'What cultural symbol patterns?'
Bill Whistletop says 'You're interested in the cultural symbol patterns I see. I can give you a book of cultural symbol patterns but I will need you to run a few tasks for me before I can give them to you. What do you say; would you like to run a few short [tasks] for me?'
You say, 'I will do your tasks.'
Bill Whistletop says 'I have four tasks that need to be completed. The first is an adventure into [Beholder's Maze] where you will clear a path for young Halflings so they can make a safe passage through the maze to East Karana. The next you will be tasked with venturing into the heart of [the Hole] where you hunt for some ingredients for my drip clock. The third task leads you into [Karnor's Castle] where you will collect bone specimen for my collection. The final task you will adventure into [Stillmoon temple] and learn about the creatures that inhabit the area.'
You say, 'I will go to the Beholder's Maze.'
**Task given to get your Journeyman's Book:**
_You have been assigned the task 'Storming the Maze'._
1) Kill 10 Minotaurs
2) Loot four Minotaur Axes
3) Return four Axes
4) Explore the ruins
5) Kill 10 Evil Eyes
6) Loot one Polished Bone Bracelet
_Receive Journeyman's Book._
_Completion text:_ I'm glad to see you have made it back. Surely you have some proof of these HUGE eyes now that you mention! With the bracelet I will say that you have earned these cultural symbol patterns.
**Task given to get your Expert's Book:**
1) Kill 50 Elemental Warriors in The Hole
2) Collect 10 Drops of Mercury
3) Return to Bill Whistletop and give him four Drops of Mercury
4) Kill five Elemental Monstrosities
5) Collect one Drop of Mercury
6) Return to Bill Whistletop and give him the Mercury
_Receive Expert's Book._
**Task given to get your Master's Book:**
1) Kill 80 Decayed Soldiers in Karnor's Castle
2) Collect 40 Bone Chips from the soldiers
3) Return to Bill Whistletop and give him four Bone Chips
4) Kill five Spectral Knights in Karnor's Castle
5) Collect one Bone Chip
6) Return the Bone Chip to Bill Whistletop
_Receive Master's Book._
**Task given to get your Grandmaster's Book: Creatures of the Temple**
1) Kill 120 Stillmoon Creatures
2) Loot 80 Fractured Drake Claws
3) Deliver four Claws to Bill Whistletop
4) Kill 10 10 Stillmoon stone drakes
5) Loot one Razor Sharp Drake Claw
6) Deliver Claw to Bill Whistletop
_Receive Grandmaster's Book._
**Types of Armor**

_Lightring - Ranger, Rogue_
_Longstrider - Druid_

# Erudite Cultural Armor (DoN)

**Level:** 20 | **Maximum Level:** 125 | **Quest Type:** Quest | **Repeatable:** Yes | **Monster Mission:** No


## Quest Text / Walkthrough

**Level:** 20
**Maximum Level:** 125
**Monster Mission:** No
**Repeatable:** Yes
**Can Be Shrouded?:** No
**Quest Type:** Quest
**Group Size:** Solo
**Min. # of Players:** 1
**Max. # of Players:** 1

Click here for an overview of cultural armor introduced with Dragons of Norrath.
ERUDIN (Paineel Below)
Find Gorion Telld`Mare in Erudin, found by Soulbinder Altira Mamar and the Priest of Discord.
Items to return for the Ancestral Erudite Armor book:
Belt Pouch (bought and dropped off random low level mobs)
Bathide Wristband (dropped in The Warrens)
Bamboo Wakizashi Bo (dropped in The Warrens)
Kobold Finger Bones (dropped in The Warrens)
Task given for the Journeyman's Book:
1) Kill 20 Kerrans in Kerra Island
2) Loot four Catman Skins
3) Give Catman Skins to Gorion
4) Kill 10 Kerra Pumas
5) Loot four Medium Quality Cat pelts
6) Give Pelts to Gorion
Task given for Expert's Book:
1) Kill 50 Highland Tigers in the Stonebrunt Mountains
2) Loot 20 Low Quality Cat Pelts
3) Give Pelts to Gorion
4) Kill five Mature Pandas
5) Loot one Panda Pelt
6) Give Pelt to Gorion
Task given for Master's Book:
1) Kill 80 Bulthars in Siren's Grotto
2) Loot 40 Bulthar Trunks
3) Give Bulthar Trunks to Gorion
4) Kill five Sirens
5) Loot one Siren Parts
6) Give Siren Parts to Gorion
Task given for Grandmaster's Book:
1) Kill 120 Denizens of Thundercrest
2) Loot 80 Fractured Drake Claws
3) Give Fractured Drake Claws to Gorion
4) Kill 10 Denizens of Thundercrest
5) Loot 1 Razor-Sharp Drake Claw
6) Give the Razor-Sharp Drake Claw to Gorion
..........................................................................................
PAINEEL
Find Korzan Virimove in Paineel in the courtyard at the bottom of building that the elevator is in, across the way from the LDoN NPC.
You say, 'Hail, Korzan Virimove'
Korzan Virimove says 'Hello, _____. If you'll excuse me, I'm trying to study these [inscriptions] and you are blocking my light."
You say, 'What inscriptions?'
Korzan Virimove says 'These inscriptions are from a stone tablet that was found in the bottom of the Hole.  They detail a forgotten methodology for making Erudite armor. These patterns were created by our ancestors before our race was divided. It staggers my mind to think what we Erudites could have lost such a legacy. These armor [designs] are unlike anything I've ever seen before."
You say, 'What designs?'
Korzan Virimove says 'Yes, it's very difficult to explain without the actual armor patterns to look at.  Would you like a copy of these ancestral armor patterns? I can create a pattern [book] for you, provided you can bring me the necessary ingredients. I may also have some additional armor [patterns] provided you are willing to help me with my research."
You say, 'What book?'
Korzan Virimove says 'Very well. I will need a belt pouch for the cover, a bamboo wakizashi bo for the spine, a kobold finger bone for the clasp, and a bathide wristband to grind down and make a salve which will protect and preserve the finished product. I have heard these items can be found in the Warrens. Return with them and the pattern book shall be yours.'
Quest Items to get your Ancestral Book:
Belt Pouch (bought and dropped off random low level mobs)
Bathide Wristband (dropped in The Warrens)
Bamboo Wakizashi Bo (dropped in The Warrens)
Kobold Finger Bones (dropped in The Warrens)
In Erudin:
Gorion Telld`Mare says 'Excellent work ______. Here are the armor patterns worn by our ancestors. May they serve you well. It will take me some time to decipher the entirety of these patterns. Return to me later and we'll talk of what more can be gleaned from these artifacts.'
You receive Ancestral Erudite Armor.
You say, 'What patterns?'
Korzan Virimove says 'The meanings of the more advanced inscriptions continue to elude me. I've long ago exhausted this city's vast libraries. Now, I require a field agent to seek out new information and artifacts. Would you be interested in leading expeditions to [Kerra Isle], the [Stonebrunt], [Siren's Grotto], or the newly discovered [valley] beyond lavastorm mountains? If you are able to revover more information for me, I can further my studies and reward you with more armor patterns drafted by our anscestors. What say you?'
Task given to get your Journeyman's Book:
1) Kill 20 Kerrans in Kerra Island
2) Loot four Catman Skins
3) Give Catman Skins to Gorion
4) Kill 10 Kerra Pumas

6) Give Pelts to Gorion

Entered: Mon Feb 21 13:25:35 2005
Modified: Sat Feb 8 15:50:13 2020 | | **Click here** [quest=3056] for an overview of cultural armor introduced with Dragons of Norrath.
_Find Dosan Vis`Moor in Neriak Commons at the Blue Flame weapons building._
**Quest Items to get your Ancestral Book:**
Cutthroat Insignia Ring, Raw-hide Belt, Snake Venom Sack, Low Quality Cat Pelt
**Task given to get your Journeyman's Book:**
1) Kill 20 Ogre Guards in Najena
2) Loot four Whore's Bane
3) Return the four Whore's Bane to Dosan Vis`Moor
4) Kill 10 Magicians in Najena
5) Loot one Reagent Pouch
6) Return pouch to Dosan Vis`Moor
_Receive Journeyman's Book._
**Task given to get your Expert's Book:**
1) Kill 50 Gnome Miners in Solusek's Eye
2) Loot 20 Gnome Meat
3) Return meat to Dosan Vis`Moor
4) Kill five CWG models in Solusek's Eye
5) Loot one Broken Frost Module
6) Return module to Dosan Vis`Moor
_Receive Expert's Book._
**Task given to get your Master's Book:**
1) Kill 80 Froglok Krups in Sebilis
2) Loot 40 Wax Flowers
3) Return Wax Flowers to Dosan Vis`Moor
4) Kill five Froglok Ilis Shaman
5) Loot one Nodding Blue Lily
6) Deliver Lily to Dosan
_Receive Master's Book._
**Task given to get your Grandmaster's Book:**
1) Kill 120 Lavaspinners in Lavaspinner's Lair
2) Loot 80 Arachnid Eye Clusters (from Lavaspinners Lair instanced zones)
3) Deliver four Arachnid Eye Clusters to Dosan Vis`Moor
4) Kill 10 Lavaspinners
5) Loot one Deadly Arachnid Mandible (from Lavaspinners Lair instanced zones)
6) Deliver Deadly Arachnid Mandible to Dosan Vis`Moor
_Receive Grandmaster's Book._
**Types of Armor**
_Dreadguard - Cleric, Shadowknight, Warrior_
_Duskwoven - Enchanter, Magician, Necromancer, Wizard_
_Nightfall - Rogue_

Entered: Mon Feb 21 13:25:46 2005
Modified: Tue Dec 5 05:21:04 2023 | | **Click here** [quest=3056] for an overview of cultural armor introduced with Dragons of Norrath.
_Find Vidi Cogsworthy in Ak'Anon at +830, -130 at King Ak'Anon's building._
You say, 'Hail, Vidi Cogsworthy'
Vidi Cogsworthy says 'Wha? Who are you? Go away! You're breaking my [concentration].'
You say, 'Your concentration?'
Vidi Cogsworthy says 'Yes, I'm trying to concentrate on my calculations! You see, I discovered yesterday that based upon the gnomish arm-torso aspect ratio our current [armor] designs are actually patterned with suboptimal. . . Sub. . . Uh. . .. Blast! You've broken my concentration!'
You say, 'What armor?'
Vidi Cogsworthy says 'Yes, well, before you interrupted me I was trying to redesign Ak'Anon's armor patterns. I believe with my newest calculations, I can increase the efficiency of our designs by a significant margin. Assuming, of course, I can finish my calculations. It's hard to work with strangers running up and hailing me all day!' Vidi sighs and then continues, 'Tell you what, if you leave me alone, I'll make you a pattern book with my new designs. Would you be [interested] in that, or perhaps you're more interested in [symbols] I've been studying?'
You say, 'I am interested'
Vidi Cogsworthy says 'Great. To make you a pattern book, I'll need some zombie skin and darkbone marrow from the Estate of Unrest. I can use those to create the parchment and ink for the book. I'll also need some of the battle beads from Crushbone to help bind the book and a preserved orc hide to create the cover. While you're out gathering these things, I can finish my calculations. Hopefully in peace!'
**Quest Items to get your Ancestral Book:**
_Darkbone Marrow, Zombie Skin, Crushbone Battle Beads, Preserved Orc Hide_
Vidi Cogsworthy says 'Hey, it's you! Here's your book. Turns out my protractor was broken and I miscalculated the average angle of incidence of the gnomish shoulder blade. So, uh, you may feel some tightness in your left shoulder when wearing armor made by this pattern book. Just don't do any jumping jacks and you should be fine. Otherwise my design was a huge improvement! Best of luck, \_\_\_\_\_\_!'
You say, 'Symbols?'
Vidi Cogsworthy says 'These symbols when used in conjunction with my improved armor can improve them even more! If you're interested in them I have some [work] that you could do to earn some patterns for them.'
You say, 'What work?'
Vidi Cogsworthy says 'I need you to do a bit of exploration and gathering for me. My inventions always require various new components and I just don't have the time to get them myself. There are several different places I need things from so you have your pick. You could head to [Crushbone], [the Hole] in Paineel, [Skyfire Mountains], or to the [Lavaspinner's Lair].'
**Journeyman's Book of Gnomish Culture**
You say, 'Crushbone.'
You have been assigned the task 'Orcish Expedition'.
Vidi Cogsworthy says 'Crushbone as you probably know is filled with orcs. I need you to head there and take care of their legionnaires as they've always caused problems for me when I went there to research. While you're doing that bring back some of their teeth to show me what you've accomplished.'
Task Details:
Crushbone: Kill 20 legionnaires
Crushbone: Loot 2 Rotting Orc Tooth
Ak'Anon: Deliver 2 Rotting Orc Tooth to Vidi Cogsworthy
Crushbone: Kill 10 orc emissaries
Crushbone: Loot 1 Orc Ribcage
Ak'Anon: Deliver 1 Orc Ribcage to Vidi Cogsworthy
Vidi takes the rib cage from you and throws it into the water. 'Nasty things, I just wanted to make sure you'd really killed them. Maybe now they'll think twice before interfering with the great Vidi Cogsworthy! And of course here are the patterns I mentioned.'
_You receive the Journeyman's Book of Gnomish Culture._
**Expert's Book of Gnomish Culture**
You say, 'The Hole.'
You have been assigned the task 'Mercury Recovery'.
Vidi Cogsworthy says 'The Hole can be a very dangerous place, you'll need to be careful out there. One of the bigger hazards are the elemental warriors. I require the mercury that flows through their veins for my latest invention, the mercury drip clock.'
Task Details
Ruins of Old Paineel: Kill 50 elemental warriors
Ruins of Old Paineel: Loot 10 Drop of Mercury
Ak'Anon: Deliver 4 Drop of Mercury (unstacked) to Vidi Cogsworthy
Ruins of Old Paineel: Kill 5 elemental monstrosities
Ruins of Old Paineel: Loot 1 Drop of Mercury
Ak'Anon: Deliver 1 Drop of Mercury to Vidi Cogsworthy
( **dialogue and task information needed**)
_You receive the Expert's Book of Gnomish Culture._
**Master's Book of Gnomish Culture**
You say, 'Skyfire Mountains.'
You have been assigned the task 'Scales of Skyfire'.
Vidi Cogsworthy says 'Watch out when you're in Skyfire, there's all sorts of nasty things there, but some wonderful components for my inventions. I need you to go hunt some old wurms for me. I've run out of my supply of wurm meat.'
Task Details
Skyfire Mountains: Kill 80 wurms
Skyfire Mountains: Loot 40 Wurm Meat
Ak'Anon: Deliver 4 Wurm Meat to Vidi Cogsworthy
Skyfire Mountains: Kill 5 skyfire drakes
Skyfire Mountains: Loot 1 Skyash Bile
Ak'Anon: Deliver 1 Skyash Bile to Vidi Cogsworthy
Vidi snatches the skyash bile from your hands. 'Ah ha! This is perfect I must get to work on it right away. Before I forget here's those patterns you wanted.'
_You receive the Master's Book of Gnomish Culture._
**Grandmaster's Book of Gnomish Culture**
You say, 'Lavaspinner's Lair.'
You have been assigned the task 'The latest in clockworks!'.
Vidi Cogsworthy says 'You'll never guess what new creation I'm working on now. A clockwork animal! No, no, no not one of those that have been made before, an entirely new type of animal. But I seem running low on claws for it The poor thing has to be able to defend itself after all. I need you to go get me some claws from the drakes in the lair. Those claws are highly resistant to heat and so will be perfect for my Felostiporaus.'
Vidi Cogsworthy says 'The claws I'm after can only be found on the elder drakes. You may need to talks with the operatives in the Lavastorm Mountains for some help finding them.'
_You must be in a Dragons of Norrath mission to get the drops you need._
Task Details
Lavaspinner's Lair: Kill 120 delve drakes
Lavaspinner's Lair: Loot 80 Fractured Drake Claw
Ak'Anon: Deliver 4 Fractured Drake Claw to Vidi Cogsworthy
Lavaspinner's Lair: Kill 10 Lavaspinner Spiders
Lavaspinner's Lair: Loot 1 Deadly Arachnid Mandibles
Ak'Anon: Deliver 1 Deadly Arachnid Mandibles to Vidi Cogsworthy
Vidi snatches the mandibles from you and dances gleefully in a circle, 'Hahahaha! I can finish the Felostiporaus now! No one will ever regard clockworks the same again. Here are the diagrams I promised you. Now leave me be to get my work finished.'
You have been given: Grandmaster's Book of Gnomish Culture
**Note: the Ak'Anon Forge may be found at +1225, -730, outside the Forge of Defiance. There are two forges next to each other, but only one will work for the combines.**
**Types of Armor**
_Autoactuated - Cleric, Paladin, Shadowknight, Warrior_
_Metalofiber - Enchanter, Magician, Necromancer, Wizard_
_Servolinked - Rogue_

# Troll Cultural Armor (DoN)

**Level:** 20 | **Maximum Level:** 90 | **Quest Type:** Quest | **Repeatable:** Yes | **Monster Mission:** No


## Quest Text / Walkthrough

**Level:** 20
**Maximum Level:** 90
**Monster Mission:** No
**Repeatable:** Yes
**Can Be Shrouded?:** No
**Quest Type:** Quest
**Group Size:** Solo
**Min. # of Players:** 1
**Max. # of Players:** 1

Click here for an overview of cultural armor introduced with Dragons of Norrath.
Find Zermak in Grobb, in the room behind the bar standing near the brew barrel.
You say, 'Hail, Zermak'
Zermak says 'Yer looking a might bit uncomfortable dere, ______. Whadda matter? Your [armor] chafing you?'
You say, 'What armor?'
Zermak says 'Tell me 'bout it. Da only armor we trolls ever get is whats we can pull from our victims. Nothing fits and we're always clatt'ring around. Tell ye what though, the last stint I did was a job for dem dark elves, marching with the armies of Lanys T'Vyl. Vile, that woman is, but I'll say dis for 'er -- she fixed us up with some pretty good battle mail. It's easier to wear dan da trash you're wearin' and it's lighter dan anything one of us could craft. You should fix yourself up with some of dis [design].'
You say, 'What design?'
Zermak says 'Heh heh. Before I left Lany's army, I stole da armor patterns made fo' us troll troops. Had to bash dat dark elf artisan good to get him to let go, but I got 'em! Tell you what -- I can't give away my only copy, but if you bring me da [ingredients] to make another pattern book, I can fix you up with a book of yer own. Or mebbe you already got a book and you need sometin [more].'
You say, 'What ingredients?'
Zermak says 'Good. Good. First I need a fire beetle carapace for the cover, den a patch of zombie skin for the pages, a golden bandit tooth for the clasp, and a lion tail for the spine. I hear dese ting can be found in the Western Karanas so bring dem to me and I make a pattern book for you, ya?'
Quest Items to get your Ancestral Book:
Fire Beetle Carapace, Zombie Skin, Golden Bandit Tooth, Lion Tail
Hand in the items:
Zermak says '______, you are one smarts troll to come up with dese tings so quick.  It too bad we can't fight together under Lanys no more.  Me and you could have bashed some elf heads in good, no?  Heh, heh, here's your pattern book.'
You say, 'More?'
Zermak says 'Da armor in dat book is nice an' all, but dey could be better. Mebbe I give you patterns for reinforcements if you do some tings to prove yerself. I got some other patterns, but you prolly not ready for all of dem yet. I give you patterns dependin on what you can do in [Highpass], [Lower Guk], [Old Sebilis] an' da [valley] beyond Lavastorm.'
Journeyman's Book of Troll Culture
You say, 'Highpass.'
You have been assigned the task 'Hotly Contested'.
Zermak says 'Pesky lil gnolls need to be taught a lessin. Dey always chargin' Highpass Keep yelling about the glory of Zek. Den dey get wasted by dem hoomanz and bring shame to the big Zek war face. Wait by the gate and kill dem gnolls before the humans do. . . den bring me back dere rain water.'
Hotly Contested:
1) Kill 20 Gnolls in Highpass Hold
2) Loot four Rain Waters
3) Deliver Rain Waters to Zermak
4) Kill 10 Smugglers in Highpass Hold
5) Loot one Human Blood
6) Deliver Blood to Zermak
Good. Dat work nicely. You earned yerself some reinforment [sic] patterns.
You receive the Journeyman's Book of Troll Culture.
Expert's Book of Troll Culture
You say, 'Lower Guk?'
Zermak says 'I tink you ready to get some revenge on dem froggies. Dey can't kick us out da Grobb and not pay da price! Go to der old city and scout tings out a bit! Sneak in dere amongst de undead ghoul froggies and rip 'em apart. Bring me dey phos-frus. Mebbe when you get back we see if you up for some warrin' with de real live froggies.'
You have been assigned the task 'Takin' da Fight to da Froggies'.
Takin' da Fight to da Froggies:
1) Kill 50 Froglok Ghoul in Lower Guk
2) Loot 20 Phosphorous Powder
3) Deliver Phosphorous Powder to Zermak
4) Kill five Froglok Wan Knights in Lower Guk
5) Loot one Froglok Meat
6) Deliver one Froglok Meat to Zermak
Ya! Dem froggies never knew what hit dem! Take dese patterns, dey make yer armer better.
You receive the Expert's Book of Troll Culture.
Master's Book of Troll Culture
You say, 'Old Sebilis.'
Zermak says 'De tuffest froggies are da sebilis froggies. Once you can handle dem, you can handle just bout any kind o froggy. Go deep into da ruins and flatten dem krup froglok good. Bring me back soma dose wax flowers day carry. Day look real pret. . . Um, I mean, day good for luring frogloks into me club. Har Har! Ya know wat I mean. . . Right?'
Task given to get your Master's Book:
1) Kill 80 Froglok Krups in Sebilis
2) Loot 40 Wax Flower
3) Deliver 40 Wax Flowers to Zermak
4) Kill five Froglok Ilis Shaman in Old Sebilis
5) Loot one Nodding Blue Lily
6) Deliver one Nodding Blue Lily to Zermak
Receive Master's Book.
Grandmaster's Book of Troll Culture
Task given to get your Grandmaster's Book:
1) Kill 120 Lavaspinners in Lavaspinner's Lair
2) Loot 80 Arachnid Eye Clusters
3) Deliver 4 Arachnid Eye Clusters to Zermak
4) Kill 10 Lavaspinners in Lavaspinner's Lair
5) Loot 1 Deadly Arachnid Mandibles
6) Deliver one Deadly Arachnid Mandible to Zermak

# Ogre Cultural Armor (DoN)

**Level:** 20 | **Maximum Level:** 125 | **Quest Type:** Quest | **Repeatable:** Yes | **Monster Mission:** No

**Level:** 20
**Maximum Level:** 125
**Monster Mission:** No

**Max. # of Players:** 1

Click here for an overview of cultural armor introduced with Dragons of Norrath.
Find Garth Okken in Oggok at +262, -208 inside the armor shop next to the forge.
Items required for Ancestral Ogre Armor Book:
-Cat Skull Cap from A Pickclaw Mindripper (2nd floor of Runnyeye)
-Fractured Femur from A Pickclaw Bonemender (2nd floor of Runnyeye)
-Muddite Mud from Muddites (Gorge of King Xrobb)
-Large Brick of Unrefined Ore
You say, 'Hail, Garth Okken'
Garth Okken says 'Hrmph!  What der you want? A [clubbin]?'
You say, 'I want a clubbin'
Garth Okken says 'You do wants a clubbin! Ha ha! You pass da test den! Anyone who wants a clubbin' from me must be too tuff for a clubbin! Leave me with jusa broken club! How you get to be so tuff, _____? You don't even wear da good [armor]!'
You say, 'What armor?'
Garth Okken says 'Aye, ha ha. Da gud armor! Normally ogres not good at making tings, just breakin' tings, ya? But dis one ogre, a crazy war face named Kizrak da Tyrant, he design da gud armor and gave me da [pattern] book. Told me to pass it out to da other tuff ogres. Since you tuff enough to ask me for a clubbin', you tuff enough for dis armor! Mebbe you even tuff enough to earn [medals].'
You say, 'What medals?'
Artisan Garth Okken says 'Yah. If'n you think you ready fer something a lil more challengin', I can send you on many missions.  You take as many as you want.  To prove you good, you make impressions in book.  Da book do da work so you no worry about it.'
Task given to get your Blessed Book of Ogre Culture: 'Blessed Impressions'
Task given to get your Revered Book of Ogre Culture: 'Revered Impressions':
Task given to get your Master's Book:
Details needed.
Task given to get your Grandmaster's Book:
Details needed.
Seals of Darkhollow
You say, 'What underground place?'
Garth Okken says 'Da underground is open. Get me a Purty Geode. I give you new pattern book for seals.'
You have been assigned the task 'Darkhollow Geode'.
Loot 1 Large Darkhollow Geode (0/1) - ALL
Find a Large Darkhollow Geode and bring it back.
???
Reward:  Seals of Darkhollow

6) Deliver Bone Marrow to Yar
_Receive Simple Book of Dwarven Culture._
**Task given to get your Ornate Book:**
1) Kill 50 Mermaids in Kedge Keep
2) Loot 20 Deepwater Ink
3) Deliver four Deepwater Ink to Yar Shiverbow

5) Loot one Shark Skin
6) Deliver Shark Skin to Yar Shiverbow
_Receive Ornate Book of Dwarven Culture._

1) Kill 80 Thought Horrors in The Deep (must have "thought horror" in name)

3) Return four Thought Horror Fangs to Yar Shiverbrow

Modified: Tue Dec 5 05:21:04 2023 | | **Click here** [quest=3056] for an overview of cultural armor introduced with Dragons of Norrath.
_Find Sieeva Koan in East Cabilis, location +851, -439._
You say, 'Hail, Sieeva Koan'
Sieeva Koan says 'Hello, \_\_\_\_\_. Returning home from conquests abroad, I hope. It fills me with pride to see there are still Iksar willing to venture out into Norrath and crusade for our empire once again. Too many of us have holed up in this city, dreaming of the [glory] days of the Iksar empire.'
You say, 'Glory days?'
Sieeva Koan says 'From what I have heard things were much different then, our military more refined, our craftsmanship more detailed. I have been studying the writings in the ruins of old Cabilis. They are helping me piece together the [techniques] of our ancestors.'
You say, 'What techniques?'
Sieeva Koan says 'From what I have learned, our craftsmanship has regressed over the years. In fact, I've uncovered some armor patterns and symbol patterns within the ruins that put our current designs to shame. Would you be interested in learning the [cultural armor patterns] and [cultural symbol patterns]? They will take time to master, but if you are patient, they will serve you well.'
You say, 'I am interested in learning the cultural armor patterns'
Sieeva Koan says 'Excellent. To make you a pattern book I will need a patch of bloodgill scales for the cover, a scroll for the pages, a bloodstone ring for the clasp, and a cat's eye agate to grind down and make a salve which will protect and preserve the finished product. I have heard these items can be found in the Swamp of No Hope and the Lake of Ill Omen. Bring them to me and the pattern book shall be yours.'
You say, 'What about the cultural symbol patterns?'
Sieeva Koan says 'You're interested in the cultural symbol patterns I see. I can give you the symbol patterns but I will need you to run a few tasks for me before I can give them to you. What do you say; would you like to run a few short [tasks] for me?'
You say, 'What tasks?'
Sieeva Koan says 'I have four tasks that need to be completed. The first is an adventure into [Lake of Ill Omen] where you will collect some shields for my shield collection. The next you will be tasked with venturing into the [Dreadlands] where you will confront any clans that are preventing us from doing buisness. The third task leads you into [Sebilis] where you will learn if there is any weakness in Trakanon's army. The last task will lead you onto an adventure into [Thundercrest] where you will learn about the creatures that inhabit the area.'
**Items to return for the Ancestral Iksar Armor book:**
Bloodgill Scales (dropped by bloodgill goblins)
A Scroll (dropped by sarnak adherants)
Bloodstone Ring (dropped by random low-to-midlevel mobs)
Cats Eye Agate (dropped and vendorbought)
**Task given for the Journeyman's Book: Tales of Sieeva**
1) Kill 20 Goblin Warriors in the Lake of Ill Omen
2) Loot four Green Goblin Skins
3) Return skins to Sieeva
4) Kill 10 Pond Sturgeons (Firiona Vie / Lake of Ill Omen zone line)
5) Loot one Pondfish Spine
6) Return spine to Sieeva
_Receive Journeyman's Book._
**Task given for Expert's Book: Protect the Operation**
1) Kill 50 Tundra Yeti in the Dreadlands
2) Loot 20 Frosty Datura
3) Return Frosty Daturas to Sieeva
4) Kill five Dracnid silkmistresses
5) Loot one Drachnid Silk
6) Return Drachnid Silk to Sieeva
_Receive Expert's Book._
**Task given for Master's Book: Scouting the Enemy**
1) Kill any 80 Froglok Krups in Sebilis
2) Loot 40 Wax Flowers
3) Return four Wax Flowers to Sieeva
4) Kill five Froglok Ilis Shaman
5) Loot one Nodding Blue Lilly
6) Deliver Nodding Blue Lily to Sieeva _Receive Master's Book._
**Task given for Grandmaster's Book:**
1) Kill 120 Thundercrest Isle creatures (must have 'Isle' in name)
2) Loot 80 Fractured Drake Claws
3) Return Drake Claws to Sieeva
4) ?
5) ?
6) ?
_Receive Grandmaster's Book._

Modified: Tue Dec 5 05:21:04 2023 | | **Click here** [quest=3056] for an overview of cultural armor introduced with Dragons of Norrath.
_Find Vuro Padfoot in Shar Vahl at -435, -40, in the building just inside the city's south entrance._
**Ancestral Vah Shir Armor**
You say, 'Hail, Vuro Padfoot'
Vuro Padfoot says 'Hello, \_\_\_\_\_\_. Returning from Norrath? It fills me with pride to see there are still Vah Shir willing to venture off this moon and restore our foothold on [Norrath] once again.'
You say, 'Norrath?'
Vuro Padfoot says 'From what I have heard, things were much different back when we lived on the planet. I have been studying the writings transcribed from before our kind was blasted from the face of Norrath. They are helping me piece together the [techniques] of our ancestors.'
You say, 'What techniques?'
Vuro Padfoot says 'From what I have learned, our craftsmanship has regressed over the years. Our relative lack of competition from other races has made us docile and lazy on this moon. In fact, I've uncovered some forgotten [armor patterns] and a few [cultural symbol patterns] that put our current designs to shame. Would you be interested in learning these ancient patterns? They will take time to craft, but, if you are patient, they will serve you well.'
You say, 'I am interested in learning the armor patterns.'
Vuro Padfoot says 'Excellent. To make you a pattern book, I will need a piece of shadeling silk for the cover, a glowing heart stone for the ink, a cracked rhino beetle carapace for the clasp, and a damaged hopper hide to grind down and make a salve which will protect and preserve the finished product. I have heard these items can be found around Shadeweaver's Thicket and the Paludal Caverns. Bring them to me and the armor patterns of our ancestors will be yours.'
_The Glowing Heart Stone drops only from A Glowing Muck Digger in Paludal Caverns. The rest are common drops in Shadeweaver's Thicket. Hand in the items._
Vuro Padfoot says 'Excellent work, \_\_\_\_\_\_. Here is your pattern book. May you use this to restore the standing of our race across Norrath!'
_You receive the book Ancestral Vah Shir Armor._
You say, 'What cultural symbol patterns?'
Vuro Padfoot says 'You're interested in the cultural symbol patterns I see. I can give you the cultural symbol patterns but I will need you to run a few tasks for me before I can give them to you. What do you say: would you like to run a few short [tasks] for me?'
You say, 'What tasks?'
Vuro Padfoot says 'I have four tasks that need to be completed. The first is an adventure into [Paludal Caverns] where you will collect some items for a cap that I'm making. The next, you will be tasked with venturing into [Dawnshroud] where you hunt for some ingredients that I seem to be missing for my feast. The third task leads you into [The Grey] where you will gather some jewelry supplies for me. The final task will lead you on an adventure into [Stillmoon temple] where you will learn about the creatures that inhabit the area.'
**Journeyman's Book of Vah Shir Culture**
You say, 'Paludal Caverns.'
You have been assigned the task 'Vuro's Cavern Adventure'.
Vuro Padfoot says 'Paludal Caverns? Let me see what I have for Paludal Caverns. Ohh I do have a task for Paludal Caverns. I was in the process of making a cape but I have run out of a few key items. I know they can be found in Paludal. Venture into Paludal Caverns and explore the owl bear cave in the east side of the caverns.'
Task Details
Paludal Caverns: Explore the owlbear cave in the east
Paludal Caverns: Kill 20 owlbears
Paludal Caverns: Loot 4 Damaged Owlbear Pelts
Shar Vahl: Deliver 4 Damaged Owlbear Pelts to Vuro Padfoot
Paludal Caverns: Explore the recondite camp in the south west
Paludal Caverns: Kill 10 recondites
Paludal Caverns: Loot 1 Recondite Insignia Ring
Shar Vahl: Deliver 1 Recondite Insignia Ring to Vuro Padfoot
_The cave you must explore is at -1940, -395. The recondite camp is near Shadowhaven._
Vuro Padfoot says 'You did a fine job. I will be wearing my cape in no time. You have certainly earned these cultural symbol patterns.'
_You receive the Journeyman's Book of Vah Shir Culture._
**Expert's Book of Vah Shir Culture**
You say, 'Dawnshroud.'
You have been assigned the task 'Vuro's Feast'.
Vuro Padfoot says 'I never have the right cooking utensils when I'm cooking. It's so annoying to be in the middle of baking a feast and to not have exactly what you need. Explore the pond in the north west of Dawnshroud and see if you can find some utensils for me.'
Task Details
Dawnshroud Peaks: Explore the pond in the north west
Dawnshroud Peaks: Kill 50 wolves
Dawnshroud Peaks: Loot 4 A Tattered Wolf Pelts
Shar Vahl: Deliver 4 A Tattered Wolf Pelts to Vuro Padfoot
Dawnshroud Peaks: Explore the small camp site that is setup south west of Netherbian Lair
Dawnshroud Peaks: Kill 5 stonegrabbers
Dawnshroud Peaks: Loot 1 A Stonegrabber Boulder
Shar Vahl: Deliver 1 A Stonegrabber Boulder to Vuro Padfoot
_The pond is in the northwest, as indicated. The small camp site is as +1230, +580._
Vuro Padfoot says 'Just in time, the feast is about ready. That shell should be perfect for this perfect meal. You have earned those cultural symbol patterns.'
_You receive Expert's Book of Vah Shir Culture._
**Master's Book of Vah Shir Culture**
You say, 'The Grey.'
You have been assigned the task 'Supplies for Vuro'.
Vuro Padfoot says 'I recently started to create jewelry. This piece here is the most requested piece in the city. My supplies are running low and I need you to travel to The Grey and gather some supplies for me. You can start by killing shimmering rockfiends and scraping the shiny granite chips from them.'
Task Details
The Grey: Kill 40 shimmering rockfiends
The Grey: Loot 4 Shiny Granite Chips
Shar Vahl: Deliver 4 Shiny Granite Chips to Vuro Padfoot
The Grey: Kill 5 sun revenants
The Grey: Loot 1 Dark Skeleton Remains
Shar Vahl: Deliver 1 Dark Skeleton Remains to Vuro Padfoot
_Shimmering rockfiends spawn from traps that may be found throughout The Grey. The northeast part of the zone has a relatively high concentration of the traps._
Vuro Padfoot says 'Superb work. I will grind these bones into many usable charms for my jewelry. You have earned these cultural symbol patterns for your hard work.'
_You receive Master's Book of Vah Shir Culture._
**Grandmaster's Book of Vah Shir Culture**
You say, 'Stillmoon Temple.'
You have been assigned the task 'Creatures of Stillmoon'.
Vuro Padfoot says 'You will adventure into the Stillmoon temple to learn about the different creatures that inhabit the area. Travel to Stillmoon temple and learn about the creatures of the temple. You will need to be careful while you are in the temple. The creatures that you seek can only be found in the most dangerous sections of the temple. You should travel to the Lavastorm Mountains and meet up with one of our operatives. There you will get further information on where you can enter the temple at.'
Task Details
Stillmoon Temple: Kill 120 denizens of Stillmoon
Stillmoon Temple: Loot 80 Glossy Kirin Manes
Shar Vahl: Deliver 4 Glossy Kirin Manes to Vuro Padfoot
Stillmoon Temple: Kill 10 Stillmoon Loremasters
Stillmoon Temple: Loot 1 Impeccable Kirin Horn
Shar Vahl: Deliver 1 Impeccable Kirin Horn to Vuro Padfoot
_You must be in a Dragons of Norrath mission to get the drops you need._
**Need hand-in dialogue.**
_You receive Grandmaster's Book of Vah Shir Culture._
**Need missing dialogues, faction hits if any, list of creatures that qualify for kills, etc.**
