# Froglok Cultural Armor (DoN)

**Level:** 20 | **Maximum Level:** 125 | **Quest Type:** Quest | **Repeatable:** Yes | **Monster Mission:** No


## Quest Text / Walkthrough

**Level:** 20
**Maximum Level:** 125
**Monster Mission:** No
**Repeatable:** Yes
**Can Be Shrouded?:** No
**Quest Type:** Quest
**Group Size:** Solo
**Min. # of Players:** 1
**Max. # of Players:** 1

Click here for an overview of cultural armor introduced with Dragons of Norrath.
Find Srekta Froreki in the Rathe Mountains at -2200, +190.
You say, 'Hail, Srekta Froreki'
Srekta Froreki says 'Welcome back to the swamps, ______. Cripes! You've been away for awhile; that's the worst case of dry-foot I've ever seen. You should stick around and rehabilitate; there's plenty of interesting [things] to be found right around here.'
You say, 'What things?'
Srekta Froreki says 'A group of us just returned from an expedition into deepest Guk. Turns out that some of our ancestors had learned to create some rather remarkable armor, well, you know, before they were cursed to undeath and all. The designs must have been lost during our years of enslavement but we've recovered a few pieces, enough to make a [pattern book] so we can reproduce their designs. You should see these; the craftsmanship is remarkable! We also found some [cultural symbol patterns] if you were interested in them.'
You, 'I want to make a pattern book.'
Srekta Froreki says 'Well, I only have one pattern book but I can make you a copy. To do this I will need a torn sash for the cover, a low quality cat pelt for the pages, a dune tarantula abdomen for the clasp, and a snake venom sac to grind down and make a salve which will protect and preserve the finished product. I have heard most of these items can be found in the Oasis of Marr. Bring them to me and the pattern book shall be yours.'
The items drop from various mobs in the Northern and Southern Desert of Ro, as well as the Oasis of Marr.  Hand in the items.
Srekta Froreki says 'Well done, ______. As promised, here are the armor patterns of our ancestors. May they serve you well.'
Your faction standing with Citizens of Gukta got better.
Your faction standing with Protectors of Gukta got better.
Your faction standing with High Council of Gukta got better.
Your faction standing with Lorekeepers of Gukta got better.
Your faction standing with Guktan Elders got better.
You gain experience!!
You receive Ancestral Froglok's Armor.
You say, 'What cultural symbol patterns?'
Srekta Froreki says, 'You're interested in cultural symbol patterns, I see. I can give you the cultural symbol patterns but I will need you to run a few tasks for me before I can give them to you. What do you say, would you like to run a few short [tasks] for me?'
You say, 'I will run your tasks.'
Srekta Froreki says, 'I have four tasks that need to be completed. The first is an adventure into the desert of [South Ro] where you will gather some charms to replace some broken charms on my necklace. The next you will be tasked with venturing into the heart of [Kithicor] where you will hunt for a few items that I can use to create a new cloak. The third task leads you into [Velketor's Labyrinth] where you will collect a few items to make my old hat appear like a new one. The final task I have for you is an adventure into the [Stillmoon temple] where you will learn about the creatures that inhabit the area.'
Journeyman's Book of Froglok Culture
You say, 'South Ro?'
You have been assigned the task 'Srekta's Charms'.
Srekta's Charms
1) Southern Desert of Ro:  Kill 20 dervish cutthroats
2) Southern Desert of Ro:  Loot 4 Cutthroat Golden Teeth
3) Mountains of Rathe:  Deliver 4 Cutthroat Golden Teeth to Srekta Froreki
4) Southern Desert of Ro:  Explore the merchant camp in the desert
5) Southern Desert of Ro:  Kill 10 crypt mummies
6) Southern Desert of Ro:  Loot 1 Rotting Zombie Skull
7) Mountains of Rathe:  Deliver 1 Rotting Zombie Skull to Srekta Froreki
Upon handing in the Rotting Zombie Skull:
Your task 'Srekta's Charms' has been updated.
I'm glad to see you have made it back. Let me look at that skull. Yes that is indeed rare and will go perfect on my necklace. Thank you. You have earned these cultural symbol patterns.
Receive Journeyman's Book of Froglok Culture.
Expert's Book of Froglok Culture
You say, 'Kithicor?'
You have been assigned the task 'Srekta's Cloak Collection'.
Srekta's Cloak Collection
Kithicor Forest: Kill 50 zombies
Kithicor Forest: Loot 20 Shriveled Flesh
Mountains of Rathe: Deliver 4 Shriveled Flesh to Skekta Froreki
Kithicor Forest: Explore the lake in the south east
Kithicor Forest: Kill 5 dread wolves
Kithicor Forest: Loot 1 Essence of Moonlight
Mountains of Rathe: Deliver 1 Essence of Moonlight to Skekta Froreki
Only zombies with "zombie" in the name count toward your task, but you can loot the Shriveled Flesh from anything and have it count.  Any wolf with "dread" in the name, including "dreaded," will count as a dread wolf.
Upon handing in the Essence of Moonlight:
Oh dear what a glorious cloak this will be. Thank you. You have earned these cultural symbol patterns.
You receive your Expert's Book of Froglok Culture.
Master's Book of Froglok Culture
You say, 'Velketor's Labyrinth?'
You have been assigned the task 'Srekta's Hat of Shame'.
Srekta's Hat of Shame
Velketor's Labyrinth: Kill 80 icepaw kobolds
Velketor's Labyrinth: Loot 20 Icepaw Kobold's Paw
Mountains of Rathe: Deliver 4 Icepaw Kobold's Paw to Srekta Froreki
Velketor's Labyrinth: Explore the entrance to the labyrinth
Velketor's Labyrinth: Kill 5 gargoyles
Velketor's Labyrinth: Loot 1 Block of Velium
Mountains of Rathe: Deliver 1 Block of Velium to Srekta Froreki
Upon handing in the Block of Velium:
Your task 'Srekta's Hat of Shame' has been updated.
What a beautiful piece of velium you have there. It will look wonderful on my hat as I parade around the streets of Gukta. You have earned these cultural symbol patterns.
Has this dialogue changed since the frogloks were kicked out of Gukta?
You receive your Master's Book of Froglok Culture.
Grandmaster's Book of Froglok Culture.
You say, 'Stillmoon Temple?'
You have been assigned the task 'Scouting the Temple'
Scouting the Temple
Stillmoon Temple: Kill 120 Stillmoon creatures
Stillmoon Temple: Recover 80 patches of rough goblin skin from Stillmoon goblins
Mountains of Rathe: Take 4 samples of the rough goblin skin to Srekta
Stillmoon Temple: Kill 10 stillmoon dirt tenders
Stillmoon Temple: Recover a stone necklace from the stillmoon dirt tenders
Mountains of Rathe: Deliver the stone necklace to Srekta
Upon handing in the stone necklace:
Need completion dialogues.
You receive your Grandmaster's Book of Froglok Culture.
Types of Armor
Farsight - Rogue, Shaman
Lorekeeper - Necromancer, Wizard
Protectorate - Cleric, Paladin, Shadowknight, Warrior

Modified: Tue Dec 5 05:21:04 2023 | | **Notice: Cultural tradeskills were recently given a major overhaul. Much of this information may be outdated.**
**We would like to have this guide on the wiki. The wiki article about Dragons of Norrath Cultural Armor can be found here. If you have any information to contribute, you can do so directly if you desire.**

---

This is an overview of how Cultural Armor quests in Dragons of Norrath work. For more detailed information related to individual races, use the quest links on the lefthand side of this writeup.
This cultural armor works in tiers (note the indicated levels are requirements for getting the tasks from quest NPCs; you can make the low-stat armor at earlier levels, but not the augmentations):
First Tier: Simple
-Tradeskill components obtainable at 30th level.
Second Tier: Ornate
-Tradeskill components obtainable at 40th level.
Third Tier: Intricate
-Tradeskill components obtainable at 60th level.
Fourth Tier: Elaborate
-Tradeskill components obtainable earlier than 70th level, but you won't be able to get the task for the required book until then.
There are plate, chain, leather, and silk armor combines. Plate and chain is done through smithing in cultural forges. Leather and silk is done through tailoring in cultural sewing kits.
Note that mold and pattern combines produce an eight-slot backpack full with the following items:
20x Chest Slot
20x Legs Slot
20x Arms Slot
20x Head Slot
20x Hands Slot
20x Feet Slot
40x Wrist Slot
Navigation: The Books [quest=3056] \| Armor Production [quest=3056] \| Symbol (Aug) Production [quest=3056] \| Trivials [quest=3056] \| Appendix [quest=3056]
(Links found in this guide go to a same-page appendix.)
..........................................................................................................................
**Obtain Armor Books**
See the "Tradeskill Quests" [quest=3056] NPC in your home city. Perform tasks to obtain an Ancestral Armor Book, Simple Book, Ornate Book, Intricate Book, and Elaborate Book.
..........................................................................................................................
**Smithing Recipes:**
MOLDS = Ancestral Armor Book + High Quality Firing Sheet + Huge Block of Clay + Compacted Backpack + Sculpting Tools
BARS [quest=3056] = Cultural Metal [quest=3056] \+ Item Drop [quest=3056] \+ Cultural Hammer [quest=3056]
SHEETS [quest=3056] = Cultural Hammer [quest=3056] \+ Water Flask + (Tier's) Bar [quest=3056] (made in the above combine)
ARMOR PRODUCTION [quest=3056] = Armor Mold + # (Tier's) Sheet(s)/Bar(s) [quest=3056] \+ Large Brick of Cultural Metal [quest=3056] \+ Cultural Hammer [quest=3056]
\*\*NOTE: # is 3 for Chest and Legs; 2 for Feet and Head; 1 for Arms, Hands, and Wrists
\*\*NOTE: Chain combines use BARS; Plate combines use SHEETS
_This produces armor with minor stats and augmentation slots 7, 11, 12._
\- \- \- - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - -
**Tailoring Recipes:**
NEEDLE [quest=3056] = Embroidering Needle + Cultural Brewing Essence [quest=3056]
SEWING KIT [quest=3056] = Water Flask + Needle Mold + Thimble Mold + Small Brick of Cultural Metal [quest=3056]
\*\*NOTE: Just to make things a little more complicated, Wood Elves and Halflings use bits instead of a small brick.
PATTERNS = Ancestral Armor Book + Roll of Plain Parchment + Large Bottle of Ink + Compacted Backpack + A Quill
SWATCHES [quest=3056] = Cultural Spool of Thread [quest=3056] \+ Cultural Needle [quest=3056] \+ Culture-Specific Silk Swatch or Dropped Hide/Pelt [quest=3056]
ARMOR PRODUCTION [quest=3056] = Armor Pattern + # (Tier's) Swatch(es) [quest=3056] (produced in the above combine) + Large Spool of Cultural Thread [quest=3056] \+ Cultural Needle [quest=3056]
\\*\\* NOTE: # is 3 for Chest and Legs; 2 for Feet and Head; 1 for Arms, Hands, and Wrists
_This produces armor with minor stats and augmentation slots 7, 11, 12._
..........................................................................................................................
**The Symbols (Augmentations)**
Symbols can be made by anyone of any deity in any culture's forge or sewing kit.
SYMBOL PATTERN PRODUCTION:
Inside a Book Binding (bought from local vendors), produce (Tier's) (Slot) Symbol Pattern:
Recipe = (Tier's) Book of (Race) Culture + Compacted Backpack + A Quill + Large Bottle of Ink + Roll of Vellum
(\*\*Quality of Vellum varies by Tier: Blessed = Roll; Revered = Good; Sacred = High Quality; Eminent = Superb)
**Blessed Water of ( Deity [quest=3056])** can be bought from local vendors.
**JEWELCRAFT COMBINES:**
Blessed Water of ( Deity Name [quest=3056]) = Blessed Water + Jade Shard
Revered Water of ( Deity Name [quest=3056]) = Blessed Water + Lambent Stone
Sacred Water of ( Deity Name [quest=3056]) = Blessed Water + Crushed Diamond Dust
Eminent Water of ( Deity Name [quest=3056]) = Blessed Water + Purescale Ore
**SLOT 11 AUGMENTATION PRODUCTION (SMITHED):**
(Tier's) (Armor) Symbol of (Deity Name)
**Recipe** = (Tier's) Water of (Deity Name) + (Tier's) (Slot) Symbol Pattern + Item
( _\*\*Item varies by Tier: Blessed = Warbone Chips; Revered = Fire Opal; Sacred = Shissar Scales; Eminent = Metallic Drake Scales_)
**SLOT 11 AUGMENTATION PRODUCTION (TAILORED):**
(Tier's) (Armor) Symbol of (Deity Name)
**Recipe** = (Tier's) Water of (Deity Name) + (Tier's) (Slot) Symbol Pattern + Item
( _\*\*Item varies by Tier: Blessed = Silk Swatch; Revered = Drachnid Silk Swatch; Sacred = Nightmare Arachnid Swatch; Eminent = Glossy Drake Hide_)
..........................................................................................................................

**For an overview of the Dragons of Norrath expansion, see this wiki article.**
**For an overview of progression, see quest entries for Norrath's Keepers [quest=3061] and the Dark Reign [quest=3062].**

---

_This raid task is of Planes of Power Elemental-to-Time difficulty. It begins with Lieutenant Ekiltu Verlor ( **click for map image**) in the Norrath's Keepers camp of the Lavastorm Mountains. This task has a 3-day, 20-hour lockout timer._
_Pre-requisites for requesting this task:_
\- Minimum 6 players; maximum 42 players
\- Must be kindly faction with Norrath's Keepers
\- Completion of all Tier 3 tasks and flags
You say, 'Hail, Lieutenant Ekiltu Verlor'
Lieutenant Ekiltu Verlor says 'Those faithful to the way of Norrath's Keepers may take on the greatest challenges and prove their worth to Firiona Vie.'
You have been assigned the task 'Guardian of the Sands'.
There have been several sightings of a massive guardian that has risen in the sand gardens of Stillmoon Temple. This guardian has been very protective of the area and we have lost contact with several scouts that were sent to gather more information. The scouts that have survived the attacks report that the guardian is allied with the sand goblins of the region. It is simply too dangerous to continue our exploration of the temple as long as that guardian is around. We need you to remove it.

---

**Task Steps**
Touch the portal to Stillmoon Temple 0/1 (The Broodlands)
Confront the Guardian 0/1 (Stillmoon Temple)
**TASK LOCKS HERE**
Kill Shogurei 0/1 (Stillmoon Temple)

---

**The Event**
_Warning! While in this zone, keep off the sand. Stepping on the sand causes multiple goblin spawns that aggro your party (additional to static spawns already found in the zone's base population)._
_Clear your way to "Shogurei, Guardian of the Sands" (far northeastern part of the zone), and engage him (he is NOT rooted in place, so can be pulled - unknown if any leash range), and kill him. This is a fairly straightforward encounter._
Shogurei, Guardian of the Sands says 'You have defiled this place with your murderous presence. As life bleeds out of you and you gasp your final breath, know that you have met your end at the hands of Shogurei, guardian of these sacred sands.'
_He hits for a max ~2,300; has ~775,000 hitpoints; flurries; single-target rampages; and casts a few spells:_
Denial of Flight: Targeted AE 150', Magic (-300)
1: Decrease HP when cast by 7500
2: Root
Explosion of Sand: PB AE 500', Magic (-250)
1: Decrease HP when cast by 1500
2: Decrease Attack Speed by 30%
3: Decrease Movement by 30%
Hammer of Absolution: AE PC 250', Magic (-300)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 3000
2: Stun (5.00 sec)

---

**Completion & Loot**
_The task completes upon his death._
Shogurei, Guardian of the Sands has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
Shogurei, Guardian of the Sands's corpse says 'The garden stands defiled, but you have brought me honor in defeat.'
Your faction standing with Stillmoon Acolytes has been adjusted by -1.
Your faction standing with Kessdona has been adjusted by -1.
Your faction standing with Rikkukin has been adjusted by -1.
With the guardian out of the way, exploration of the temple can continue.
_He drops 9x "Quintessence of Sand" + 2 items from this loot table:_
Cape of Serenity
Fired Glass Bracelet
Necklace of Sandstorms
Polished Sunsword
Sand-Molded Leather Bracer
Wrapped Glass Shard
_Task Rewards:_
9x "Radiant Crystal"
1x "Norrath's Keepers Token" (faction **turn-in** [quest=3134] item)
_Note: If doing this raid as part of progression, you'll want to check in with some NPCs to make sure your flags are up to date. See **this quest entry** [quest=3061] for more details._
**Submitted by:** Saraban of Darkwind

**For an overview of the Dragons of Norrath expansion, see this wiki article.**
**For an overview of progression, see quest entries for Norrath's Keepers [quest=3061] and the Dark Reign [quest=3062].**

---

_This raid task is of Planes of Power elemental difficulty and has a 2-day, 20-hour lockout timer. It begins with Lieutenant Ekiltu Verlor ( **click for map image**) in the Norrath's Keepers camp of the Lavastorm Mountains._
_Pre-requisites for requesting this task:_
\- Level 68+
\- Minimum "amiable" faction with Norrath's Keepers
\- Minimum 6 players; maximum 24 players
\- Completion of "Turn the Tides of Sand"
\- Completion of "Recover the Lost Map"
\- Completion of "Defend the Kirin Contact"
\- Completion of "Sickness of the Spirit" (group mission)
You say, 'Hail, Lieutenant Ekiltu Verlor'
Lieutenant Ekiltu Verlor says 'Those faithful to the way of Norrath's Keepers may take on the greatest challenges and prove their worth to Firiona Vie.'
You have been assigned the task 'Calling Emoush'.
You must hurry. The Dark Reign are slowly making their way deeper into Tirranun's Delve. We have heard they seek a magic that the delve goblins possess that allows them to create a great, sentient, and dangerous dervish of stone. This dervish is a danger to anyone in the delve, but if the Dark Reign are able to capture and harness the dervish, they will most certainly use it in the army they build. You must find a way to put an end to this magic posthaste!

---

Touch the portal to Tirranun's Delve 0/1 (The Broodlands)
Confront Emoush the Destroyer 0/1 (Tirranun's Delve)
**TASK LOCKS HERE**
Kill Emoush the Destroyer 0/1 (Tirranun's Delve)

---

_The entrance to the instanced zone is a cave in the northeastern part of the Broodlands._
_Once in the zone, make your way to the far north end (general mob population is no more difficult than the standard zone). There, you'll get a location update for the second step (which also locks the task)._
_You'll see a dervish named Emoush the Destroyer alongside three goblins: Mystic Shiflor, Mystic Griknok and Mystic Braggle._
_Emoush the Destroyer isn't yet attackable (non-aggro). It will only be active (aggro/attackable) as long as all three goblin Mystics are in their unconscious state. To render a Mystic unconscious, DPS it to 0% at which point it collapses to the ground. Three minutes later, it wakes up again and re-aggros._
_With this in mind, you'll want to 'kill' the three Mystics as close to the same time as possible so that you have a larger timeframe to DPS Emoush the Destroyer (who regenerates some even while invulnerable). Each Mystic hits for a max ~1,100; has ~90,000 hitpoints; and is immune to all crowd control methods._
Mystic Griknok says 'You no stop us! Emoush will destroy!'
Mystic Braggle says 'You no stop us! Emoush will destroy!'
Mystic Shiflor says 'You no stop us! Emoush will destroy!'
The goblin mystic falls to the ground unconscious.
Your faction standing with Minions of Tirranun has been adjusted by -1.
Your faction standing with Tirranun has been adjusted by -1.
_Once all three Mystics are in their unconscious states, you see:_
The cavern trembles as Emoush awakens.
Emoush the Destroyer swells with magic as it glides toward you.
_Emoush the Destroyer is undead; hits for a max ~1,500; flurries; has ~300,000 hitpoints; is immune to fire-based damage; and casts "Rumbling Stone":_
Rumbling Stone: PB AE 350', Prismatic (-350)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 1000
2: Stun (1.00 sec)
_If a Mystic wakes up (three minute timer on their deaths), Emoush goes non-aggro / unattackable:_
Emoush is once again protected by the goblin magic empowering him.
_Once again, kill all three Mystics to resume attacking Emoush._
_NOTE: Each time the Mystics wake up, Emoush increases its max hitpoints (health) and increases its max hit._

---

**Completion & Loot**
Emoush the Destroyer has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
Emoush the Destroyer's corpse falls to the ground inanimate.
Your faction standing with Minions of Tirranun has been adjusted by -1.
Your faction standing with Tirranun has been adjusted by -1.
Emoush is gone. The goblin's plans now thwarted, you rest safely in the knowledge that your services will not go unrewarded.
_Emoush the Destroyer drops 6x "Glowing Stone Fragment" (progression item) + 1 item from its loot table:_
Band of Ashen Rain
Lava Crystal Scepter
Ring of Living Malachite
Spider Queen Incubation Band
_Task Rewards:_
11 Radiant Crystals
1x "Norrath's Keepers Token" (faction **turn-in** [quest=3134] item)
**Submitted by:** Saraban of Darkwind

- Lava Crystal Scepter [item=34938]

!**Faction with Norrath's Keepers**
_The Norrath's Keepers camp can be found in the Lavastorm Mountains tunnels that lead to The Broodlands (they are in the tunnels west of the Broodlands). In order to begin more advanced tasks within the Norrath's Keepers camp, you must be of "indifferent" faction or higher._
_There will be faction requirements as you move along in progression:_
\- Tier 1 requires "indifferent"+
\- Tier 2 requires "amiable"+
\- Tier 3 requires high "amiable"+
\- Tier 4 requires "kindly"+
\- Tier 5 requires "warmly"+
DoN progression faction amounts for Norrath's Keepers
You start at -100: apprehensive (good toons)
or start at -600: mid-threatening (evil toons)
+100 (good toons): 5 tasks
+600 (evil toons): 30 tasks
0: indifferent. Start Tier 1.
+30: 3 solo tasks
+20: Children of Gimblax (group)
+60: 3 extra tokens from junk missions
110: amiable. Start Tier 2
+30: 3 solo tasks
+20: Sickness of the Spirit (group)
+20: Calling Emoush (raid)
+120: 6 extra tokens from junk missions
300: mid-amiable (+400 on the faction window). Start Tier 3
+30: 3 solo tasks
+20: History of the Isle (group)
+20: Trial of Perseverance (raid)
+20: Volkara's Bite (raid)
+120: 6 extra tokens from junk missions
510: kindly.(+600 on the faction window) Start Tier 4
+20: Guardian of the Sands (raid)
+20: Goblin Dojo (raid)
+20: An End to the Storms (raid)
+180: 9 extra tokens from junk missions
750: warmly. (+850 on the faction window) Start Tier 5
+20: Origins of the Curse (group)
+20: The Curse of Ju'rek (raid)
+20: In the Shadows (raid)
_There are several ways to increase faction:_
\- Kill Dark Reign guards (+1 per kill).
\- Wayfarer Mercenary Elanye's solo tasks (available at apprehensive **or lower**):
\-\-\- Blood of the Winged (+20)
\-\-\- Goblin Broth (+20)
\- Wayfarer Mercenary Bitral's solo tasks (available at apprehensive **or lower**):
\-\-\- Population Control (+20)
\-\-\- Gathering Silk (+20)
\- "Norrath's Keepers Tokens" are rewarded from all group missions and raids (LORE item, so turn it in each time you get one):
\-\-\- Turned in to Wayfarers Mercenary Youra (at apprehensive or lower) or Tatsujiro the Serene (in the Norrath's Keepers camp at indifferent+) (+20 faction each)
\- There are 37 non-progression group missions (the first eight are available at apprehensive):
\-\-\- Boldger Bristlebeard (Lavaspinner's Lair & Tirranun's Delve):
\-\-\-\-\-\- Grounding the Drakes (apprehensive)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Lavaspinner's Locals (apprehensive)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Diving for Lavarocks (indifferent)
\-\-\-\-\-\- The Drake Menace (indifferent)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Infested (amiable)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Lavaspinner Hunting (amiable)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Storming the Goblin Temple (kindly)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Forbin's Elixir (kindly)
\-\-\-\-\-\- A Goblin's Escort (warmly)
\-\-\-\-\-\- A Halfling's Greed (warmly)
\-\-\- Bianca Galbraith (Thundercrest Isles):
\-\-\-\-\-\- Simple Task (apprehensive)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Throes of Contagion (apprehensive)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Scions of Thundercrest (indifferent)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Splitting the Storm (indifferent)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Holy Hour (amiable)
\-\-\-\-\-\- The Creator (amiable)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Behind Closed Doors (kindly)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Lair Unguarded (kindly)
\-\-\-\-\-\- House of the Autumn Rose (warmly)
\-\-\- Kanetheus Forestwalker (Stillmoon Temple & The Ascent):
\-\-\-\-\-\- Animated Statue Plans (apprehensive)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Tracking the Kirin (apprehensive)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Death Comes Swiftly (indifferent)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Diseased Pumas (indifferent)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Best Laid Plans (amiable)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Storm Dragon Scales (amiable)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Scales of Justice (kindly)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Tea for Thy Master (kindly)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Signal Fires (warmly)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Sudden Tremors (warmly)
\-\-\- Gordish Frozenheart (Accursed Nest):
\-\-\-\-\-\- Lair of the Blackwing (apprehensive)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Scrap Metal (apprehensive)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Spider's Eye (indifferent)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Dragon's Egg (indifferent)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Lost Comrades (amiable)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Web of Lies (amiable)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Clues (kindly)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Rivals (kindly)
\- There are two non-progression mini-raids:
\-\-\- Gordish Frozenheart:
\-\-\-\-\-\- Circle of Drakes (amiable+)
\-\-\-\-\-\- The Rampaging Monolith (amiable+)
\- There are three non-progression full-size raids:
\-\-\- Captain Areha Burina:
\-\-\-\-\-\- Fanning the Flames (amiable+)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Reflections of Silver (high amiable+)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Kessdona's Perch (high amiable+)
\- You'll also gain faction regularly as you go through the progression itself.

---

**Tier 1: Gift of the Keepers**
_Upon reaching indifferent faction (and if Level 65+), you can begin progression by speaking with Chieftain Relae Aderi:_
You say, 'Hail, Chieftain Relae Aderi'
Chieftain Relae Aderi nods his head in acknowledgement and smiles. 'Welcome to the Norrath's Keepers' enclave. We are busy protecting the way of the right and honorable now. Our world is at a crossroads and we must endeavor to ensure we maintain order as much as we can. Should you wish to [help] us, do say so.'
You say, 'I wish to help you'
Chieftain Relae Aderi says 'Those are encouraging words and we will gladly take your offer. Please see Nylaen Kel`Ther, my trusted friend. While his tasks may not seem glorious, they are necessary for us to gain a foothold against the Dark Reign, the followers of Lanys T`Vyl, the Child of Hate. They have their grim eyes set on seeing the end of us all. To begin, from the volcano, we need ingredients to create an antidote, help collecting Delvian teeth for our arrowheads, and handling goblin greed in the Stillmoon Temple. When you satisfy these needs, then we shall speak again.'
You have received a character flag!
_Now talk with Private Nylaen Kel`Ther, next to Chieftain Relae Aderi to receive three different solo tasks:_
Creating an Antidote
Burning Arrows
Greed of the Goblins
_After completing these tasks, speak with Chieftain Relae Aderi again:_
You say, 'Hail, Chieftain Relae Aderi'
Chieftain Relae Aderi says 'Welcome back, \_\_\_\_\_. News of your good deeds is passing quickly through our ranks. I wish we could say your work is done here, but it is not. The goblins of Tirranun's Delve heard of your efforts against their kin in the Stillmoon Temple and they have vowed revenge. One in particular, Gimblax, plans to hunt you down. We know where he is now and if you move quickly, perhaps you can reach him first. Speak to Dilar Nelune and he will give you further guidance.'
You have received a character flag!
_Next, speak with Keeper Dilar Nelune to receive a single-group task:_
Children of Gimblax
_After completing this task, speak with Chieftain Relae Aderi again:_
You say, 'Hail, Chieftain Relae Aderi'
Chieftain Relae Aderi says 'It is time to repay you for all you have done. Firiona Vie has chosen to share a divine gift with you that will only grow stronger if you continue to help us. This magic to be instilled with you is a fitting reward, but you will fast lose it should you ever aid the Dark Reign -- in fact, it will disappear instantly!'
You have received a character flag!
You have gained the ability "Gift of the Keepers" at a cost of 0 ability points.
_The new ability "Gift of the Keepers" gives a maximum stats increase of 10 per stat._

---

**Tier 2: Valor of the Keepers**
_You must have amiable faction standing or better to request Tier 2 tasks. You can raise faction by doing the miscellaneous missions offered by the NPCs in the camp (each mission results in a Token which can be turned in for +20 faction). First speak with Chieftain Relae Aderi again:_
You say, 'Hail, Chieftain Relae Aderi'
_If below amiable, you see:_
Chieftain Relae Aderi says 'Your contributions certainly do shine, \_\_\_\_\_, but to further help us is a matter of trust. You must prove that you are ready for the higher levels of confidentiality by aiding us with more work.'
_If amiable or higher, you see:_
Chieftain Relae Aderi says 'I am delighted to see that you got to Gimblax before he was able to get to you. It would be a great loss to us if he were to find you and carry out his plans! You have done so well for us that we have much more for you to do if you are still willing. If you wish to help us turn the sand goblins' anger, recover the lost map, and protect our kirin contact, please speak to Nylaen Kel`Ther and he will give you more [work]. May the light of the world shine upon you, \_\_\_\_\_!'
You say, 'What work?'
Chieftain Relae Aderi says 'This may be presumptuous and I'm not certain the kind of company you keep, but should you find the will and way, there are stories of a dragon of lava that goes by the very name of that volcano, Tirranun. We have found an entrance that would seem to lead to its lair, but none of us have found the numbers or bravery to attempt to find it. With your associations, perhaps you would have better luck, at your leisure of course. We must learn all we can about the dragons for we fear that the Dark Reign wish to gain control over them to use against us. In the meantime, we have bestowed upon you a reward for your assistance. Safe travels, \_\_\_\_\_.'
You have received a character flag!
_(This flag grants you access to the non-progression raid "Fanning the Flames".)_
_Now, speak with Private Nylaen Kel`Ther (need to be Level 67+) to receive three solo tasks:_
Turn the Tides of the Sand
Recover the Lost Map
Defend the Kirin Contact
_After completing these three tasks, speak with Chieftain Relae Aderi again:_
You say, 'Hail, Chieftain Relae Aderi'
Chieftain Relae Aderi says 'Your advancement and accomplishments are many and you have our gratitude, but I must implore you to continue to help us. There are few as capable as you to accept the challenges that lie ahead. Dilar Nelune has been charged with giving you the information needed to making an offering of peace and reassurance to our kirin contact. Also, I have permitted Ekiltu Verlor to give you access to all the information you would need to bring an end to the terrible dervish, Emoush. Bring me evidence you have overcome this challenge.'
You have received a character flag!
_Next, hail Keeper Dilar Nelune to receive a group mission:_
Sickness of the Spirit
_After that, speak with Lieutenant Ekiltu Verlor for your first raid event:_
Calling Emoush (6-24 players)
_Loot the "Glowing Stone Fragment" from Emoush the Destroyer, and bring it to the Chieftain to complete Tier 2 progression:_
Chieftain Relae Aderi says 'Norrath's Keepers acknowledges your steadfastness and resolve in our name and Firiona herself wishes to strengthen the gift she has bestowed upon you so you may serve us with greater power.'
You have received a character flag!
_You get 1-2 AAs and the Special AA "Valor of the Keepers" which adds 250 hp/mana/end to your base stats. You also receive your 69th level spell/tome:_
Bard - Spell: Storm Blade
Beastlord - Spell: Growl of the Panther
Berserker - Tome of Destroyer's Volley
Cleric - Spell: Blessed Touch (scribes as "Ward of Retribution")
Druid - Spell: Serpent Vines
Enchanter - Spell: Rune of Rikkukin
Magician - Spell: Summon: Molten Orb
Monk - Tome of Dragon Fang
Necromancer - Spell: Shadow Orb
Paladin - Tome of Guard of Righteousness
Ranger - Spell: Earthen Shackles
Rogue - Tome of Assassin's Feint
Shadowknight - Tome of Soul Shield
Shaman - Spell: Spirit of the Panther
Warrior - Tome of Cyclone Attack
Wizard - Spell: Claw of Vox

---

**Tier 3: Embrace of the Keepers**
_To request Tier 3 content, you must be at least high amiable with Norrath's Keepers (+400) and be at least Level 68._
You say, 'Hail, Chieftain Relae Aderi'
Chieftain Relae Aderi bows as he recognizes you. 'Your good deeds in the name of the Keepers is gaining you a great reputation and the requests for your assistance come from more and more places. I would ask you to continue with your good work and we still have several tasks you may be interested in. Your skill and prowess have earned you entrance into the more [lauded] circles of our ranks, so what we ask now is of greater importance.'
You have received a character flag!
_(This flag grants you access to the non-progression raids "Reflections of Silver" & "Kessdona's Perch".)_
You say, 'Lauded?'
Chieftain Relae Aderi says 'Indeed. The people who now know and respect you are some of the closest to Firiona Vie, the Chosen of Tunare, and the leader of Norrath's Keepers. If you wish to stand alongside those who support and serve the Chosen, then you must continue to prove your worth as they all did. There are many [quests] that must be undertaken. You must speak to Nylaen Kel`Ther for those. And you must meet Areha Burina. She has uncovered a path to the [wyrms] of Stillmoon Temple, though our interest in them right now isn't immediate or required.'
You say, 'What quests?'
Chieftain Relae Aderi says 'You must protect the purity of the waters of Stillmoon Temple, secure the knowledge of the scrolls of the temple, and thwart the attack of the Snowfoot goblins. Speak to Nylaen again and he will give you more information. When you are successful, return to me and I will direct you to my officers for more challenging work.'
You say, 'What wyrms?'
Chieftain Relae Aderi says 'There are two dragons that protect and serve in the Stillmoon Temple and Ascent -- Rikkukin and Kessdona. They are mates of a breed of dragon made of precious metals. They are believed to be the first children of Veeshan on Norrath. They are dragons of great wisdom and knowledge, but don't be fooled by their sleek beauty. Once in the past, I believe dragons of their kind had betrayed our own Firiona. We fear they intend to betray the good of Norrath too if we lend our minds and hearts to them. You will nearly need an army of your own to overcome these dragons and we have no requirement of you to that end, but they are yours to discover.'
_Speak with Private Nylaen Kel`Ther to receive three solo tasks:_
Protect the Waters
The Sacred Scrolls
Snowfoot Revenge
_Once done those tasks, speak with the Chieftain again for a flag:_
You say, 'Hail, Chieftain Relae Aderi'
Chieftain Relae Aderi nods. 'We are always on the defensive and must continue to keep our minds and spirits up and charging ahead. The tasks we present to you now are more daunting. Having proven yourself and gained our trust brings greater challenges to you. We ask you to learn the history of Thundercrest Isle, endure a Trial of Perseverance issued by the goblins in the Stillmoon Temple, and take on the great spider queen, Volkara. You'll need to speak to Ekiltu Verlor for particulars and then you must return to me and give me proof you have overcome these tasks. And do introduce yourself to Areha Burina.'
You have received a character flag!
_Next, speak with Keeper Dilar Nelune for a group mission:_
History of the Isle
_You'll also need to complete two progression raids from Lieutenant Ekiltu Verlor:_
Trial of Perseverance (6-24 players)
Volkara's Bite (6-42 players)
_Loot "Goblin Warlord's Beads" from the warlord in the "Trial of Perseverance", and loot "Lava Spider Spinners" from Volkara in "Volkara's Bite". Hand in these two items to Chieftain Relae Aderi:_
Chieftain Relae Aderi says 'You've become a great contributor to our cause, \_\_\_\_\_. There will be gifts bestowed upon you for this!'
You have received a character flag!
Chieftain Relae Aderi says 'Your deeds continue to astound and impress us, even Firiona Vie has taken notice of you as we carry on about your good work. She has decided to grant you greater power once again.'
You have received a character flag!
_You get 1-2 AAs and the Special AA "Embrace of the Keepers" which increases your buff slot limit._

---

**Tier 4: Power of the Keepers**
_In order to continue on in Tier 4, you must, again, have kindly faction. Hail Commander Zaerr Ty`Dar for another flag:_
You say, 'Hail, Chieftain Relae Aderi'
Chieftain Relae Aderi says 'Every day we learn more about the Nest and, sadly, the intentions of the Dark Reign toward the good folk of Norrath. These are indeed troubling times, but I have no doubt that we will prevail with Firiona at our side. Go speak to Ekiltu Verlor. With your skills and talents, we believe you may have a good chance to overcome Shogurei, the Guardian of the Sands. With a smaller force, you may be able to accept the challenge issued by the goblins of Thundercrest Isles. We must attempt to beat this trial ahead of the Dark Reign so that perhaps the goblins may favour us and ease their anger towards us. You must bring proof you have conquered both of these tasks.'
You have received a character flag!
_Now, you need to complete two progression raids from Lieutenant Ekiltu Verlor:_
Guardian of the Sands (6-42 players)
Goblin Dojo (6-24 players)
_Loot "Quintessence of Sand" and "Meditation Stone" from those two raids (respectively), and give them to Chieftain Relae Aderi:_
Chieftain Relae Aderi says 'You are indeed rising in everyone's estimation as you continue to overcome great odds Your efforts will not go unnoticed.'
You have received a character flag!
Chieftain Relae Aderi says 'You have done a great service to Norrath's Keepers. What you have learned may have given us the edge we need over the Dark Reign. They are forever sending spies and doing all they can to undermine our efforts to bring peace and harmony in Norrath. While you have risen in our estimation since we first set eyes upon you, you would like to put your [dedication] to the test before we admit you as a Keeper.'
You say, 'My dedication?'
Chieftain Relae Aderi says 'Lanys T`Vyl and her Dark Reign lackeys grow stronger each day and there is little time to waste. Areha Burina has some extremely challenging work for you, particularly a meeting with the dragon of [storms].'
You say, 'What dragon of storms?'
Chieftain Relae Aderi says 'We expect that Yar`Lir, the storm dragon, is exceedingly powerful and unique. We have learned from you and the scrolls obtained from the temple, that Yar`Lir is the dragon responsible for casting a veil of magic and storms over the Nest to protect it from view and keep it hospitable for the wyrms. You must go to see her, but I would expect her to be none-to-pleased to see you there and you will likely need to [defend] yourself. However, we would not be the keepers of these lands if we did not at least attempt to approach her civilly.'
You say, 'Defend myself?'
Chieftain Relae Aderi says 'Should she attack you and you are forced to draw your weapons, know this -- killing her will disrupt the veil she casts here. This might be the opportunity that the Dark Reign looks for, but we will contend with that from here. There is more, we believe, as we haven't found where most of the dragons eggs are kept in the Nest. This is why we are sending you -- to learn what you can. Also, if you can, retrieve one of Yar`Lir's fangs. Areha Burina will lead you to her.'
You have received a character flag!
_Now, speak with Captain Areha Burina to request another progression raid:_
An End to the Storms (6-54 players)
_Loot "Yar'lir's Fang" from this raid, and hand it to Chieftain Relae Aderi:_
Chieftain Relae Aderi says 'Our efforts to keep the Dark Reign at bay have been furthered by your honor and prowess, \_\_\_\_\_. And once more Firiona wishes to grant you even greater magic and strength to carry you forward.'
_You get 1-2 AAs + the Special AA "Power of the Keepers" (increases chance of critical hits/spells, heals, and DoTs)_

---

**Tier 5: Sanctity of the Keepers**
_In order to continue on in Tier 5, you must have at least warmly faction. Speak with Chieftain Relae Aderi:_
You say, 'Hail, Chieftain Relae Aderi'
Chieftain Relae Aderi says, 'As we feared, Yar'lir was not kind to us, but it is to be expected. This fang will be treasured and protected while our researcher study it. It is not time to dawdle though. I'm sure you're already aware that the death of Yar'Lir caused a great disturbance in the Nest. Each time the storm dragon's magic is interrupted, the Nest and resting place of the eggs of the Nest's dragons become darker and we're not sure why. In order to learn more, please talk to Dilar Nelune and Ekiltu Verlor. They have new work for you -- to investigate a curse in the Nest and to learn about a dark drake by the name of Ju'rek. You will need to bring me proof of your success.'
_Next, speak with Keeper Dilar Nelune for a group mission:_
Origins of the Curse
_You'll also need to complete another progression raid from Lieutenant Ekiltu Verlor:_
The Curse of Ju'rek (6-24 players)
_Loot "Diseased Wing Fragment" from this raid, and hand it to Chieftain Relae Aderi:_
Chieftain Relae Aderi says 'Gracious, that curse certainly seems to run deep. You have accomplished what very few have been able to and we'd like to reward you. As you may have already ascertained, there is a [dragon] in the Nest that we have yet to confront. That falls to you now.'
You have received a character flag!
You say, 'What dragon?'
Chieftain Relae Aderi says 'We hear that the dragon in the Nest was Yar'Lir's mate, also a dragon of storms. But as you confronted Yar'Lir and disrupted her magic, the twisting caverns of the Nest were fully bared and a sickly darkness washed over them. It seems the curse you investigated was somehow unleashed at that moment and the dragon of the Nest was touched and darkened by it, but we don't know more than that. He is angry, though, without question. Speak to Areha Burina.'
You have received a character flag!
_Now, speak with Captain Areha Burina to request a final progression raid:_
In the Shadows (6-54 players)
_Loot "Shadowscale of Vishimtar" from this raid, and hand it in to Chieftain Relae Aderi to finish off your progression:_
Chieftain Relae Aderi says 'Your wondrous deeds will become woven into bards' songs, \_\_\_\_\_! You are a soldier of balance and good and for that Firiona is granting the greatest divine gift that any more can bear. Use it well and continue your service of keeping evil at bay, \_\_\_\_\_.'
_You get 1-2 AAs and the Special AA "Sanctity of the Keepers" (increases spell resist caps)._

---

**Finished**
You say, 'Hail, Chieftain Relae Aderi'
Chieftain Relae Aderi says 'Well the pieces have begun to fall into place. With all that you accomplished in bringing us artifacts and information, and the death of Yar`Lir, we have learned something disturbing. The curse that was laid in the Nest was designed to come alive when the magic of the Nest was weakened. It would seem someone knew that, one way or another, Norrathians would kill Yar`Lir and [break] the veil that protected the Nest underneath her.'
You have received a character flag!
You say, 'What about the break?'
Chieftain Relae Aderi says 'As you know, Yar`Lir was Vishimtar's mate, another dragon of storms. It was not just Yar`Lir who held the veil of magic and storms over the Nest -- Vishimtar worked with her. When Yar`Lir was killed and the magic veil interrupted, that's when it happened. The [dormant curse] in the Nest was brought to life and it crept through the caverns, destroying eggs and creatures in its path, including Vishimtar. While he was not killed, his body and magic became corrupted and he was made more powerful.'
You say, 'What about the dormant curse?'
Chieftain Relae Aderi says 'What plagues us now is the origin of the curse. We are not sure who is responsible, but we are devoted to finding out. You can help us no further now, but I would say you'd done enough, wouldn't you? You have certainly proved yourself and can now consider yourself one of Norrath's Keepers.'

- Spell: Claw of Vox [item=46860]

!**Faction with the Dark Reign**
_The Dark Reign camp (/loc 3075, -320) can be found in the Lavastorm Mountains tunnels leading to The Broodlands._
_There will be faction requirements as you move along in progression:_
\- Tier 1 requires "indifferent"+
\- Tier 2 requires "amiable"+
\- Tier 3 requires "amiable"+ (300)
\- Tier 4 requires "kindly"+
\- Tier 5 requires "warmly"+ (770)
_There are several ways to increase faction:_
\- Kill Norrath's Keepers guards (+1 per kill).
\- Wayfarer Mercenary Varein [npc=17813]'s solo tasks (available at apprehensive **or lower**):
\-\-\- Blood Money [quest=4873] (+20)
\-\-\- Spider's Webs [quest=4872] (+20)
\- Wayfarer Mercenary Jeryx [npc=17812]'s solo tasks (available at apprehensive **or lower**):
\-\-\- Meat for Stew [quest=3216] (+20)
\-\-\- Taunting the Brood [quest=3097] (+20)
\- Dark Reign Tokens [item=34990] are rewarded from all group missions and raids (LORE item, so turn it in each time you get one):
\-\-\- Turned in [npc=17808] to Wayfarer Mercenary Limann [npc=17808] (at apprehensive or lower) or Xeib Darkskies [npc=17792] (in the Dark Reign camp at indifferent+) (+20 faction each)
\- There are 37 non-progression group missions (the first eight are available at apprehensive):
\-\-\- Talontar [npc=17793] (Lavaspinner's Lair & Tirranun's Delve):
\-\-\-\-\-\- Grounding the Drakes (apprehensive)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Lavaspinner's Locals (apprehensive)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Diving for Lavarocks (indifferent)
\-\-\-\-\-\- The Drake Menace (indifferent)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Infested (amiable)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Lavaspinner Hunting (amiable)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Storming the Goblin Temple (kindly)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Forbin's Elixir (kindly)
\-\-\-\-\-\- A Goblin's Escort (warmly)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Halfling's Treasure Map (warmly)
\-\-\- Daleynn Spiritshadow [npc=17788] (Thundercrest Isles):
\-\-\-\-\-\- Simple Task (apprehensive)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Throes of Contagion (apprehensive)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Scions of Thundercrest (indifferent)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Splitting the Storm (indifferent)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Holy Hour (amiable)
\-\-\-\-\-\- The Creator (amiable)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Behind Closed Doors (kindly)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Lair Unguarded (kindly)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Plunder the Hoard (warmly)
\-\-\- Celrak Blightblood [npc=17791] (Stillmoon Temple & The Ascent):
\-\-\-\-\-\- Animated Statue Plans (apprehensive)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Tracking the Kirin (apprehensive)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Death Comes Swiftly (indifferent)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Diseased Pumas (indifferent)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Best Laid Plans (amiable)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Storm Dragon Scales (amiable)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Scales of Justice (kindly)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Tea for Thy Master (kindly)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Signal Fires (warmly)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Sudden Tremors (warmly)
\-\-\- Pericolo L`Morte [npc=17789] (Accursed Nest):
\-\-\-\-\-\- Lair of the Blackwing (apprehensive)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Scrap Metal (apprehensive)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Spider's Eye (indifferent)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Dragon's Egg (indifferent)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Lost Comrades (amiable)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Web of Lies (amiable)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Clues (kindly)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Rivals (kindly)
\- There are two non-progression mini-raids:
\-\-\- Pericolo L`Morte [npc=17789]:
\-\-\-\-\-\- Circle of Drakes (amiable+)
\-\-\-\-\-\- The Rampaging Monolith (amiable+)
\- There are three non-progression full-size raids:
\-\-\- General Lereh Dirr [npc=17790]:
\-\-\-\-\-\- Fanning the Flames (amiable+)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Reflections of Silver (high amiable+)
\-\-\-\-\-\- Kessdona's Perch (high amiable+)
\- You'll also gain faction regularly as you go through the progression itself.

---

**Tier 1: Gift of the Dark Reign**
_Upon reaching indifferent faction (and if Level 65+), you can begin progression. Speak with Commander Zaerr Ty`Dar:_
You say, 'Hail, Commander Zaerr Ty`Dar'
Commander Zaerr Ty`Dar sizes you up and snickers. 'How pathetic. What could you possibly do for me? You stand in the heart of the outpost of the might Dark Reign -- soldiers of Lanys T`Vyl. With her on our side, why would we need you? I assume you think you can [help].
You say, 'Of course I can help'
Commander Zaerr Ty`Dar says 'Hm? I could at the very least send you on a quest that would -- with almost certainty -- see the end of you. Very well, then. Go talk to Sirrikis. He has some menial tasks suitable for a whelp like you. We need ingredients to create burning arrows, help collecting basilisk blood, and assistance in clearing a path for our equipment in the Stillmoon Temple. When you satisfy these needs, then mayhap I will entertain your homely face again.'
You have received a character flag!
_Next, hail Officer Sirrikis Ryktor to get three solo tasks:_
\- The Burning Poison
\- Blood of the Basilisk
\- Clearing the Path
_After completing these tasks, speak with Commander Zaerr Ty`Dar again:_
You say, 'Hail, Commander Zaerr Ty`Dar'
Commander Zaerr Ty`Dar says 'Bah! I expected someone to report that you were deceased, \_\_\_\_\_. That not the case, obviously, perhaps I can give you a more challenging task. I have entrusted Vacax Rol`Tas with some more significant assignments. Go speak to him about the goblin's note in Tirranun's Delve and should he be satisfied with your work, perhaps I may talk again.'
You have received a character flag!
_Now, speak with Officer Vacax Rol`Tas for a mission:_
\- Have Note Will Travel
_With that mission completed, return to the Dark Reign camp, and speak with Commander Zaerr Ty`Dar again:_
Commander Zaerr Ty`Dar says 'If it will entice you to keep risking your lowly life for us, I have a rather rare reward for you -- a gift of magic from Lanys T`Vyl, the Child of Hate. It just might improve the more you help us. Do know that if you EVER turn your back on the Dark Reign and favor the Keepers, you will lose this gift in an instant.'
You have received a character flag!
You have gained the ability "Gift of the Dark Reign" at a cost of 0 ability points.
_The new ability "Gift of the Dark Reign" gives a maximum stats increase of 10 per stat._

---

**Tier 2: Tenacity of the Dark Reign**
_You must have amiable faction standing or better to request Tier 2 tasks. You can raise faction by doing the miscellaneous missions offered by the NPCs in the camp (each mission results in a Token which can be turned in for +20 faction). First, speak with Commander Zaerr Ty`Dar again for a flag (this checks you for amiable+ faction):_
You say, 'Hail, Commander Zaerr Ty`Dar'
_If below amiable, you see:_
Commander Zaerr Ty`Dar says 'What in the...? While a measure of arrogance can be tolerated, your stupidity cannot. You will go no further with me until you do more work for the Dark Reign. Pay your dues, fool!'
_If amiable or higher, you see:_
You say, 'Hail, Commander Zaerr Ty`Dar'
Commander Zaerr Ty`Dar looks you up and down with distaste. 'You have indeed surprised us all. Not just a few eves ago your name came up while we took a meal. Oh, how we laughed. But, as you are here, perhaps you have earned some insight into the great army of the Dark Reign. Our purpose -- nay, our destiny -- is to carry on the [work] of our most-feared and revered leader, Lanys T`Vyl.
You say, 'What work?'
Commander Zaerr Ty`Dar says 'We shall be victorious in robbing the lands of those ignorant and frail races of light and good. Should you stay the path with us, perhaps you will be on the favored side of darkness as we rule Norrath! In order to even be considered for such an honor, you must continue to provide for us. I believe Sirrikis is ready to trust you with [endeavors] that are somewhat more sensitive. And you will notice that I have told Lereh Dirr that if you are foolish enough, you may attempt to slay the great lava dragon known as [Tirranun].'
You have received a character flag!
_(This flag grants you access to the non-progression raid "Fanning the Flames".)_
You say, 'What endeavors?'
Commander Zaerr Ty`Dar says 'Indeed, you must aid us with gathering muddy blood from sand goblins, creating an army of stone, and gathering kirin brains. See Officer Sirrikis for these tasks. Should you complete all of this work, which I highly doubt, I may grant you even more challenging assignments. Now, start marching. Your stench is annoying me.'
You say, 'What about Tirranun?'
Commander Zaerr Ty`Dar says 'I don't believe you are ready, but stranger things have happened -- not withstanding your success so far. Should you survive a meeting with this dragon of lava, we have much to learn. His death will likely grant us enough knowledge to find a way to access the other dragons that inhabit this newly discovered land. Should we learn enough about these dragons, we may be able to exploit their kind and their knowledge for our army and serve Lanys' will in Norrath -- to see the end of the good.'
_Now, speak with Officer Sirrikus Ryktor and receive three tasks:_
\- Army of Stone
\- Blood from Sand
\- Reap the Kirin Mind
_Once these tasks are done, head back to the Commander and speak with him to receive a flag:_
You say, 'Hail, Commander Zaerr Ty`Dar'
Commander Zaerr Ty`Dar says 'You continue to overcome the challenges that some of the grunts of the Dark Reign seem unable to. I suppose I shall continue to push the limits of your abilities to see at what point you end in a bloody mess. I've asked Vacax to assign you with stealing drake eggs for the Dark Reign. The potion we can make with them shall be deadly, indeed. As I don't appear to be able to get rid of you, I shall direct you to Aleeth Zyrv. The mission to investigate Emoush that he has for you is quite a bit more delicate and will require you to bring along those with skill that can be trusted. Return to me with proof when you have completed these assignments.'
You have received a character flag!
_Next, speak to Officer Vacax Rol`Tas, who gives you another mission:_
\- Drake Eggs
_With this mission complete, speak with Captain Aleeth Zyrv for your first progression raid event:_
\- Calling Emoush (6-24 players)
_Loot the "Glowing Stone Fragment" from Emoush the Destroyer, and bring it to Commander Zaerr Ty`Dar:_
Commander Zaerr Ty`Dar says 'Interesting that you continue to put yourself in harms way for the Dark Reign and survive. Lanys has authorized me to improve your abilities somewhat so you can continue to risk your hide for us. Talk to me again and if the Dark Reign considers you worthy, I will have more work.'
You have received a character flag!
_You get 1-2 AAs and the Special AA "Tenacity of the Dark Reign" (adds 250 hp/mana/end to your base stats). You also receive your 69th level spell/tome._
Bard - Spell: Storm Blade
Beastlord - Spell: Growl of the Panther
Berserker - Tome of Destroyer's Volley
Cleric - Spell: Blessed Touch (scribes as "Ward of Retribution")
Druid - Spell: Serpent Vines
Enchanter - Spell: Rune of Rikkukin
Magician - Spell: Summon: Molten Orb
Monk - Tome of Dragon Fang
Necromancer - Spell: Shadow Orb
Paladin - Tome of Guard of Righteousness
Ranger - Spell: Earthen Shackles
Rogue - Tome of Assassin's Feint
Shadowknight - Tome of Soul Shield
Shaman - Spell: Spirit of the Panther
Warrior - Tome of Cyclone Attack
Wizard - Spell: Claw of Vox

---

**Tier 3: Embrace of the Dark Reign**
_In order to continue on after this step, you must have high amiable faction (+400 faction). Hail Commander Zaerr Ty`Dar for another flag:_
You say, 'Hail, Commander Zaerr Ty`Dar'
Commander Zaerr Ty`Dar snarls as you approach. 'Still nosing around, are you? While it's somewhat appropriate you're willing to be fodder for the Dark Reign. Your desire to please is almost embarrassing -- unless you are smarter than that and have [greater motives]. I highly doubt that, however.
You have received a character flag!
_(This flag grants you access to the non-progression raids "Reflections of Silver" & "Kessdona's Perch".)_
You say, 'I have greater motives...'
Commander Zaerr Ty`Dar says 'Is that so? If your desire is to serve Lanys T`Vyl and this ascending army, then I can respect that to some degree. Unless you are simply willing to give us your body for our undead contingent, you have many [quests] before you get there. Should you succeed, then perhaps I won't want to sever your head from that neck of yours each time I see you. Once again, Sirrikis has the details for you. As promised, I have given Lereh Dirr instructions to lead you to the greater [wyrms] we have uncovered the hiding place of, though our interest in them right now isn't critical.'
You say, 'What quests?'
Commander Zaerr Ty`Dar says 'You must contaminate the waters of Stillmoon Temple, gather knowledge from the scrolls of the temple, and thwart the attack of the Snowfoot goblins. When you are successful, return to me and I will direct you to my officers for more challenging work.'
You say, 'What wyrms?'
Commander Zaerr Ty`Dar says 'The dragons of the Temple and Ascent -- Rikkukin and Kessdona -- are mates of a type of dragon we've not seen. Scales of metal have been recovered from the temple and they hold a magic that might play a vital part for us. They are dragons of great wisdom and knowledge and besting them would be a feat that few are prepared for. But as few are foolish enough to take on the task, I thought you might be. You will find that Sirrikis can open the way to these dragons for you.'
_Speak to Officer Sirrikis Ryktor to be assigned three solo tasks:_
\- Muddy the Waters
\- Snowfoot Attack
\- Knowledge is Power
_After completing these tasks, speaking with the Commander again:_
You say, 'Hail, Commander Zaerr Ty`Dar'
Commander Zaerr Ty`Dar sighs. 'Why must you look at me so expectantly? There is a long way to go before I would offer any respect. And if you're looking for thanks, your foolish gaze is in the wrong direction. Now, go speak to Vacax again and get details on a mission that might be very satisfying for you. I'd do it myself if I could -- you must attempt to collect a scroll of great power. Also, there is an interesting test of perseverance you must overcome in the temple. You'll need to speak to Aleeth Zyrv for particulars. And also speak to Aleeth who is pursuing Volkara in Tirranun's Delve.
You have received a character flag!
_Next, speak with Officer Vacax Rol`Tas to be assigned a group mission:_
\- The Gilded Scroll
_You'll also need to complete two progression raids from Captain Aleeth Zyrv:_
\- Volkara's Bite (6-42 players)
\- Trial of Perseverance (6-24 players)
_Loot "Goblin Warlord's Beads" from the warlord in the "Trial of Perseverance", and loot "Lava Spider Spinners" from Volkara in "Volkara's Bite". Hand in these two items to Commander Zaerr Ty`Dar:_
Commander Zaerr Ty`Dar says 'Such a wonder that you're forever returning here like a rat looking for scraps. Though your reek is foul, your work has earned you more power from Lanys' great magic. There will be more work for you if you've adequately proven yourself.'
You have received a character flag!
Commander Zaerr Ty`Dar says 'And so Norrath's Keepers loses to our greatness once again. You've given me and Lanys plenty to mull over and snicker about. Your service to the Dark Reign has been painful, but satisfying to me. Continue to serve and we will reward you well.'
You have received a character flag!
_You get 1-2 AAs and the Special AA "Embrace of the Dark Reign" (increases buff slot limit by one)._

---

**Tier 4: Power of the Dark Reign**
_In order to continue on in Tier 4, you must, again, have kindly faction. Hail Commander Zaerr Ty`Dar for another flag:_
You say, 'Hail, Commander Zaerr Ty`Dar'
Commander Zaerr Ty`Dar says 'The path through the temples and mountains is fraught with danger. We have lost many who have gone on expeditions thus far. As you seem willing -- and pestering -- it's now your turn. Go speak to Aleeth. These assignments are not for the faint of heart and you hold the reputation of the Dark Reign in your hands -- something I will not tolerate being sullied. In the temple, you will need many allies to overcome Shogurei, the Guardian of the Sands. With a smaller force, you may be able to accept the challenge issued by the goblins of Thundercrest Isle. The audacity of challenging us, the army of the Child of Hate, is beyond me, but go!'
You have received a character flag!
_Now, you'll need to complete two progression raids from Captain Aleeth Zyrv:_
\- Guardian of the Sands (6-42 players)
\- Goblin Dojo (6-24 players)
_Loot "Quintessence of Sand" and "Meditation Stone" from those two raids (respectively), and give them to Commander Zaerr Ty`Dar:_
Commander Zaerr Ty`Dar says 'What of it? If you expect a dark elf of my stature to kowtow to you, your mind is seething with delusions. I suppose someone may ask me to reward you should you continue to prove yourself.'
You have received a character flag!
Commander Zaerr Ty`Dar says 'Once again you exceeded my expectations, but I still find your manner deplorable. There is something about you that leads me to think you believe you are better than the rest of us. I suppose we shall see, shan't we? And you will likely have your chance to [prove] it. Shogurei's essence did reveal some secrets. He was gifted with the power of wind and storm, a magic bestowed upon it by the dragon of Thundercrest Isles. Now we know the magic the dragon holds, but we must send you to set eyes upon her.'
You say, 'How should I prove it?'
Commander Zaerr Ty`Dar says 'Lanys T`Vyl and the rest of the Dark Reign grow more powerful by the day and our work becomes ever more important to continue this glorious trend. Lereh has some difficult employment for you, most notably a confrontation with the dragon of [storms].'
You have received a character flag!
You say, 'What dragon of storms?'
Commander Zaerr Ty`Dar says 'Yar`Lir is perhaps the most powerful dragon known to our kind. Your work thus far has indicated to us, especially through the scrolls gathered from the temple, that Yar`Lir is the dragon responsible for casting a veil of magic and storms over the Nest to protect it from view and keep it hospitable for the wyrms. We want you to [kill] her. Lereh knows the path to Yar`Lir.'
You say, 'Why should I kill her?'
Commander Zaerr Ty`Dar says 'You must kill her to disrupt her magic and weaken the area. Soon, we shall rule the dragons and Norrath, and this is but a step on that path. This is one dragon that you must defeat before you are allowed to learn more of our knowledge of this place and to consider you a soldier of the Dark Reign. There is more than just her to defeat as well, which I will inform you of if you defeat the dragon of storms. You must return and give me one of her fangs.'
_Now, speak with General Lereh Dirr to request another progression raid:_
\- An End to the Storms (6-54 players)
_Loot "Yar'lir's Fang" from this raid, and hand it to Commander Zaerr Ty'Dar:_
Commander Zaerr Ty`Dar says 'Some might show you gratitude for your achievements, but I shan't. Lanys Y`Vyl has once again allowed you a greater touch of the magic of Hate. If I deem you worthy, I may have more work for you.'
You have received a character flag!
_You get 1-2 AAs + the Special AA "Power of the Dark Reign" (increases critical melee/magical attacks and heals)._
_If this is a server-first, completion of Tier 4 opens up The Accursed Nest (previously locked to everyone) on your server._

---

**Tier 5: Fervor of the Dark Reign**
_In order to continue on in Tier 5, you must have at least high warmly faction (770). Speak with Commander Zaerr Ty`Dar:_
You say, 'Hail, Commander Zaerr Ty`Dar'
Commander Zaerr Ty`Dar says 'I suppose, as much as it makes my stomach turn, I can say you've earned my respect. It is no small feat to kill a dragon, and one of Yar`Lir's caliber. Not only that, but the death of Yar`Lir has caused a significant upheaval in the Nest and we're unclear as to why. Figuring that out is now your job. There is some evil at work there and we need to know what it is... so perhaps we can work with it. Officer Vacax has a job for you -- to ambush a group of meddlers who are attempting to obtain information ahead of us. You will need to speak with Aleeth about a creature named Ju`rek and bring me evidence of your success.'
You have received a character flag!
_Next, speak with Officer Vacax Rol`Tas for a group mission:_
\- Rival Party
_You'll also need to complete another progression raid from Captain Aleeth Zyrv:_
\- The Curse of Ju'rek (6-24 players)
_Loot "Diseased Wing Fragment" from this raid, and hand it to Commander Zaerr Ty`Dar:_
Commander Zaerr Ty`Dar says 'From the first days when you were collecting ingredients for us, I would have never have expected you to come so far. While you do not rival my power, you have come as close as any -- and without so annoying me that I ran you through with my blade. The moment you killed the [dragon] of the storms, the Nest below was indeed weakened and the grim dragon of the Nest was awakened and angered.'
You say, 'What dragon?'
Commander Zaerr Ty`Dar says 'Our rogues caught a glimpse of that dragon in the Nest and it appeared to be a dragon like Yar`Lir, but is no longer. It has been affected somehow, twisted and embroiled by shadow. You must go find it and kill it, at all costs. There is so much to learn and one last trophy to claim for Lanys T`Vyl, the child of Hate. Bring me Vishimtar's shadowy scale.'
_Finally, speak with General Lereh Dirr for the last raid event:_
\- In the Shadows (6-54 players)
_Loot "Shadowscale of Vishimtar" from this raid, and hand it in to Commander Zaerr Ty`Dar to finish off your progression:_
Commander Zaerr Ty`Dar says 'It has been a long journey, \_\_\_\_\_. For the last time, the Child of Hate offers you the greatest power of Hate that a mere mortal can tolerate. You have served the Dark Reign well.'
_You get 1-2 AAs and the Special AA "Fervor of the Dark Reign" (increases spell resist caps)._

---

**Finished**
You say, 'Hail, Commander Zaerr Ty`Dar'
Commander Zaerr Ty`Dar says 'You have done what most could not. From what we can piece together from the scrolls, the minds of the kirins obtained for us, and the death of Yar`Lir, there has been another that has brought a remarkable [darkness] over the Nest. As much as the Dark Reign would enjoy the credit, we cannot claim this great disruption.'
You have received a character flag!
You say, 'What darkness?'
Commander Zaerr Ty`Dar says 'Yar`Lir was Vishimtar's mate, another dragon of storms. Together, the two of them used their magic to veil the Nest. When Yar`Lir was killed and the magic veil interrupted, an incredible and delightful event took place. A [dormant curse] in the Nest was brought to life that destroyed the eggs of the Nest and turned many of the creatures within it, including Vishimtar. He was not killed by the curse, but corrupted and made more powerful than any dragon we have ever seen!'
You say, 'What about the dormant curse?'
Commander Zaerr Ty`Dar says 'What remains a mystery is the source of the curse. Someone or something was able to get into the Nest before any of us were able to... somehow. We will not rest until we learn the origins of that curse, for the magic that created it is certainly one we ought to have on our side, wouldn't you say? For now, your work with us is complete and we have all agreed to reward you well for all you've done in the name of Lanys T`Vyl and the outpost. You may now safely and indisputably call yourself one of the Dark Reign.'

---

---

**For an overview of the Dragons of Norrath expansion, see this wiki article.**
**For an overview of progression, see quest entries for Norrath's Keepers [quest=3061] and the Dark Reign [quest=3062].**

---

_This raid task begins with Captain Areha Burina ( **click for map image**) in the Norrath's Keepers camp of the Lavastorm Mountains._
_Pre-requisites for requesting this task:_
\- Minimum 6 players; maximum 54 players
\- Must be warmly faction with Norrath's Keepers
\- Completion of all Tier 4 tasks and flags (including the raids)
\- Completion of "Origins of the Curse" (group mission)
\- Completion of "The Curse of Ju`rek" (raid)
You say, 'Hail, Captain Areha Burina'
Captain Areha Burina bows. 'I see a good heart in you and a measure of bravery. Serve Firiona Vie and Norrath's Keepers well!'
You have been assigned the task 'In the Shadows'.
Terrible events came to pass as Yar`Lir was taken from her perch. There was a dragon that protected the Nest beneath the islands in the sky, but now that place has turned to ruin and darkness. After seeing the effects the corruption has caused the Nest, we can only guess what has happened to the dragon that inhabited it. Go now and be swift. If this dragon is a plague on our lands, it must be stopped.

---

**Task Steps**
Touch the portal to The Nest 0/1 (The Broodlands)
Confront Vishimtar the Fallen 0/1 (The Accursed Nest)
**TASK LOCKS HERE**
Kill Vishimtar the Fallen 0/1 (The Accursed Nest)

---

**The Event**
_In this raid instance, you will find a formidable foe: Vishimtar the Fallen, who comes with numerous adds and tricks. Vishimtar hits for a max ~4,500; AE rampages (mitigated to ~1,000 per hit); and casts "Vishimtar's Shadow", "Vishimtar's Aura", and "Withering Corruption" throughout the event:_
Vishimtar the Fallen says 'The nest, the children... must be protected.'
Vishimtar's Shadow: PB AE 2000', Chromatic (-500)
1: Decrease HP when cast by 1250
2: Decrease Hitpoints by 850 per tick
4: Decrease Movement by 5%
11: Decrease Mana by 5 per tick
Vishimtar's Aura: Self 0', Magic (0)
1: Increase Damage Shield by 15
2: Increase Spell-Damage Shield by 250
Withering Corruption: Single Target, Magic (-1000)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 100 per tick
2: Cast Upon Fade: Vishimtar's Corruption
Vishimtar's Corruption: Self 0', Magic (-1000)
1: Decrease AC by 600
2: Max HP change by (-75%)
3: Decrease Agro Multiplier by 95%
_At about 75% health, 4x "incorporeal shadow" spawn. These don't hit hard at all and have relatively few hitpoints. If killed, a "lifedrain shadow" spawns in its place. The lifedrain shadows hit for a max ~2,000 and flurry, so you want to avoid killing the incorporeal shadows if you can._
_3-6 minutes after Vishimtar is initially aggro'd and every 75-120 seconds thereafter, a "tainted egg" spawns at a random location. If these aren't killed within a few moments of spawning (blunt-weapons only), multiple mobs called "a corrupted drakeling" spawn from it and attack. These hit for a max ~1,200 and flurry._
_At 50% health, the four "corrupted drake" mobs around the room go aggro. These are snareable and, if killed, immediately respawn._
_At 25% health, Vishimtar locks health and starts casting "Creeping Doom" on random targets (45-second intervals, maximum four targets at a time). This spell must be cured before it fades (48 seconds), otherwise the inflicted player is death touched. To cure this, go to one of the "mournful spirit" dervishes that roam around the perimeter of the event area and say "shoulder my burden" to it:_
You say, 'Hail, mournful spirit'
An otherworldly voice rings in your mind, 'I can sense that you have been afflicted by Vishimtar, \_\_\_\_\_. You will not survive long - soon death will take you and corrupt your body into a twisted shell of hatred. Allow me to help you. I can [shoulder your burden].'
You say, 'Shoulder my burden'
The mournful spirit draws corruption from your body and absorbs it, destroying itself in the process.
mournful spirit died.
_For each cure of "Creeping Doom", Vishimtar's health unlocks 1%._
Creeping Doom: Single Target, Unresistable (-1000)
1: Decrease Stats by 120
2: Cast Upon Fade: Creeping Doom Trigger
_If anyone dies or goes linkdead at any time in the event, "a spirit wraith" spawns in their place. These skeletons hit for a max ~1,500; have zonewide aggro; and are mezzable (note: pet deaths don't spawn adds)._
_Note: Throughout the event, you'll sometimes get a "cloud of concentrated chaos" spawn. You can kill these to fill up everyone's health and mana._

---

**Completion & Loot**
_Kill Vishimtar to complete the task:_
Vishimtar the Fallen has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
Vishimtar crumples. His corrupted form no longer casts shadows upon the cavern walls.
Your faction standing with Vishimtar has been adjusted by -1.
Your faction standing with Cursed Drakes has been adjusted by -1.
Vishimtar's feeble attempts to protect the Nest were no match for your prowess.
_Following Vishimtar's death, all adds despawn and a chest spawns with loot. Vishimtar himself drops 9x "Shadowscale of Vishimtar" (Tier 5 progression item) + 1x "Eye of Vishimtar" (turn-in item for **a mask** [quest=3090])._
_The chest contains 3 items from this loot table:_
Accursed Earhoop of Pain
Belt of the Black Dragon
Cloak of Deepshadow
Cord of the Cunning
Dark Heart of Vishimtar
Dragonkiller
Face of Unfeeling
Mottled Black Dragonscale
Obsidian Pauldrons
Rod of Shaded Wrath
Skull of Vishimtar
_Task Rewards:_
9x "Radiant Crystal"
1x "Norrath's Keepers Token" (faction **turn-in** [quest=3134] item)
_Note: If doing this raid as part of progression, you'll want to check in with some NPCs to make sure your flags are up to date. See **this quest entry** [quest=3061] for more details._
**Submitted by:** Saraban of Darkwind

- Belt of the Black Dragon [item=34903]
- Cloak of Deepshadow [item=34901]
- Dark Heart of Vishimtar [item=34905]
- Dragonkiller [item=35002]
- Face of Unfeeling [item=35004]
- Mottled Black Dragonscale [item=35003]
- Norrath's Keepers Token [item=34991]
- Obsidian Pauldrons [item=35206]

- Rod of Shaded Wrath [item=34902]

# Norrath's Keepers Tier 3b: Trial of Perseverance

**Level:** 70 | **Maximum Level:** 125 | **Quest Type:** Task | **Repeatable:** Yes | **Monster Mission:** No


## Quest Text / Walkthrough

**Level:** 70
**Maximum Level:** 125
**Monster Mission:** No
**Repeatable:** Yes
**Can Be Shrouded?:** No
**Quest Type:** Task
**Time Limit:** 06:00:00
**Group Size:** Raid
**Min. # of Players:** 6
**Max. # of Players:** 24

For an overview of the Dragons of Norrath expansion, see this wiki article.
For an overview of progression, see quest entries for Norrath's Keepers [quest=3061] and the Dark Reign [quest=3062].
This raid task begins with Lieutenant Ekiltu Verlor (click for map image) in the Norrath's Keepers camp of the Lavastorm Mountains. It has a 2-day, 20-hour lockout timer.
Pre-requisites for requesting this task:
- Level 69+
- Minimum 6 players; maximum 24 players
- Must be high amiable faction with Norrath's Keepers
- Completion of all Tier 2 tasks and flags
- Completion of "Protect the Waters"
- Completion of "The Sacred Scrolls"
- Completion of "Snowfoot Revenge"
You say, 'Hail, Lieutenant Ekiltu Verlor'
Lieutenant Ekiltu Verlor says 'Those faithful to the way of Norrath's Keepers may take on the greatest challenges and prove their worth to Firiona Vie.'
You have been assigned the task 'Trial of Perseverance'.
There are still more parchments and tomes we wish to learn about in Stillmoon Temple. Some of these documents may provide the Dark Reign with the ability to bring ruin to the world. You must use your strength, stamina, and wherewithal to uncover these scrolls and secure them before Dark Reign can get their hands on them.
Task Steps
Touch the portal to Stillmoon Temple 0/1 (The Broodlands)
Approach Osashi 0/1 (Stillmoon Temple)
TASK LOCKS HERE
Kill the goblin warlord 0/1 (Stillmoon Temple)
The Event
The entrance to the instance is a portal in the north-central part of the Broodlands (will be marked on your compass).
In the instanced zone, find Osashi close to the zone-in area and speak with him:
You say, 'Hail, Osashi'
Osashi says 'You have come at the goblins' behest?  Very well.  Perseverance is important to all, regardless of what you seek, be it wealth, knowledge, or power.  This trial will show me just how committed you are to obtain your desires.  When you being this trial, several goblins will wait near the gong and attempt to ring it after one minute.  Do not let this happen and you shall prove yourself in this trial.  Are you [ready] to begin?'
You say, 'We're ready!'
a Stillmoon goblin says 'Someone ring the bell!  Summon help!'
Osashi immediately despawns and 4x "a Stillmoon goblin" appear around the bell next to him.
All goblins in this event must be kept aggro'd. If any goblin is left idle for any longer than 10-15 seconds, it "rings the bell". If you allow this to happen, then you get "an Ancient Stillmoon Guardian" instead of the goblin warlord. This represents a failure, and you'll have to repeat the event.
When you kill "a Stillmoon goblin", another one spawns in its place. These mobs are NOT auto-aggro, and due to line-of-sight, you'll have to keep watch to make sure none are left idle. These goblins hit for a max ~1,100 and are immune to mez and stun.
After a certain number of goblin kills, "a Stillmoon warlord" appears. This mob hits for a max ~1,600; AE rampages; and casts "Gelid Breath":
Gelid Breath: Targeted AE 20', Cold (-275) (2 minutes)
1: Decrease HP when cast by 1250
2: Decrease Chance to Hit by 10% with 1H Blunt
3: Decrease Effective Casting Level by 12
4: Increase Curse Counter by 50
Completion & Loot
Kill the warlord to complete the task:
a Stillmoon warlord has been slain by _____!
a Stillmoon warlord's corpse mutters an ancient curse as you end his life.
Beyond the wildest dreams and plans of the creatures that call Stillmoon their home, you have defeated the Trial of Perseverance and bested them all!
It drops 6x "Goblin Warlord's Beads" (progression item) + 1 item from this loot table:
Bracer of the Warriors
Hoop of the Drakeborn
Icefloe Hammer
Stillmoon Silk Bracer
Task Rewards:
13 Radiant Crystals
1x "Norrath's Keepers Token" (faction turn-in [quest=3134] item)
Note: If doing this raid as part of progression, you'll want to check in with some NPCs to make sure your flags are up to date. See this quest entry [quest=3061] for more details.Submitted by: Saraban of DarkwindRewards:
- Bracer of the Warriors [item=35031]
- Goblin Warlord's Beads [item=35032]
- Hoop of the Drakeborn [item=35807]
- Icefloe Hammer [item=34978]
