**For an overview of the Dragons of Norrath expansion, see this wiki article.**
**For an overview of progression, see quest entries for Norrath's Keepers [quest=3061] and the Dark Reign [quest=3062].**

---

_This group mission begins with Keeper Dilar Nelune ( **click for map image**) in the Norrath's Keepers camp of the Lavastorm Mountains._
_Pre-requisites for requesting this task:_
\- Minimum group average level 68+
\- Minimum "amiable" faction with Norrath's Keepers
\- Completion of "Turn the Tides of Sand"
\- Completion of "Recover the Lost Map"
\- Completion of "Defend the Kirin Contact"
You say, 'Hail, Keeper Dilar Nelune'
Keeper Dilar Nelune says 'Should we all perform to Chieftain Aderi's expectations, the races of good and light will bring an end to the threat of evil upon Norrath.'
You have been assigned the task 'Sickness of the Spirit.'
You may recall the kirin ally that the chieftain mentioned and that you protected. Well, this same kirin has shied away from us since it felt the anger of its kin. Many have tried and failed to win this kirin's heart to our side once again. Now, we ask that you make an offer of peace as you are the one that protected it. If we can secure this, we might be able to gain an upper hand against the Dark Reign. Within the courtyard of Stillmoon Temple is the keeper of the cherry blossom forest. He will summon the kirin for you if you help him in his quest to find the perfect cherry blossom. You may have luck in finding this blossom upon certain goblins tending the cherry blossom grove.

---

!
_Map points for "stillmoona.txt" map files:_
P 218.0000, -950.0000, 41.0000, 240, 0, 0, 1, Librarian
P 700.0000, -1262.0000, 0.0000, 240, 0, 0, 1, Scroll\_Thief
P -55.0000, -191.0000, -33.0000, 240, 0, 0, 1, Stillmoon\_Courtyard\_Gardener\_(roam)
P -381.0000, -194.0000, -33.0000, 240, 0, 0, 1, Stillmoon\_Forest\_Keeper
P -1000.0000, -450.0000, 0.0000, 240, 0, 0, 1, Wong\_Li
P -700.0000, -630.0000, 0.0000, 240, 0, 0, 1, Nethran\_trigger

---

Touch the portal to Stillmoon Temple 0/1 (The Broodlands)
_Your compass will direct you to the entrance._

---

Loot 1 Perfect Cherry Blossom from Stillmoon courtyard gardener 0/1 (Stillmoon Temple)
_"Stillmoon courtyard gardener" is a goblin that wanders around the zone-in courtyard. It hits for a max ~750; has ~35,000 hitpoints; and is mezzable/stunnable. Kill it, and loot a "Perfect Cherry Blossom"._

---

Deliver 1 Perfect Cherry Blossom to Stillmoon Forest Keeper 0/1 (Stillmoon Temple)
_"Stillmoon Forest Keeper" is a drake found in the west end of the courtyard._
Stillmoon Forest Keeper says 'Ah! The perfect blossom . . . I can hardly believe my eyes! For years have I searched in vain for those perfect petals only to find out that gardener was keeping it from me all this time. I humbly thank you for your offer, kind adventurers and perhaps I can return the favor for I know who it is you seek. With this in hand I can summon the hidden one for you. Now go and greet him before he hides again!'

---

Speak with the Kirin Grandmaster 0/1 (Stillmoon Temple)
_The Kirin Grandmaster is a mob named "Wong Li", who is in the small garden in the far west end of the zone. It is initially non-KOS._
You say, 'Hail, Wong Li'
Wong Li says 'Congratulations for finding me adventurer. I simply could not resist meeting the one who had found the perfect Cherry Blossom. I know your side wishes to unlock the secrets that lay within these temple walls but you must earn my trust before I will part with its treasures. Lately a sickness of the spirit has infected some of my brethren. Those few of us able to resist this illness are too weak to seek the cause of this malady and thus we need you to help us in this endeavor. We think that the new goblin tending to the bath house, Nethran may have something to do with this. Find him and see if he has any clues on his person. Alas, I can not tell you exactly how to bring him out of hiding except to say that he is easily angered. Perhaps you can use this to lure him out. . .'

---

Loot 1 Ancient Note from Nethran 0/1 (Stillmoon Temple)
_Kill mobs in the bath house area until "Nethran" spawns alongside 2x "goblin laborer." They will approach from opposite directions, and do NOT see invisibility. (It only takes 2-3 mob kills to spawn them.)_
Nethran says 'Out! Out! OUT! You be makin' a fine mess of me baths! Get outta here before I make a nice meal o' you!'
_One laborer is a cleric; the other a pure caster. They each hit for a max ~800; have ~85,000 hitpoints; and are mezzable and stunnable. Nethran hits for a max ~950 and is a wizard. Kill Nethran, and loot an Ancient Note from its corpse._
Nethran has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
Nethran walks three steps before collapsing upon himself in a small heap.
Your faction standing with Stillmoon Acolytes got worse.
Your faction standing with Kessdona got worse.
Your faction standing with Rikkukin got worse.

---

Deliver 1 Ancient Note to Kirin Grandmaster 0/1 (Stillmoon Temple)
_Bring the note to Wong Li._
Wong Li says 'Nethran had this on him, you say? This is very dire news indeed. This note speaks of an ancient and powerful curse that long ago descended upon this temple and its inhabitants. I can not translate all of the glyphs though but perhaps if you spoke with the temple librarian we might be able to uncover more about this plague. I will send word ahead so he will be expecting you.'

---

Speak with Temple Librarian 0/1 (Stillmoon Temple)
_The library is upstairs in the large east room (looks like a "+" sign on the map)._
You say, 'Hail, a Stillmoon librarian'
a Stillmoon librarian says 'Yes outsider, I have been foretold of your arrival. The scroll you seek, however, was stolen from here but mere moments before you arrived. Find the thieves and you will find the scroll you need!'

---

Loot 1 Fables of Winged Harmony from scroll thief 0/1 (Stillmoon Temple)
_Head downstairs and into the sand area. In the northeastern corner of the zone are two small rooms. In the southern of those two rooms you will find 1x "scroll thief" and 3x "scroll thief underling" (/loc 1100, -700). They can be split from one another, each hits for a max ~750 (some are rogues that can backstab for ~2,200) and is mezzable and stunnable. Kill one, and loot "Fables of Winged Harmony" from its corpse._

---

Deliver 1 Fables of Winged Harmony to Kirin Grandmaster 0/1 (Stillmoon Temple)
_Take this back to Wong Li, and be ready for a fight._
Wong Li says 'Very good adventurer, very good indeed! You have played your part in my plan most brilliantly but now I am forced to slay you, outsider. You see, I can not risk you upsetting my plans.'
_He respawns and attacks._
Wong Li says 'Your bones will litter these gardens today!'
Kill 1 Twisted Kirin Grandmaster 0/1 (Stillmoon Temple)
_The aggressive Wong Li is Level 75 and a druid. It hits for a max ~1,100 and has ~47,000 hitpoints. Kill it to complete your task._
The Kirin cries out, 'This cannot be! I was destined to rule the temple!'
Your faction standing with Stillmoon Acolytes got worse.
Your faction standing with Kessdona got worse.
Your faction standing with Rikkukin got worse.
_This completes the task:_
Surprisingly, the sly Kirin turned to betrayal. Though this journey has ended on a sour note, perhaps there are still others willing to listen to offers of peace.
_Rewards:_
48 Radiant Crystals
1x "Norrath's Keepers Token" (faction **turn-in** [quest=3134] item)
- Norrath's Keepers Token [item=34991]
- Radiant Crystal [item=34988]

_This group mission begins with Keeper Dilar Nelune ( **click for map image**) in the Norrath's Keepers camp of the Lavastorm Mountains._
_Pre-requisites for requesting this task:_
\- Average group level must be at least 68
\- Minimum "indifferent" faction with Norrath's Keepers

You say, 'Hail, Keeper Dilar Nelune'
Keeper Dilar Nelune says 'Should we all perform to Chieftain Aderi's expectations, the races of good and light will bring an end to the threat of evil upon Norrath.'
You have been assigned the task 'Children of Gimblax'.
Relae must have told you that Gimblax plans to extract vengeance upon you for killing the goblins in the temple. We have learned roughly where this foul goblin is and highly recommend you find your way to him before he finds you.

---

Touch the cave entrance to Tirranun's Delve 0/1 (The Broodlands)
_The direction to the instanced entrance will be marked on your compass._

---

Slay Gimblax 0/1 (Tirranun's Delve)
_Upon zoning into Tirranun's Delve, you find yourself in the far northern part of the zone. Gimblax is in the opposite end: Alone in a small cave at the far south. Make your way across the zone. Be careful as some mobs see invis. Charge Gimblax, and kill him. He hits for a max ~1,200; flurries; procs "Goblin Headbutt" (3-second stun); and has ~87,000 hitpoints._
Gimblax says 'You will die for your treatment of my children!'
Gimblax has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
Gimblax's corpse looks at you in disbelief before collapsing.
As news of their leader's death spreads, the remaining goblins of the delve rapidly fall into chaos and disarray. Gimblax's threat is no more. It will likely be some time before you will need to be concerned with them again.
_This completes the task._
_Rewards:_
Up to 25 Radiant Crystals (number varies according to group's level)
1x "Norrath's Keepers Token" (faction **turn-in** [quest=3134] item)
- Norrath's Keepers Token [item=34991]
- Radiant Crystal [item=34988]

---

**For an overview of the Dragons of Norrath expansion, see this wiki article.**
**For an overview of progression, see quest entries for Norrath's Keepers [quest=3061] and the Dark Reign [quest=3062].**

---

_This raid task begins with Lieutenant Ekiltu Verlor ( **click for map image**) in the Norrath's Keepers camp of the Lavastorm Mountains._
_Pre-requisites for requesting this task:_
\- Minimum 6 players; maximum 24 players
\- Must be kindly faction with Norrath's Keepers
\- Completion of all Tier 3 tasks and flags
\- **This raid requires six live players present in the zone**
You say, 'Hail, Lieutenant Ekiltu Verlor'
Lieutenant Ekiltu Verlor says 'Those faithful to the way of Norrath's Keepers may take on the greatest challenges and prove their worth to Firiona Vie.'
You have been assigned the task 'Goblin Dojo'.
It is peculiar that the goblins in Thundercrest Isles have issued a challenge to Norrathians. As much as we don't often bend to threats and arrogance, we are curious what the outcome of this challenge might be -- and it's better that we find out and be prepared for it, rather than the Dark Reign. Be on your guard. It could very well be a trap.

---

**Task Steps**
Touch the portal to Thundercrest Isles 0/1 (The Broodlands)
Enter the Dojo 0/1 (Thundercrest Isles)
**TASK LOCKS HERE**
Overcome the Stormreach Challenge 0/1 (Thundercrest Isles)

---

**Entry**
_Approach the Storm Reach Sensei, who opens the doors to the Dojo and enters._
Storm Reach Sensei says 'Welcome to the Storm Reach Dojo. I am pleased that you have taken up our humble challenge. As soon as you are ready, bring at least six of your fellows into the dojo and we shall begin. Be aware that this is a contest to the death. Do not enter the dojo unless you are prepared to pay that price.'
_Six players must be inside the Dojo before this event activates. Don't enter the Dojo until you're ready._

---

**The Event: Waves of Goblins & The First Wave**
_In this event, you will be fighting against waves of goblins. Six people must be present in the dojo in order to activate the event._
_The numbers in the waves:_
\- 1st wave: five goblins
\- 2nd wave: four goblins
\- 3rd wave: three goblins
\- 4th wave: two goblins
\- 5th wave: one named
_Upon zoning in, you are dropped at the portal to the dojo. Enter it, and approach Storm Reach Sensei in the middle of the opposite wall. Your would-be opponents are all spawned already, but aren't attackable yet. You'll see the following:_
The Storm Reach Sensei says, 'Good, you have decided to meet the challenge. I will not bore you with unworthy tales of our training methods or our meager accomplishments, certainly your own deeds far outshine our own. We are but meager servants to the Strom Mistress, Yar`Lir, and are of little consequence. We have been asked to test you and, certainly, ourselves in a battle to the death. The residents of this place are away at tasks beyond those such as we, and for a short while we have this room to ourselves. We must finish our contest before they return, and so there is little time for pleasantries.'
The Storm Reach Sensei says, 'A winner will be declared if they are the only combatants alive in this room. Your first challenge will be to defeat the Disciples of The Five Elements. Certainly they will be easily defeated by such mighty beings, but it is within their hearts to try. Your battle will begin in one minute. Prepare yourselves.'
In a clear, ringing voice, The Disciple of Metal says, 'Please forgive my poor humor, but I am very eager to test your mettle.'
The Disciple of Earth rumbles, 'When we have defeated you, I shall see that your corpses rot in the soil as is proper.'
The Disciple of Fire shouts, 'I make no apologies. I shall burn your hearts out!'
The Disciple of Water says, 'Ah, yes. You must forgive Fire, for he cares little for pleasantries. It is part of his charm. You most certainly will be worthy opponents. I am honored to face you.'
The Disciple of Wood says, 'May our efforts here earn favor with those that watch over us all.'
_The first wave attacks: Five disciples:_
\- Disciple of Earth: hits 240-1500, average 630, ~83,000 hitpoints
\- Disciple of Fire: hits 100-700, average 350, ~57,000 hitpoints
\- Disciple of Metal: hits 300-1300, average 690, ~60,000 hitpoints
\- Disciple of Water: hits 100-700, average 300, ~65,000 hitpoints
\- Disciple of Wood: hits 170-900, average 460, ~78,000 hitpoints
_Upon the death of the fifth disciple, you see:_
The Storm Reach Sensei says, 'Congratulations! You have defeated the Disciples of the Five Elements. You have proven yourself worthy to face the Four Emotions. They have mastered their emotions and from them they gain strength. Each has become a conduit for the emotions of our kind, a paradigm, if you will. Certainly you shall defeat them, for you are the champions of your people and they are slaves. We shall begin again in two minutes. Rest and consult with each other, if you have need.'

---

**The Event: The Second Wave**
_Two minutes after completion of the first wave, the Masters attack:_
The Master of Rage shouts, 'I hate you! I will rend you into bite-sized chunks of flesh and feed them to the puma!' Rage's face darkens with blood and flecks of spittle fly from his mouth as he pants and stares at his enemies.
The Master of Deceit smiles broadly. 'You certainly will find Rage difficult to stop. I see it boiling up inside many of you. Allow it to take over, and Rage will ruin you.'
The Master of Doubt mutters, 'I. . . You. . . ' he sighs, 'I do not know what will happen next. I see the same misgiving in some of you. Could it be that you are like me? No, that seems unlikely.'
The Master of Contentment drawls, 'Ah! I am pleased to meet you all. This is a fine challenge for us, is it not? I find this sort of exercise invigorating, though rarely do I find invigoration to be a good thing.'
_The four masters attack:_
\- Master of Contentment: hits 190-990, average 490, ~100,000 hitpoints
\- Master of Deceit: hits 310-1500, average 770, ~110,000 hitpoints
\- Master of Doubt: hits 240-1020, average 500, ~101,000 hitpoints, casts "Doubt" and "Mental Discord"
\- Master of Rage: hits 80-1200, average 540, ~98,000 hitpoints, rampages
_Upon the death of the fourth master, you see:_
The Storm Reach Sensei says, 'Well done. It is rare that an opponent is able to defeat the goblin Emotions. Your next test will be greater, I fear. You must face our three Animals. These are grandmasters of our arts and have never suffered defeat against opponents outside of these grand islands of the sky. You look weary. You shall have three minutes to prepare for the next contest.'

---

**The Event: The Third Wave**
_Three minutes after the previous wave, the three Grandmasters attack:_
\- Grandmaster Beetle: hits 150-850, average 510, ~152,000 hitpoints, casts "Whirling Beetle Strike"
\- Grandmaster Puma: hits 150-800, average 400,~140,000 hitpoints
\- Grandmaster Snake: hits 120-730, average 280, ~126,000 hitpoints, casts "Venomed Mist"
_Upon the death of the third grandmaster, you see:_
The Storm Reach Sensei says, 'Amazing, simply amazing! Never before have our grandmasters been defeated by anyone other than your next opponents. Prepare yourself, for next you must face Silk and Steel. You have four minutes to prepare, use those minutes wisely.'

---

**The Event: The Fourth Wave**
_Four minutes after the third wave, "Steel" and "Silk" attack:_
In a voice more fluent and soft than any you've heard a goblin use, Silk says, 'Forgive my brother. He has a keen mind but a blunt mouth. We look forward to this test of our skills. Thank you for honoring us by accepting the challenge.'
\- Steel: hits 130-830, average 350, ~221,000 hitpoints, highly resistant to weapons
\- Silk: hits 30-420, average 210, ~202,000 hitpoints, flurries, casts "Corporeal Torment"

---

**The Event: The Final Wave**
_Upon the death of Steel and Silk, you see:_
The Storm Reach Sensei says, 'Wonderful! You have defeated all of the students of the Storm Reach Dojo! Celebrate your victory. Remember this day.'
The Storm Reach Sensei says, 'You have beaten all of my students, and have only had an advantage in numbers. Experience has taught me that such advantages usually lead to disaster caused by overconfidence or reliance on the lesser skills of others. Today you have proven that my experience is not always correct, a humbling moment.'
The Storm Reach Sensei says, 'You should return to those that sent you and tell them of your success. It will be a moment of shame for me to return to my mistress and explain the defeat of my students. I offer you my humble congratulations.'
The Storm Reach Sensei says, 'However, our challenge is not over. You were to defeat all of the members of the Storm Reach Dojo to prove your worth. You have indeed defeated all of the students of my humble dojo, but you have not defeated the master of this insignificant school. To complete the challenge, you must defeat the school's master.'
_Now, Storm Reach Sensei attacks._
_Storm Reach Sensei hits for a max ~3,100; has ~336,000 hitpoints; casts "Whirling Smash" and "Fist of Mastery"; and single-target rampages._

---

**Completion & Loot**
Storm Reach Sensei has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
Storm Reach Sensei's corpse falls slowly to the floor, a small smile on his face.
You've done well. Thank you for representing us.
_The Storm Reach Sensei drops 6x "Meditation Stone" (progression item) + 1 item from this loot table:_
Kilt of the North Winds
Leaguewalker Leggings
Thunderheart
Vestment of the Far Horizon
_Rewards:_
15 Radiant Crystals
1x "Norrath's Keepers Token" (faction **turn-in** [quest=3134] item)
_Note: If doing this raid as part of progression, you'll want to check in with some NPCs to make sure your flags are up to date. See **this quest entry** [quest=3061] for more details._
**Submitted by:** Saraban of Darkwind
- Kilt of the North Winds [item=36008]
- Leaguewalker Leggings [item=36791]
- Meditation Stone [item=34992]
- Radiant Crystal [item=34988]
- Thunderheart [item=35008]
- Vestment of the Far Horizon [item=39052]

**For an overview of the Dragons of Norrath expansion, see this wiki article.**
**For an overview of progression, see quest entries for Norrath's Keepers [quest=3061] and the Dark Reign [quest=3062].**

---

_This raid task begins with Captain Areha Burina ( **click for map image**) in the Norrath's Keepers camp of the Lavastorm Mountains._
_Pre-requisites for requesting this task:_
\- Minimum 6 players; maximum 54 players
\- Must be kindly faction with Norrath's Keepers
\- Completion of all Tier 3 tasks and flags
\- Completion of "Guardian of the Sands" (raid)
\- Completion of "Goblin Dojo" (raid)
You say, 'Hail, Captain Areha Burina'
Captain Areha Burina bows. 'I see a good heart in you and a measure of bravery. Serve Firiona Vie and Norrath's Keepers well!'
You have been assigned the task 'An End to the Storms'.
Make your way to the isles in the sky. There you will find the great tempest dragon. We want to show a hand of good faith, but do proceed with care. We are almost certain this will be a hopeless mission and you will more than likely be required to defend yourself. Should she wish to see the end of the Norrathians for invading her home, then you must do what is necessary to prevent it.

---

**Task Steps**
Touch the portal to Thundercrest Isles 0/1 (The Broodlands)
Confront Yar`Lir 0/1 (Thundercrest Isles)
**TASK LOCKS HERE**
Kill Yar`Lir 0/1 (Thundercrest Isles)

---

**The Event**
_Get everyone inside Yar`Lir's chamber before she reaches 95% health, as the doors to her chamber lock at that point._
Yar`Lir the Living Storm says 'I so seldom receive visitors here. You will be my entertainment!'
_Yar`Lir the Living Storm hits for a max ~4,800; AE rampages (mitigated to ~900-1000 per hit); and casts "Wind Shear", "Icicle Blizzard", and "Yar`Lir's Cone of Storms":_
Icicle Blizzard: Targeted AE 75', Cold (-475)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 3000
Wind Shear: Single Target, Chromatic (-425)
1: SpinStun
Wing Buffet: PB AE 1000', Chromatic (-425)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 2500
Yar'Lir's Cone of Storms: Directional AE 500', Chromatic (-425)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 5000
_She is permanently rooted in place and sees invisibility (but not Shroud of Stealth). Over the course of the fight, she'll change forms (unique abilities depending on her form):_
\- Yar`Lir the Matriarch (spawns several "a blizzard seeker" adds)
\- Yar`Lir the Hurricane`s Eye (spawns adds called "an unstable storm" which AE the DOT "Force Quake")
\- Yar`Lir the Mistress of Winds (adds "Tempest Wind" to her spell lineup)
Tempest Wind: Targeted AE 30', Magic (0)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 600

---

**Completion & Loot**
_Kill Yar`Lir to complete the event:_
Yar`Lir the Living Storm has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
Yar`Lir struggles for a moment, causing a great torrent of weather to rock her treasure chamber. As the mighty dragon comes crashing down, the wind dies.
Your faction standing with Yar`lir has been adjusted by -1.
Your faction standing with Vishimtar has been adjusted by -1.
Unfortunately, Yar`Lir could not be reasoned with. You did what must be done to protect the denizens of Norrath from her irresponsible control over wind and weather.
_Yar`Lir drops 9x "Yar`Lir's Fang" (progression) + 1x "Eye of Yar`Lir" (turn-in item for **a mask** [quest=3090]). The chest that spawns after her death contains 3 items from this loot table:_
Crescent Palemoon
Crystallized Glass Staff
Drake Scale Tambourine
Drake Scaled Bracer
Etched Clay Ornament
Head of the Putrid Drake
Lightning Singed Mantle
Mask of Raining Tears
Oculus of Yar`Lir
Shroud of the Surging Storm
Stormeye Band
Thundercrash Girdle
Urazasishi
Windfury Katana
Windwatcher Sash
Yarusha, the Storm's Fury
_Task Rewards:_
8x "Radiant Crystal"
1x "Norrath's Keepers Token" (faction **turn-in** [quest=3134] item)
_Note: If doing this raid as part of progression, you'll want to check in with some NPCs to make sure your flags are up to date. See **this quest entry** [quest=3061] for more details._
**Submitted by:** Saraban, Darkwind
- Norrath's Keepers Token [item=34991]
- Radiant Crystal [item=34988]

This solo task begins with Private Nylaen Kel`Ther (click for map image) who may be found at the Norrath's Keepers camp in the caves of the Lavastorm Mountains (/loc 3750, 470).
Pre-requisites for requesting this task:
- Level 65+

You say, 'Hail, Private Nylaen Kel`Ther'
Private Nylaen Kel`Ther says 'We all have the opportunity to rise to greatness with Norrath's Keepers and our honorable allies across the lands.'
You have been assigned the task 'Greed of the Goblins'.
As sad as it makes us all, it would seem the Dark Reign has made a bargain with the goblins of the Stillmoon Temple. Whatever the deal that was made, the goblins have been extremely hostile toward Norrath's Keepers. Their numbers are so great that many of our beloved kin have been slaughtered. We need to lessen the threat of the goblins to us all and I believe you are perfect for this task, so please kill 11 Stillmoon laborers.
Kill 11 Stillmoon laborers 0/11 (Stillmoon Temple)
Only "a Stillmoon laborer" counts toward this kill step. These are found anywhere where goblins can spawn. Upon killing the 11th, you see:
You have completed the task for Norrath's Keepers!
Your faction standing with Norrath's Keepers got better. (+10)
Your faction standing with Firiona Vie got better. (+10)
Your faction standing with Lanys T`Vyl got worse. (-10)

**For an overview of the Dragons of Norrath expansion, see this wiki article.**
**For an overview of progression, see quest entries for Norrath's Keepers [quest=3061] and the Dark Reign [quest=3062].**

---

_This solo task begins with Private Nylaen Kel`Ther ( **click for map image**) in the Norrath's Keepers camp in the caves of Lavastorm Mountains (/loc 3750, 470)._
_Pre-requisites for requesting this task:_
\- Level 67+
\- Minimum "amiable" faction with Norrath's Keepers
\- Completion of all Tier 1 tasks and flags
You say, 'Hail, Private Nylaen Kel`Ther'
Private Nylaen Kel`Ther says 'We all have the opportunity to rise to greatness with Norrath's Keepers and our honorable allies across the lands.'
You have been assigned the task 'Defend the Kirin Contact'.
We'd thought we made headway against the Dark Reign, but it would appear that the source that helped us stay a step ahead is endangered. One of our scouts was able to find a sympathetic ear with a kirin in the mountain pass known as The Ascent. The agreement was that this kirin would warn us of any of the Dark Reign's advances and plans. We were told that some kirin students learned of the arrangement and have been attempting to kill our ally. As beautiful as the creatures are, some are dark and deadly in their intentions. We believe if we protect our source, the other kirin may decide to abandon their anger for our ally.

---

Kill 9 kirins 0/9 (The Ascent)
_Any kirin in the zone counts towards this step. There may be a few spawns in the bottom half of the zone, but they are primarily found in the top half, meaning you need to invis or fight past everything else on the way up._
_Upon killing the last kirin, you see:_
You have completed the task for Norrath's Keepers!
Your faction standing with Norrath's Keepers got better. (+10)
Your faction standing with Firiona Vie got better. (+10)
Your faction standing with Lanys T`Vyl got worse. (-10)
Your faction standing with Dark Reign got worse. (-10)
_Rewards:_
Experience
216 platinum, 5 gold, 6 silver, 2 copper
Faction with Norrath's Keepers

You say, 'Hail, Private Nylaen Kel`Ther'
Private Nylaen Kel`Ther says 'We all have the opportunity to rise to greatness with Norrath's Keepers and our honorable allies across the lands.'
You have been assigned the task 'Recover the Lost Map'.
As much as most of us respect and revere all life, we have come up against a problem that does tax our beliefs and we need assistance. The pumas of the temple are not to blame for their anger toward us. Norrathians are to blame for disturbing the sacred lands of the dragons and stirring hatred of us. We must accept this error and deal with the consequences. The pumas have killed one of our cartographers in Stillmoon Temple who was sent to create maps to guide you in tasks there. Sadly, those maps have not been found and may be used against the sacred creatures of the temple, as well as the Keepers.

---

Loot 8 Shredded Map Piece 0/8 (The Ascent)
_Head to the Ascent, and kill pumas and leopards in the zone until you've looted 8x "Shredded Map Piece" (uncommon drop -- about 1-in-3)._
_Upon looting the last map piece, you see:_
--You have looted a Shredded Map Piece.--
You have completed the task for Norrath's Keepers!

# Norrath's Keepers Tier 2a: Turn the Tides of the Sand

**Level:** 67 | **Maximum Level:** 125 | **Quest Type:** Task | **Repeatable:** No | **Monster Mission:** No


## Quest Text / Walkthrough

**Time:** 1 Hour
**Level:** 67
**Maximum Level:** 125
**Monster Mission:** No
**Repeatable:** No
**Can Be Shrouded?:** No
**Quest Type:** Task
**Group Size:** Solo
**Min. # of Players:** 1
**Max. # of Players:** 1

For an overview of the Dragons of Norrath expansion, see this wiki article.
For an overview of progression, see quest entries for Norrath's Keepers [quest=3061] and the Dark Reign [quest=3062].
This solo task begins with Private Nylaen Kel`Ther (click for map image) in the Norrath's Keepers camp in the caves of Lavastorm Mountains (/loc 3750, 470).
Pre-requisites for requesting this task:
- Level 67+
- Minimum "amiable" faction with Norrath's Keepers
- Completion of all Tier 1 tasks and flags
You say, 'Hail, Private Nylaen Kel`Ther'
Private Nylaen Kel`Ther says 'We all have the opportunity to rise to greatness with Norrath's Keepers and our honorable allies across the lands.'
You have been assigned the task 'Turn the Tides of the Sand'.
The goblins of the Stillmoon Temple are still battling with us, but we believe we may have learned a way to turn the tides of their anger. It is not a pleasant task and may seem counterproductive at first, but do heed my words. Their blood seems to be made partly of earth and with that blood, we can create an enchanted potion and perhaps make them see the error of their ways. Go loot 7 Sandy Goblin Blood so we can once and for all end the danger to the Keepers.
Loot 7 Sandy Goblin Blood 0/7 (Stillmoon Temple)

a Stillmoon dirt tender

a Stillmoon witchdoctor

**For an overview of the Dragons of Norrath expansion, see this wiki article.**
**For an overview of progression, see quest entries for Norrath's Keepers [quest=3061] and the Dark Reign [quest=3062].**

---

_This group mission begins with Keeper Dilar Nelune ( **click for map image**) in the Norrath's Keepers camp of the Lavastorm Mountains._
_Pre-requisites for requesting this task:_
\- Level 69+
\- Must be high amiable faction with Norrath's Keepers
\- Completion of all Tier 2 tasks and flags
\- Completion of "Protect the Waters"
\- Completion of "The Sacred Scrolls"
\- Completion of "Snowfoot Revenge"
You say, 'Hail, Keeper Dilar Nelune'
Keeper Dilar Nelune says 'Should we all perform to Chieftain Aderi's expectations, the races of good and light will bring an end to the threat of evil upon Norrath.'
You have been assigned the task 'History of the Isle'.
We've uncovered documents that lead us to believe that the history of Thundercrest Isles is hidden in the walls deep within the confines of one of the islands. Within these walls are several coves where the very secrets of Yar`Lir, the storm dragon, are stored. If you're ever going to see Yar`Lir, you're going to need to read what these walls say.

---

!

---

Touch the portal to Thundercrest Isle 0/1 (The Broodlands)
_Your compass will direct you to the instanced zone._

---

Look through the lower section of the Halls of History 0/1 (Thundercrest Isles)
_Task Window Says:_ You must go forth into the storm dragon Yar`Lir's Halls of History, within her lair atop the Thundercrest Isles, to uncover the secrets -- indeed, the very essence -- of the dragon's history.
_The Halls of History are located in the building in the far northwestern part of the zone. Note that mobs in this instanced zone respawn. Once in the northwestern building, head to the lower level of the halls._

---

Kill the sentry guarding one of the artifacts of Yar`Lir 0/1 (Thundercrest Isles)
Recover the Gift of Yar`Lir 0/1 (Thundercrest Isles)
_Task Window Says:_ There will be great strife in your attempts to find the secrets, which is why you must destroy anything in your way starting with the sentry guarding the first secret.
Kill the sentry guarding one of the artifacts of Yar`Lir 0/1 (Thundercrest Isles)
Recover the Strength of Yar`Lir 0/1 (Thundercrest Isles
_Task Window Says:_ There remain many secrets here that only you can reveal. Now, you must destroy the guardian protecting the second secret of Yar`Lir.
Kill the sentry guarding one of the artifacts of Yar`Lir 0/1 (Thundercrest Isles)
Recover the Fury of Yar`Lir 0/1 (Thundercrest Isles)
_Task Window Says:_ At last, you are nearly through these great halls of knowledge. Destroy the guardian of the final secret and you will be victorious in your search.
_It doesn't matter in which order you seek out and kill the above-mentioned sentries. All are in the lower level of the building (from the ramp leading down to the lower level, you'll find two in dead-ends to the right, and one in the dead-end to the left), Level 73, and each guard an artifact:_
Defender of the Halls
Guardian of the Halls
Protector of the Halls
_Each hits for a max ~1,800; single-target rampages; has ~53,000 hitpoints; and is stunnable, but immune to mez._
_After the death of each sentry, loot the artifact of Yar`Lir (next to its spawn point). One of the three artifacts you will loot in this instance will also contain the "Holding of Yar`Lir", the container required for the final combine, so loot this too._

---

Mold the three artifacts of Yar`Lir into the whole secret she possesses 0/1 (Thundercrest Isles)
_Task Window Says:_ You must connect the three secrets of Lir together so that their full potential can be realized. Look about you for that which will connect them, for it is almost assuredly already in your possession.
_Combine the "Gift of Yar`Lir" + "Strength of Yar`Lir" + "Fury of Yar'Lir" inside the "Holding of Yar`Lir". Do this cambine inside the instance. The result is the "Secret of Yar`Lir", and completes the task._
You have bested beasts of incredible power against all odds to recover long-protected artifacts of the great storm dragon, Yar`Lir. You are on a path to uncovering the mysteries of the Nest and why it was kept hidden in front of your very eyes for so long.
_Rewards:_
47 Radiant Crystals
1x "Norrath's Keepers Token" (faction **turn-in** [quest=3134] item)
_Note: If doing this mission as part of progression, you'll want to check in with some NPCs to make sure your flags are up to date. See **this quest entry** [quest=3061] for more details._
- Norrath's Keepers Token [item=34991]
- Radiant Crystal [item=34988]

**For an overview of the Dragons of Norrath expansion, see this wiki article.**
**For an overview of progression, see quest entries for Norrath's Keepers [quest=3061] and the Dark Reign [quest=3062].**

---

_This group mission begins with Keeper Dilar Nelune ( **click for map image**) in the Norrath's Keepers camp of the Lavastorm Mountains._
_Pre-requisites for requesting this task:_
\- Must be warmly faction with Norrath's Keepers
\- Completion of all Tier 4 tasks and flags (including the raids)
You say, 'Hail, Keeper Dilar Nelune'
Keeper Dilar Nelune says 'Should we all perform to Chieftain Aderi's expectations, the races of good and light will bring an end to the threat of evil upon Norrath.'
You have been assigned the task 'Origins of the Curse'.
According to reports from our preliminary scouting expeditions, we believe that someone, or something, unleashed a curse within the Nest shortly before our soldiers first arrived. Venture into the Next and see if you can recover any clues as to the nature of this curse.

---

!

---

Touch the portal to The Nest in the Broodlands 0/1 (The Broodlands)
_The entrance is in the southeastern part of the Broodlands._

---

Explore the Fire Lair in the Nest 0/1 (The Accursed Nest)
_Locate an NPC called "Zebeker, a necromancer`s pet". From the zone-in point, head west, and then make a left (turn south to the zone's fire wing) at the first 'intersection' in the tunnel. You'll find it there. This is a location update; you don't actually have to speak with him, but doing so will provide some information:_
You say, 'Hail, Zebeker, a necromancer`s pet'
Zebeker, a necromancer`s pet says '`Yeah? What do you want? Were you sent by my [master] to give me a good savaging? C'mon then. Make it fast.''
You say, 'Who is your master?'
Zebeker, a necromancer`s pet says '`What master? Don't tell me you wandered down here on your own?. . . I'm talking about the necromancer that unleashed the curse on this place. I used to be his summoned pet, you know? I was a good pet too--always fetching things and spying on people for him--then one day he takes me on a [mission] into this crazy dragons' nest and I accidentally dropped one little potion. Like that--,' Zebeker snaps his fingers, '--my master flies into a rage and commands me to stay here for all time. I've been stuck here ever since, watching my bones grow brittle and the walls gather dust. I don't even have anything to read!''
You say, 'What mission?'
Zebeker, a necromancer`s pet says '`No, sorry. I can't tell you what that still-hearted maggot pie of a necromancer was up to. I'm his summoned pet after all and I can't betray him even though he deserves it a thousand times over. I'm bound by his magic, you see, and until I get my [bone chips] back, I'll forever be his servant, forced to obey his blasted, ill-conceived commands. By the gods, how I hate that necromancer!''
You say, 'What bone chips?'
Zebeker, a necromancer`s pet says '`The bone chips that he used to summon me. Only if I have those can I be free of his contestable control. After I dropped the potion, he crushed my bone chips into four shards and threw them into. . . into . . . Aaagh! His magical control over me is preventing me from saying, but if you can find my crushed bone fragments, I can be free again. Then I can I can tell you everything I know about this accursed nest we're both stuck in.''

---

Find the Fragments of Zebeker's Bone Chips 0/4 (The Accursed Nest)
_From here, you have to find and kill 4x "a remnant of the curse" (Level 73 dervish mobs). These mobs are non-KOS and are not social with the other mobs in the zone. Each of them drops 1x "Fragments of Zebeker's Bone Chips". Once you have the four fragments, return to Zebeker, and turn them in. They can also be handed to Zebeker one at a time by anyone in the task._
_Reports indicate the locations of the remnants are somewhat randomized. You'll want to look in any of the five 'rooms' in the south wing (this includes the two bridge rooms)._
_The remnants hit for a max ~1,100; have ~50,000 hitpoints; and are stunnable and mezzable. They are relatively less difficult than the general zone population._
a remnant of the curse whispers faintly, 'Deathhh findsss you.'
a remnant of the curse has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
a remnant of the curse's corpse withers and shrinks to the ground

---

Return Fragments of Zebeker's Bone Chips to Zebeker 0/4 (The Accursed Nest)
_In the task window, you now see:_
Metafuze says, 'I'm free! Now, as promised, I'll tell you everything I know. My master snuck in here through a secret cave entrance and unleashed this curse upon the Nest. He wanted vengeance against the dragons for their "crimes" as he often put it. I could tell that much by his maddening rants. Unfortunately, I cannot say more. My master left me shortly after completing the curse and said that if I ever repeated his name, I would be blasted from Norrath before I could pronounce the first syllable. I believe him too!

---

Speak to Chieftain Relae Aderi 0/1 (Lavastorm Mountains)
_Chieftain Relae Aderi ( **click for map image**) is found in the Norrath's Keepers camp. Hail to complete._
You say, 'Hail, Chieftain Relae Aderi'
Chieftain Relae Aderi says, 'You say a necromancer had a hand in this? Hmmm, I expected dark magic was afoot. I must report this to Firiona and allow her to meditate upon the situation. In the meantime, you should get some rest. Norrath's Keepers may require your assistance soon.'
_Rewards:_
68 Radiant Crystals
1x "Norrath's Keepers Token" (faction **turn-in** [quest=3134] item)
_Note: If doing this mission as part of progression, you'll want to check in with some NPCs to make sure your flags are up to date. See **this quest entry** [quest=3061] for more details._
**Submitted by:** Fulast Swifttail, Titans of Saryrn, Saryrn
- Norrath's Keepers Token [item=34991]
- Radiant Crystal [item=34988]

# Norrath's Keepers Tier 5a: The Curse of Ju`rek

**Level:** 70 | **Maximum Level:** 125 | **Quest Type:** Task | **Repeatable:** Yes | **Monster Mission:** No


## Quest Text / Walkthrough

**Level:** 70
**Maximum Level:** 125
**Monster Mission:** No
**Repeatable:** Yes
**Can Be Shrouded?:** No
**Quest Type:** Task
**Time Limit:** 06:00:00
**Group Size:** Raid
**Min. # of Players:** 6
**Max. # of Players:** 24

For an overview of the Dragons of Norrath expansion, see this wiki article.
For an overview of progression, see quest entries for Norrath's Keepers [quest=3061] and the Dark Reign [quest=3062].
This raid task begins with Lieutenant Ekiltu Verlor (click for map image) in the Norrath's Keepers camp of the Lavastorm Mountains.
Pre-requisites for requesting this task:
- Minimum 6 players; maximum 24 players
- Must be warmly faction with Norrath's Keepers
- Completion of all Tier 4 tasks and flags (including the raids)
You say, 'Hail, Lieutenant Ekiltu Verlor'
Lieutenant Ekiltu Verlor says 'Those faithful to the way of Norrath's Keepers may take on the greatest challenges and prove their worth to Firiona Vie.'
You have been assigned the task 'The Curse of Ju`rek'.
You must travel into the Nest and learn as much as you can about the corruption of the place. There are a number of drakes in the caverns of the Nest that have been twisted somehow. We want you to look for a drake that has been rumored to be altered so significantly that it has unique magic. Go learn more about it and kill it if you can.
Task Steps
Touch the portal to the Nest 0/1 (The Broodlands)
Confront Ju`rek the Corrupted 0/1 (The Accursed Nest)
TASK LOCKS HERE
Kill Ju`rek the Corrupted 0/1 (The Accursed Nest)
The Event
The entrance to the instance is in the southeastern part of the Broodlands. Once inside, head east across the bridge and through the doors. Ju`rek's lair is the SE room of east wing.
Ju`rek the Corrupted hits for a max ~2,300; flurries; has ~545,000 hitpoints; and casts two spells:
Decaying Stench: PB AE 100', Disease (-600)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 1800 per tick
2: Increase Disease Counter by 45
Ju'Ok's Cry: Targeted AE 100', Poison (-550)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 1500
2: Stun (10.00 sec)
Also, Ju`rek spawns multiple adds called "a harsh wind" in waves as its health goes down. These proc "Freezing Blast"; have relatively few hitpoints; and are mezzable, but not stunnable:
Freezing Blast: PB AE 100', Magic (-300)
1: Decrease HP when cast by 1000
2: Decrease Movement by 70%
Completion & Loot
Kill Ju`rek the Corrupted to complete the task:
Ju`rek the Corrupted has been slain by ______!
Ju`rek the Corrupted's corpse falls to the ground, its massive wings beating no more.
Your faction standing with Cursed Drakes got worse.
Your faction standing with Vishimtar got worse.
Ju`rek has been slain! This is wonderful news. Perhaps with time the evil that lies within may be quelled.
Ju`rek drops 6x "Diseased Wing Fragment" (progression item) + 1 item from this loot table:
Black Banded Ring of Evisceration
Dark-Touched Mantle
Earring of Contemplation
Hammer of the Last Clutch
18 Radiant Crystals
1x "Norrath's Keepers Token" (faction turn-in [quest=3134] item)
Note: If doing this raid as part of progression, you'll want to check in with some NPCs to make sure your flags are up to date. See this quest entry [quest=3061] for more details.Rewards:
- Black Banded Ring of Evisceration [item=38559]
- Dark-Touched Mantle [item=38536]
- Diseased Wing Fragments [item=34899]

You have been assigned the task 'Blood of the Basilisk'.
Sirrikis grumbles seeing that you are still alive. 'Don't look at me as though I misjudged you, blundering fool! As you seem to be so persistent, I do have a job for you to do, if you manage to not foul that up. It would seem we need some simmering blood from the basilisks in Tirranun's Delve. Bring us five of those as well. Don't even think to ask us what it's for. You're too ignorant to understand. Now remove yourself from my sight and loot 5 Simmering Basilisk Blood.

# Dark Reign Tier 1b: Have Note Will Travel

**Level:** 65 | **Maximum Level:** 125 | **Quest Type:** Task | **Repeatable:** Yes | **Monster Mission:** No


## Quest Text / Walkthrough

**Level:** 65
**Maximum Level:** 125
**Monster Mission:** No
**Repeatable:** Yes
**Can Be Shrouded?:** No
**Quest Type:** Task
**Time Limit:** 06:00:00
**Group Size:** Group
**Min. # of Players:** 3
**Max. # of Players:** 6

For an overview of the Dragons of Norrath expansion, see this wiki article.
For an overview of progression, see quest entries for Norrath's Keepers [quest=3061] and the Dark Reign [quest=3062].
This group mission begins with Officer Vacax Rol`Tas (click for map image) in the Dark Reign camp of the Lavastorm Mountains.
Pre-requisites for requesting this task:
- Average group level minimum 68
- Minimum "indifferent" faction with Dark Reign
- Completion of "Blood of the Basilisk"
- Completion of "Clearing the Path"
- Completion of "The Burning Poison"
You say, 'Hail, Officer Vacax Rol`Tas'
Officer Vacax Rol`Tas laughs, 'These are indeed good days for the will of hate and fear. We will overcome the races of light and prevail. Pledge allegiance to Lanys T`Vyl!'
You have been assigned the task 'Have Note Will Travel'.
Hm. I'm surprised to see you here. I won't say I'm pleased, but perhaps you may prove useful as I see you have survived other simple tasks thus far. Some believe the goblins in Tirranum's Delve plan to take up arms against us. You must go thwart their efforts. Should you fail, don't dare show your face here again.
Touch the cave entrance to Tirranum's Delve 0/1 (The Broodlands)
Once in the Broodlands, your compass will guide your direction.
LOADING, PLEASE WAIT...
You have entered Tirranun's Delve.
Look for information on the source of the uprising 0/1 (Tirranum's Delve)
Kill goblins (many of them), watching for emotes. After about 20 kills, you'll occasionally see this emote upon death:
The goblin falls before being able to sound the alarm.
Keep killing goblins until you see this:
As the Delvian falls to the ground you can hear a siren going off. Your presence has been made known. Quickly find the Goblin Task Master for the information you seek.
Now, find and kill the Goblin Task Master, located at the northern entrance to the temple. It hits for a max ~1,100; has ~170,000 hitpoints; summons; is immune to stun/mez; and procs "Goblin Headbutt" (3-second stun).
Goblin Task Master has been slain by _____!
Your faction standing with Minions of Tirranun got worse.
Your faction standing with Tirranun got worse.
Loot one Letter from Tirranun 0/1 (Tirranun's Delve)
Loot this from the task master's corpse.
Defeat all the goblin guards on your way to the entrance 0/9 (Tirranum's Delve)
Quickly make your way back to the entrance before you are caught 0/1 (Tirranum's Delve)
Kill 9x "a Delve guard" on your way out (you can't really miss them). They spawn in groups of three, but are stunnable and mezzable.
Upon reaching the entrance, the task completes and you see:
The goblins were no match for you, but Dark Reign likely has more dangerous tasks in mind for you.
39 Ebon Crystals
1x "Dark Reign Token" (faction turn-in [quest=3579] item)Submitted by: Yther OreRewards:

_This solo task begins with Officer Sirrikis Ryktor ( **click for map image**) at the Dark Reign camp in the caves, /loc 3050, -350._
_Pre-requisites for requesting this task:_
\- Level 67+
\- Minimum "amiable" faction with Dark Reign

You say, 'Hail, Officer Sirrikis Ryktor'
Officer Sirrikis Ryktor says 'Perhaps you will find a path to the higher ranks of the Dark Reign -- a place I'm sure I shall find myself someday... someday.'
You have been assigned the task 'Army of Stone'.
I have been wondering about the statues that guard the Stillmoon Temple and if there is something to the essence that brings them to life that may be of use to us. I've received the commander's permission to investigate and I want you to assist. What we must learn is that if there is a creature that sustains them with geomancy or if they manage to stand on their own. If that is the case then we must dissect that magic and perhaps we may be able to use it to create grand guardians and warriors in our own army -- disposable minions, like yourself. Find what animates them and bring me evidence. Go loot 5 Animated Guardian Essence.

---

You say, 'Hail, Officer Vacax Rol`Tas'
Officer Vacax Rol`Tas laughs, 'These are indeed good days for the will of hate and fear. We will overcome the races of light and prevail. Pledge allegiance to Lanys T`Vyl!'
You have been assigned the task 'Drake Eggs'.
We believe that eggs from the drakes in The Ascent could be some use in making powerful new potions. Go to The Ascent and collect one of these eggs for study. Once you have it, we will send you to an agent to collect it from you.

---

Touch the cave entrance to The Ascent 0/1 (The Broodlands)
_The instance entrance is in the Broodlands at approximate location 1590, 680, 52 (a cave in the northwestern part of the zone)._

---

Loot 1 Stillmoon Drake Egg from a drake in The Ascent 0/1 (The Ascent)
_The drake that drops the egg is called simply "a drake". You'll find several of these along the path upwards in the zone (usually in groups of 2-3)._
a drake has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
_Loot a Stillmoon Drake Egg from its corpse (drop is random)._

---

Kill 1 goblin guard 0/1 (The Ascent)
_Task Window Says:_ It appears that the forces of Stillmoon don't like you stealing the drake eggs. They killed our agent and now block your path out. Deal with them as you see fit and bring the eggs back to Celrak Blightblood.
_The goblin guard spawns at the zone-in along with two guardian statues. They each hit for a max ~700 and are mezzable and stunnable._
a Goblin Guard has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
a Goblin Guard's corpse falls forward, clutching its wounds.

You have been assigned the task 'Muddy the Waters'
There are servants in Stillmoon Temple that constantly toil to keep the water clean for the creatures to drink. The water there has some properties of magic that keep the inhabitants of Stillmoon very robust. We want you to go there and kill some of the servants. Without them, the water may become impure and weaken the creatures in the area, so kill 9 Stillmoon water servants as swiftly as you're able.

**For an overview of the Dragons of Norrath expansion, see this wiki article.**
**For an overview of progression, see quest entries for Norrath's Keepers [quest=3061] and the Dark Reign [quest=3062].**

---

_This group mission begins with Officer Vacax Rol`Tas ( **click for map image**) in the Dark Reign camp of the Lavastorm Mountains._
_Pre-requisites for requesting this task:_
\- Have high amiable faction with Dark Reign
\- Completion of all Tier 2 tasks and flags
\- Completion of "Muddy the Waters"
\- Completion of "Snowfoot Attack"
\- Completion of "Knowledge is Power"
You say, 'Hail, Officer Vacax Rol`Tas'
Officer Vacax Rol`Tas laughs, 'These are indeed good days for the will of hate and fear. We will overcome the races of light and prevail. Pledge allegiance to Lanys T`Vyl!'
You have been assigned the task 'The Gilded Scroll'.
The Storm Dragon, Yar'Lir, has caused one of her minions to create a scroll that can tear up the land and toss it into the air. I want that scroll. Find it and bring it back. I also want you to kill the creature that made the scroll. No, not Yar'Lir... the minion. I want to be the only one with such power.

---

Touch the portal to Thundercrest Isles 0/1 (The Broodlands)
_The zone-in is at 2400, 1865 (northwestern part of the zone). You zone into the building in the far northwestern part of the Thundercrest Isles._

---

Loot 1 Scroll 0/1 (Thundercrest Isles)
_Kill mobs in the zone and loot the items called "Scroll". When you loot, you see emotes:_
As you take the scroll from the corpse, you pause to take a closer look at it.
_These scrolls, when looted, transform into different items (useless items). Some of them trigger a detrimental effect on the looter. The scroll that updates the task triggers the same emote and transforms into "Gilded Scroll of Earth Rending". It also triggers the spawn of "Noble Kirin Scholar" at the location of the loot update. Upon spawn, it begins wandering away, so aggro it quickly (if it's left to wander off, it's not known where it winds up - possibly in the front courtyard outside the building)._

---

Kill 1 Noble Kirin Scholar 0/1 (Thundercrest Isles)
_Task Window Says:_ With the scroll in hand you can vaguely sense the one who created it. Kill everything in the hall if you have to, but destroy that Kirin!
_The Noble Kirin Scholar hits for a max ~1,200 and procs "Crushing Blow"._

---

Deliver 1 Gilded Scroll of Earth Rending to Daleynn Spiritshadow 0/1 (Lavastorm Mountains)
_Upon handing in the final scroll, the task completes and you see:_
Wonderful! I can't wait to see what this thing can do! Well done. We won't forget your help here.
_Rewards:_
50 Ebon Crystals
1x "Dark Reign Token" (faction **turn-in** [quest=3579] item)
- Dark Reign Token [item=34990]
- Ebon Crystal [item=34989]

---

_This group mission begins with Officer Vacax Rol`Tas ( **click for map image**) in the Dark Reign camp of the Lavastorm Mountains._
_Pre-requisites for requesting this task:_
\- Minimum "warmly" (770) faction with Dark Reign
\- Completion of all Tier 4 tasks and flags
You say, 'Hail, Officer Vacax Rol`Tas'
Officer Vacax Rol`Tas laughs, 'These are indeed good days for the will of hate and fear. We will overcome the races of light and prevail. Pledge allegiance to Lanys T`Vyl!'
You have been assigned the task 'Rival Party'.
Our rogues report that Norrath's Keepers has dispatched an adventuring party into the Nest to acquire a clutch of dragon eggs. We cannot let such powerful creatures, unborn though they may be, fall into the hands of our enemies. You must ambush this group. There are four of them; a wizard named Syllann, a rogue named Corgoth, a warrior named Vincent, and a cleric named Drogorus. Slay them all before they can uncover the dragon eggs and acquire them the eggs yourself. Bring one back to Pericolo L`Morte and destroy the rest!

---

!

---

Touch the portal to the Nest 0/1 (The Broodlands)
_Your compass will point you in the direction. This is the swamp entrance in the southwestern part of the Broodlands._

---

Kill 1 Syllann of Norrath's Keepers 0/1 (The Accursed Nest)
Kill 1 Corgoth of Norrath's Keepers 0/1 (The Accursed Nest)
Kill 1 Vincent of Norrath's Keepers 0/1 (The Accursed Nest)
Kill 1 Drogorus of Norrath's Keepers 0/1 (The Accursed Nest)
_These NPCs are located near 25, 1100 (from the zone-in, head west into the tunnel, and then make an immediate turn north)._
Corgoth of Norrath`s Keepers says 'Gather close, my comrades. Our enemies are near!'
Vincent of Norrath`s Keepers says 'We've got company!'
Syllan of Norrath`s Keepers says 'Careful. I've heard stories of these ones!'
Drogorus of Norrath`s Keepers says 'We've got company!'
_These four are splittable, stunnable, and mezzable. They each hit for a max ~1,000 and don't have any special abilities other than standard class spells._

---

Loot 6 Egg of a Saved Clutch 0/6 (The Accursed Nest)
_"Egg of a Saved Clutch" drop from random mobs in the metallic/springs wing of the zone (northwestern wing)._

---

Deliver 1 Egg of a Saved Clutch to Pericolo L`Morte 0/1 (Lavastorm Mountains)
_Hand in one of the eggs to Pericolo L`Morte ( **click for map image**) in Lavastorm._
Excellent work. We can't let these eggs into the hands of Norrath's Keepers. I'll inform my superiors of your success. They will be most pleased!
_Rewards:_
66 Ebon Crystals
1x "Dark Reign Token" (faction **turn-in** [quest=3579] item)
**Submitted by:** Fleven

_This raid task begins with Captain Aleeth Zyrv ( **click for map image**) in the Dark Reign camp of the Lavastorm Mountains._
_Pre-requisites for requesting this task:_
\- Minimum 6 players; maximum 24 players
\- Minimum "warmly" faction with Dark Reign
\- Completion of all Tier 4 tasks and flags
You say, 'Hail, Captain Aleeth Zyrv'
Captain Aleeth Zyrv says 'You must search the blackest recesses of your heart to serve us well, \_\_\_\_\_. Our endeavors are not for the frail.'
You have been assigned the task 'The Curse of Ju`rek'.
There is a particularly strong drake within the Nest. It seems when the weak hatchlings were corrupted, this one managed to draw more power within himself and grow strong. Defeat this drake so that we may know more of this power.

---

**Task Steps**
Touch the portal to the Nest 0/1 (The Broodlands)
Confront Ju`rek the Corrupted 0/1 (The Accursed Nest)
**TASK LOCKS HERE**
Kill Ju`rek the Corrupted 0/1 (The Accursed Nest)

---

**The Event**
_The entrance to the instance is in the southeastern part of the Broodlands._
_Ju`rek the Corrupted hits for a max ~2,300; flurries; has ~545,000 hitpoints; and casts two spells:_
Decaying Stench: PB AE 100', Disease (-600)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 1800 per tick
2: Increase Disease Counter by 45
Ju'Ok's Cry: Targeted AE 100', Poison (-550)
1: Decrease Hitpoints by 1500
2: Stun (10.00 sec)
_Also, Ju`rek spawns multiple adds called "a harsh wind" in waves as its health goes down. These proc "Freezing Blast"; have relatively few hitpoints; and are mezzable, but not stunnable:_
Freezing Blast: PB AE 100', Magic (-300)
1: Decrease HP when cast by 1000
2: Decrease Movement by 70%

---

**Completion & Loot**
_Kill Ju`rek the Corrupted to complete the task:_
Ju`rek the Corrupted has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_\_!
Ju`rek the Corrupted's corpse falls to the ground, its massive wings beating no more.
Your faction standing with Cursed Drakes got worse.
Your faction standing with Vishimtar got worse.
He has been slain. You found nothing? How worthless.
_Ju`rek drops 6x "Diseased Wing Fragment" (progression item) + 1 item from this loot table:_
Black Banded Ring of Evisceration
Dark-Touched Mantle
Earring of Contemplation
Hammer of the Last Clutch
_Task Rewards:_
18 Ebon Crystals
1x "Dark Reign Token" (faction **turn-in** [quest=3579] item)

- Dark Reign Token [item=34990]
- Dark-Touched Mantle [item=38536]

---

_This raid task begins with Captain Aleeth Zyrv ( **click for map image**) in the Dark Reign camp of the Lavastorm Mountains._
_Pre-requisites for requesting this task:_
\- Have high amiable faction with Dark Reign
\- Have minimum 6 players; maximum 42
\- Completion of all Tier 2 tasks and flags
\- Completion of "Muddy the Waters"
\- Completion of "Snowfoot Attack"
\- Completion of "Knowledge is Power"
\- Completion of "The Gilded Scroll" (group mission)
You say, 'Hail, Captain Aleeth Zyrv'
Captain Aleeth Zyrv says 'You must search the blackest recesses of your heart to serve us well, \_\_\_\_\_. Our endeavors are not for the frail.'
You have been assigned the task 'Volkara's Bite'.
So, you really wish to take on the queen of the spiders in Lavaspinner's Lair? Interesting. The reason to put an end to this spider, outside of the pleasure in ending the life of a pest, is to end the continual creation of spiders. Do not underestimate this spider, called Volkara. She has a nasty bite and is able to bring spiders to her aid at will. You will need to bring your own army to defeat her, for certain. Don't look to us for help here. You're on your own. Be the first to succeed and you will certainly capture the attention of the commander.

---

**Task Steps**
Touch the cave entrance to Lavaspinner's Lair 0/1 (The Broodlands)
Confront Volkara 0/1 (Lavaspinner's Lair)
**TASK LOCKS HERE**
Kill Volkara 0/1 (Lavaspinner's Lair)

---

**The Event**
_The entrance to the instanced zone is in the far southeastern part of the Broodlands._
_Once inside the zone, head to the far southeastern corner of the zone, where you'll find Volkara - a rather large lavaspinner._
_Volkara hits for a max ~2,000; has ~765,000 hitpoints; single-target rampages; flurries; and procs "Volkara's Bite":_
Volkara clicks is mandibles at you.
Volkara's Bite: PB AE 300', Poison (-300) _(5 minutes)_
1: Decrease HP when cast by 2000
2: Decrease Hitpoints by 200 per tick
3: Increase Poison Counter by 25
4: Decrease Endurance by 100 per tick
5: Decrease Mana by 100 per tick
_In addition, she spawns eggs ("Egg Sack") at health-based points: 80%, 60%, 40%, 20%, and 10%. These appear at the location of random players. If these aren't quickly killed, 3x "Magma Hatchling" spawn from them (these hit for a max ~800 and are stunnable, but not mezzable). You see this emote as the eggs spawn:_
Volkara stamps its legs causing eggs to fall from the ceiling.

---

**Completion & Loot**
_Kill Volkara to complete the task (adds do NOT despawn)._
Volkara has been slain by \_\_\_\_\_!
Volkara's corpse stumbles and falls to the ground.
Your faction standing with Volkara has been adjusted by -1.
Your faction standing with Volkara's Brood has been adjusted by -1.
With Volkara dead, her plans to build an army of arachnids has been thwarted. Norrathians can rest a little easier.
_She drops 9x "Lava Spider Spinners" (progression item) + 2 items from this loot table:_
Ancient Feline Leggings
Ancient Scale Link Vest
Dragon Scale-Bladed Mace
Ring of Unsung Legends
Staff of the Broodlands
Summoner's Rune Belt
_Task Rewards:_
8 Ebon Crystals
1x "Dark Reign Token" (faction **turn-in** [quest=3579] item)
- Ancient Feline Leggings [item=34896]
- Ancient Scale Link Vest [item=34977]
- Dark Reign Token [item=34990]
- Dragon Scale-Bladed Mace [item=34976]
- Ebon Crystal [item=34989]
- Lava Spider Spinners [item=34893]
- Ring of Unsung Legends [item=34894]
- Staff of the Broodlands [item=34897]
- Summoner's Rune Belt [item=34895]
